[BCS_literal_hypotheses_02] src/Development/WeilBound/RepresentationTheoryGL/ArchimedeanBaseChange/ArchimedeanBaseChangeProof/BaseChangeIsobaricNOf.lean:18-46

def CsdAsc {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] {N : ℕ} (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (a d : ℕ) (P : CuspSub E a), P.IsUnitary ∧ GlobalTheory.DiscSpec.IsNormalised P ∧ P.IsCsd ∧ PositivityTheory.Rg.Rs.AscEmb V P d

structure IsBaseChangeN {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] {n : ℕ} (π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ)) {c : ℕ}
    {N : Fin c → ℕ} (h : ∑ i, N i = n) (V : ∀ i, Submodule ℂ (Sm E (N i)))
    (σ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), SmIrr (GL (Fin (N i)) (w.adicCompletion E)))
    (m : Fin c → (E →+* ℂ) → Multiset ℂ)
    (P : ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), SmIrr (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E))) : Prop where
  pos : ∀ i, 0 < N i
  discrete : ∀ i, IsDiscrete (V i)
  csd : ∀ i, CsdAsc (V i)
  loc : ∀ i w, IsLocCompGL (V i) w (σ i w)
  inf : ∀ i τ, HasInfCharGL (V i) τ (m i τ)
  sub : ∀ w, GlobalTheory.SmGL.IsSubquotient (P w).ρ (locProd h w (σ · w))
  unram : ∀ w, IsUnramU π w → GlobalTheory.LGrp.IsSpherical (P w).ρ (GlobalTheory.LFun.KGL w n)
  inert : ∀ (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (s : w.adicCompletion E →+* w.adicCompletion E) (ϖ : w.adicCompletion E)
    (q : ℕ), (∀ x : E, s (algebraMap E _ x) = algebraMap E _ (IsCMField.complexConj E x)) → IsUnramInv ϖ s q →
    ∀ α, HasSatAt π w s ϖ q α → GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w (P w).ρ (GlobalTheory.LGrp.bcParam n α)
  split : ∀ w, IsSplitAt w → IsCompAlong π (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) (P w).ρ
  weight : ∀ τ lam, HasWeightAt π τ lam → ∑ i, m i τ = infOfWeight lam

def BaseChangeIsobaricN : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E],
    (∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), Algebra.IsUnramifiedAt (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield E)) w.asIdeal) →
    ∀ n : ℕ, 1 ≤ n → ∀ π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ), IsAutRepU π →
      ∃ (c : ℕ) (N : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, N i = n) (V : ∀ i, Submodule ℂ (Sm E (N i)))
        (σ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), SmIrr (GL (Fin (N i)) (w.adicCompletion E)))
        (m : Fin c → (E →+* ℂ) → Multiset ℂ)
        (P : ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), SmIrr (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E))), IsBaseChangeN π h V σ m P

structure IsBaseChangeU {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] {n : ℕ} (π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ)) {c : ℕ}
    {N : Fin c → ℕ} (h : ∑ i, N i = n) (V : ∀ i, Submodule ℂ (Sm E (N i)))
    (σ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), SmIrr (GL (Fin (N i)) (w.adicCompletion E)))
    (m : Fin c → (E →+* ℂ) → Multiset ℂ) : Prop where
  pos : ∀ i, 0 < N i
  discrete : ∀ i, IsDiscrete (V i)
  csd : ∀ i, CsdAsc (V i)
  loc : ∀ i w, IsLocCompGL (V i) w (σ i w)
  inf : ∀ i τ, HasInfCharGL (V i) τ (m i τ)
  unram : ∀ w, IsUnramU π w → GlobalTheory.LGrp.IsSpherical (locProd h w (σ · w)) (GlobalTheory.LFun.KGL w n)
  inert : ∀ (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (s : w.adicCompletion E →+* w.adicCompletion E) (ϖ : w.adicCompletion E)
    (q : ℕ), (∀ x : E, s (algebraMap E _ x) = algebraMap E _ (IsCMField.complexConj E x)) → IsUnramInv ϖ s q →
    ∀ α, HasSatAt π w s ϖ q α → GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w (locProd h w (σ · w)) (GlobalTheory.LGrp.bcParam n α)
  split : ∀ w, IsSplitAt w → IsCompAlong π (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) (locProd h w (σ · w))
  weight : ∀ τ lam, HasWeightAt π τ lam → ∑ i, m i τ = infOfWeight lam

def DefiniteN : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E],
    (∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), Algebra.IsUnramifiedAt (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield E)) w.asIdeal) →
    ∀ n : ℕ, 1 ≤ n → ∀ π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ), IsAutRepU π →
      ∃ (c : ℕ) (N : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, N i = n) (V : ∀ i, Submodule ℂ (Sm E (N i)))
        (σ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), SmIrr (GL (Fin (N i)) (w.adicCompletion E)))
        (m : Fin c → (E →+* ℂ) → Multiset ℂ), IsBaseChangeU π h V σ m

def AscComp : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (N : ℕ), 1 ≤ N →
    (∀ (a : ℕ) (π : CuspSub E a), a = N → IsNormalised π →
      ∃ V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N), IsDiscrete V ∧ AscEmb V π 1) ∧
    ∀ V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N), IsDiscrete V → ∀ (a d : ℕ) (π : CuspSub E a),
      IsNormalised π → AscEmb V π d → HasSpehComp V π d

attribute [local instance] GlobalTheory.LocalData.complRel GlobalTheory.LocalData.isLocalField

open GlobalTheory.BaseChangeBridge in

theorem definiteN_of : BaseChangeIsobaricN → DefiniteN := by
  intro hBC E _ _ _ hun n hn π hπ
  obtain ⟨c, N, h, V, σ, m, P, H⟩ := hBC E hun n hn π hπ
  refine ⟨c, N, h, V, σ, m, ?_⟩
  have hirr : ∀ w, (locProd h w (σ · w)).IsIrreducible := fun w =>
    (UpperTheory.Lit.Mix.multiProd_at (@UpperTheory.Lit.Mix.twoAt_charZero _ _ _ _ _ (charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap E _).injective)) (GlobalTheory.SmGL.stdBlocks N h)
      (fun i => (σ i w).smooth) (fun i => (σ i w).irred)
      (fun i => GlobalTheory.DiscreteUnit.isUnitarisable_of_isLocCompGL E (N i) (V i) (H.discrete i) w (σ i w)
        (H.loc i w)))
  exact
    { pos := H.pos
      discrete := H.discrete
      csd := H.csd
      loc := H.loc
      inf := H.inf
      unram := fun w hw => by
        obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := exists_equiv_of_isSubquotient (P w).irred (hirr w) (H.sub w)
        exact isSpherical_of_equiv e he (H.unram w hw)
      inert := fun w s ϖ q hs hq α hα => by
        obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := exists_equiv_of_isSubquotient (P w).irred (hirr w) (H.sub w)
        exact hasSatGL_of_equiv e he (H.inert w s ϖ q hs hq α hα)
      split := fun w hw => by
        obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := exists_equiv_of_isSubquotient (P w).irred (hirr w) (H.sub w)
        exact isCompAlong_of_equiv e he (hirr w) (H.split w hw)
      weight := H.weight }

theorem bridge : BaseChangeIsobaricN → GlobalTheory.Published.BernsteinUnitaryProductIrreducible → DefiniteN :=
  fun h _ => definiteN_of h

def splitCentre (t : ℝ) : GA E a :=
  infGL E a (Units.map (Matrix.scalar (Fin a)).toMonoidHom (expU fun _ : InfinitePlace E => (t : ℂ)))

variable (E a) in

def latticeGL : Subgroup (GA E a) := (ratGL E a).range ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers (splitCentre E a 1)

def IsSqInt (φ : GA E a → ℂ) : Prop :=
  (∀ t g, φ (splitCentre E a t * g) = φ g) ∧
    letI : MeasurableSpace (GA E a) := borel _
    ∃ (μ : Measure (GA E a)) (𝓕 : Set (GA E a)), μ.IsHaarMeasure ∧ IsFundamentalDomain (latticeGL E a) 𝓕 μ ∧
      ∫⁻ g in 𝓕, ‖φ g‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂μ < ⊤

structure IsDiscrete (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)) : Prop where
  aut : ∀ φ ∈ V, IsAutForm φ
  sqInt : ∀ φ ∈ V, IsSqInt φ.1
  stable : IsStable V
  ne_bot : V ≠ ⊥
  irred : ∀ W ≤ V, IsStable W → W = ⊥ ∨ W = V

def IsLocCompGL (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))
    (σ : SmIrr (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))) : Prop :=
  ∃ f : σ.V →ₗ[ℂ] Sm E a, f ≠ 0 ∧ (∀ v, f v ∈ V) ∧ ∀ g v, f (σ.ρ g v) = finRep E a (locGL E a w g) (f v)

def HasInfCharGL (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)) (τ : E →+* ℂ) (m : Multiset ℂ) : Prop :=
  Multiset.card m = a ∧ ∀ u, ∀ φ ∈ V, cap τ u φ = (m.map (u + ·)).prod • φ

attribute [local instance] GlobalTheory.LocalData.complRel GlobalTheory.LocalData.isLocalField in
omit [IsTotallyComplex E] in

def locProd {c n : ℕ} {N : Fin c → ℕ} (h : ∑ i, N i = n) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))
    (σ : ∀ i, SmIrr (GL (Fin (N i)) (w.adicCompletion E))) :=
  GlobalTheory.SmGL.multiProd (GlobalTheory.SmGL.stdBlocks N h) fun i => (σ i).ρ

end Linear

theorem lintegral_lt_top {μ μ' : Measure G} [μ.IsHaarMeasure] [μ'.IsHaarMeasure] {F F' : Set G}
    (hF : IsFundamentalDomain Γ F μ) (hF' : IsFundamentalDomain Γ F' μ') {φ : G → ENNReal}
    (hφ : ∀ γ ∈ Γ, ∀ g, φ (γ * g) = φ g) (h : ∫⁻ g in F', φ g ∂μ' < ⊤) : ∫⁻ g in F, φ g ∂μ < ⊤ := by
  have e := Measure.isMulLeftInvariant_eq_smul μ μ'
  have hF2 : IsFundamentalDomain Γ F' μ := hF'.mono (Measure.absolutelyContinuous_isHaarMeasure μ μ')
  rw [hF.setLIntegral_eq hF2 φ fun γ g => hφ γ γ.2 g, e, Measure.restrict_smul, lintegral_smul_measure]
  exact ENNReal.mul_lt_top ENNReal.coe_lt_top h

theorem integral_mul_right {μ : Measure G} [μ.IsHaarMeasure] {F : Set G} (hF : IsFundamentalDomain Γ F μ) {φ : G → ℂ}
    (hφ : ∀ γ ∈ Γ, ∀ g, φ (γ * g) = φ g) {x : G} (hx : Measure.modularCharacter x = 1) :
    ∫ g in F, φ (g * x) ∂μ = ∫ g in F, φ g ∂μ := by
  have hm : MeasurePreserving (fun g => g * x) μ μ :=
    ⟨measurable_mul_const x, by rw [GlobalTheory.LevelUnimod.map_mul_right_eq, hx, one_smul]⟩
  have hF2 : IsFundamentalDomain Γ ((fun g => g * x) ⁻¹' F) μ :=
    hF.preimage_of_equiv hm.quasiMeasurePreserving Function.bijective_id fun γ g => mul_assoc γ.1 g x
  rw [hF.setIntegral_eq hF2 (f := fun g => φ (g * x)) fun γ g => by
    change φ (γ.1 * g * x) = _
    rw [mul_assoc, hφ γ γ.2]]
  exact hm.setIntegral_preimage_emb (MeasurableEquiv.mulRight x).measurableEmbedding φ F

theorem isUnitarisable_of_embedding {H X : Type} [Group H] [AddCommGroup X] [Module ℂ X] (ρ : Representation ℂ H X)
    (ι : H → G) (hι : ∀ h, Measure.modularCharacter (ι h) = 1) (f : X →ₗ[ℂ] (G → ℂ)) (hf : Function.Injective f)
    (hc : ∀ v, Continuous (f v)) (hl : ∀ v, ∀ γ ∈ Γ, ∀ g, f v (γ * g) = f v g)
    (h2 : ∀ v, ∃ (μ : Measure G) (F : Set G), μ.IsHaarMeasure ∧ IsFundamentalDomain Γ F μ ∧
      ∫⁻ g in F, ‖f v g‖ₑ ^ 2 ∂μ < ⊤)
    (hρ : ∀ h v g, f (ρ h v) g = f v (g * ι h)) : IsUnitarisable ρ := by
  obtain ⟨μ, F, hμ, hF, -⟩ := h2 0
  have hm : ∀ v, MemLp (f v) 2 (μ.restrict F) := fun v => by
    obtain ⟨μ', F', hμ', hF', h⟩ := h2 v
    have h3 := lintegral_lt_top hF hF' (fun γ hγ g => by rw [hl v γ hγ g]) h
    refine ⟨(hc v).aestronglyMeasurable, ?_⟩
    rw [eLpNorm_lt_top_iff_lintegral_rpow_enorm_lt_top two_ne_zero ENNReal.ofNat_ne_top]
    simpa using h3
  have hi : ∀ v w, Integrable (fun g => f v g * starRingEnd ℂ (f w g)) (μ.restrict F) := fun v w =>
    (hm v).integrable_mul (hm w).star
  refine ⟨LinearMap.mk₂'ₛₗ (RingHom.id ℂ) (starRingEnd ℂ) (fun v w => ∫ g in F, f v g * starRingEnd ℂ (f w g) ∂μ)
    (fun v v' w => ?_) (fun c v w => ?_) (fun v w w' => ?_) (fun c v w => ?_), fun v w => ?_, fun v hv => ?_,
    fun h v w => ?_⟩
  · rw [← integral_add (hi _ _) (hi _ _)]
    simp [add_mul]
  · simp [mul_assoc, integral_const_mul]
  · rw [← integral_add (hi _ _) (hi _ _)]
    simp [mul_add]
  · simp [mul_left_comm _ (starRingEnd ℂ c), integral_const_mul]
  · simp only [LinearMap.mk₂'ₛₗ_apply]
    rw [← integral_conj]
    simp [mul_comm]
  · simp only [LinearMap.mk₂'ₛₗ_apply, Complex.mul_conj]
    rw [integral_complex_ofReal, Complex.ofReal_re]
    have hi2 : Integrable (fun g => Complex.normSq (f v g)) (μ.restrict F) := by
      simpa [Complex.mul_conj] using (hi v v).re
    refine (integral_pos_iff_support_of_nonneg (fun g => Complex.normSq_nonneg _) hi2).2 (pos_iff_ne_zero.2 fun h0 => hv ?_)
    have ho : IsOpen (Function.support fun g => Complex.normSq (f v g)) :=
      (Complex.continuous_normSq.comp (hc v)).isOpen_support
    rw [Measure.restrict_apply ho.measurableSet] at h0
    have h4 := hF.measure_zero_of_invariant _ (fun γ => by
      ext g
      rw [Set.mem_smul_set_iff_inv_smul_mem, Function.mem_support, Function.mem_support]
      change Complex.normSq (f v ((γ⁻¹ : Γ).1 * g)) ≠ 0 ↔ _
      rw [hl v _ (γ⁻¹).2]) h0
    have h5 := Function.support_eq_empty_iff.1 (ho.measure_eq_zero_iff μ |>.1 h4)
    refine hf (funext fun g => ?_)
    rw [map_zero]

theorem modular_eq_one {k S : Type} [Field k] [Infinite k] [CommRing S] [TopologicalSpace S] [IsTopologicalRing S] {n : ℕ}
    [LocallyCompactSpace (GL (Fin n) S)] [SecondCountableTopology (GL (Fin n) S)] (ι : GL (Fin n) k →* GL (Fin n) S)
    (h : ∀ g, ι (transInv k n g) = transInv S n (ι g)) (g : GL (Fin n) k) : Measure.modularCharacter (ι g) = 1 := by
  borelize (GL (Fin n) S)
  refine GlobalTheory.gl_character_eq_one_of_transpose_inv _ _ (Measure.modularCharacter.comp ι) (fun g g' e => ?_) g
  obtain rfl : g' = transInv _ n g := Units.ext e
  rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, MonoidHom.comp_apply, h]
  exact GlobalTheory.LevelUnimod.modular_aut _ (continuous_transInv _ n) (continuous_transInv _ n) _

variable {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] {N : ℕ}

theorem countable_latticeGL : Countable (latticeGL E N) := by
  haveI : Countable E := (Module.finBasis ℚ E).equivFun.injective.countable
  haveI : Countable (Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) E) := inferInstanceAs (Countable (Fin N → Fin N → E))
  haveI : Countable (GL (Fin N) E) := Units.val_injective.countable
  let f : FreeGroup (Option (GL (Fin N) E)) →* GA E N :=
    FreeGroup.lift fun o => o.elim (splitCentre E N 1) (ratGL E N)
  have h : latticeGL E N ≤ f.range := sup_le
    (by rintro _ ⟨γ, rfl⟩; exact ⟨.of (some γ), FreeGroup.lift_apply_of⟩)
    (Subgroup.zpowers_le.2 ⟨.of none, FreeGroup.lift_apply_of⟩)
  haveI : Countable (FreeGroup (Option (GL (Fin N) E))) := Quot.mk_surjective.countable
  exact ((Set.countable_range f).mono h).to_subtype

theorem left_latticeGL {φ : Sm E N} (h : IsAutForm φ) (h' : IsSqInt φ.1) :
    ∀ γ ∈ latticeGL E N, ∀ g, φ.1 (γ * g) = φ.1 g := by
  let S : Subgroup (GA E N) :=
    { carrier := {γ | ∀ g, φ.1 (γ * g) = φ.1 g}
      one_mem' := fun g => by rw [one_mul]
      mul_mem' := fun {x y} hx hy g => by rw [mul_assoc, hx, hy]
      inv_mem' := fun {x} hx g => by rw [← hx, mul_inv_cancel_left] }
  have : latticeGL E N ≤ S := sup_le (by rintro _ ⟨γ, rfl⟩; exact h.left γ) (Subgroup.zpowers_le.2 (h'.1 1))
  exact this

theorem isUnitarisable_of_isLocCompGL : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (N : ℕ)
    (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)), IsDiscrete V → ∀ (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (σ : LocIrr w N),
    IsLocCompGL V w σ → IsUnitarisable σ.ρ := by
  intro E _ _ _ N V hV w σ ⟨f, h0, hf, hρ⟩
  letI : MeasurableSpace (GA E N) := borel _
  haveI : BorelSpace (GA E N) := ⟨rfl⟩
  haveI := GlobalTheory.AdelicUnitaryTop.locallyCompact_adele E
  haveI := GlobalTheory.AdelicUnitaryTop.secondCountable_adele E
  haveI := GlobalTheory.AdelicUnitaryTop.t2Space_adeleRing_numberField E
  haveI : LocallyCompactSpace (Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (Ad E)) :=
    inferInstanceAs (LocallyCompactSpace (Fin N → Fin N → Ad E))
  haveI : LocallyCompactSpace (Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (Ad E))ᵐᵒᵖ :=
    MulOpposite.opHomeomorph.symm.isClosedEmbedding.locallyCompactSpace
  haveI : LocallyCompactSpace (GA E N) := Units.isClosedEmbedding_embedProduct.locallyCompactSpace
  haveI : SecondCountableTopology (Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (Ad E)) :=
    inferInstanceAs (SecondCountableTopology (Fin N → Fin N → Ad E))
  haveI : SecondCountableTopology (Matrix (Fin N) (Fin N) (Ad E))ᵐᵒᵖ :=
    MulOpposite.opHomeomorph.symm.isEmbedding.secondCountableTopology
  haveI : SecondCountableTopology (GA E N) := Units.isEmbedding_embedProduct.secondCountableTopology
  haveI := countable_latticeGL (E := E) (N := N)
  haveI : Infinite (w.adicCompletion E) := .of_injective _ (algebraMap E _).injective
  have hinj : Function.Injective f := by
    rcases ((GlobalTheory.CuspRep.isIrreducible_iff _).1 σ.irred).2 (LinearMap.ker f) (fun g v hv => by
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at hv ⊢
      rw [hρ, hv, map_zero]) with h | h
    · exact LinearMap.ker_eq_bot.1 h
    · exact absurd (LinearMap.ker_eq_top.1 h) h0
  refine isUnitarisable_of_embedding (Γ := latticeGL E N) σ.ρ (fun g => finGL E N (locGL E N w g)) (fun g => ?_)
    ((Sm E N).subtype.comp f) (Subtype.val_injective.comp hinj)
    (fun v => GlobalTheory.CuspRep.ContAut.continuous_of_isOpen_stab E N (f v) (hV.aut _ (hf v)).level)
    (fun v => left_latticeGL (hV.aut _ (hf v)) (hV.sqInt _ (hf v))) (fun v => (hV.sqInt _ (hf v)).2)
    fun g v x => congrFun (congrArg Subtype.val (hρ g v)) x
  exact modular_eq_one ((finGL E N).comp (locGL E N w))
    (fun g => (congrArg (finGL E N) (cornerGL_transInv (sLoc E w) g)).trans (cornerGL_transInv (sFin E) _)) g

end GlobalTheory.DiscreteUnit

def CuspDiscreteDecompositionGL : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (a : ℕ) (μ : Measure (GA E a)) [μ.IsHaarMeasure]
    [μ.IsMulRightInvariant], IsDiscreteSum (Rcusp μ)

def CuspFiniteVectorsGL : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (a : ℕ) (μ : Measure (GA E a)) [μ.IsHaarMeasure]
    [μ.IsMulRightInvariant] (H : Submodule ℂ (L2 (latticeGL E a) μ)), H ≤ L2cusp μ →
      IsIrredSubrep (R (latticeGL E a) μ) H → ∃ π : CuspSub E a, IsFinitePartOf μ π H

def CuspFormsBoundedGL : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (a : ℕ) (φ : Sm E a), IsCuspForm φ →
    (∀ t g, ‖φ.1 (splitCentre E a t * g)‖ = ‖φ.1 g‖) → ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ g, ‖φ.1 g‖ ≤ C

def CuspidalSpectrumGL : Prop := CuspDiscreteDecompositionGL ∧ CuspFiniteVectorsGL ∧ CuspFormsBoundedGL

variable {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] {a : ℕ}

theorem CuspFiniteVectorsGL.of_Rcusp (h : CuspFiniteVectorsGL) {μ : Measure (GA E a)} [μ.IsHaarMeasure]
    [μ.IsMulRightInvariant] {W : Submodule ℂ (L2cusp μ)} (hW : IsIrredSubrep (Rcusp μ) W) :
    ∃ π : CuspSub E a, IsFinitePartOf μ π (W.map (L2cusp μ).subtype) :=
  h E a μ _ (Submodule.map_subtype_le _ _) (isIrredSubrep_map hW)

theorem CuspFormsBoundedGL.of_isUnitary (h : CuspFormsBoundedGL) {π : CuspSub E a} (hπ : π.IsUnitary) {φ : Sm E a}
    (hφ : φ ∈ π.V) : ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ g, ‖φ.1 g‖ ≤ C :=
  h E a φ (π.cusp φ hφ) fun t g => by rw [splitCentre_eq_scalar, hπ.norm_eq hφ]

theorem CuspFormsBoundedGL.of_invariant (h : CuspFormsBoundedGL) {φ : Sm E a} (hφ : IsCuspForm φ)
    (h' : ∀ t g, φ.1 (splitCentre E a t * g) = φ.1 g) : ∃ C : ℝ, ∀ g, ‖φ.1 g‖ ≤ C :=
  h E a φ hφ fun t g => by rw [h']

theorem CuspFormsBoundedGL.isUnitary (h : CuspFormsBoundedGL) {π : CuspSub E a} (hπ : IsNormalised π) :
    π.IsUnitary := by

theorem isDiscrete_of_cusp {a : ℕ} {π : CuspSub E a} (hu : π.IsUnitary) (hn : IsNormalised π) : IsDiscrete π.V := by
  refine ⟨fun φ hφ => (π.cusp φ hφ).1, fun φ hφ => ?_, π.stable, π.ne_bot, π.irred⟩
  obtain ⟨C, hC⟩ := GlobalTheory.CompanionTheory.CuspFinGL.cuspFormsBoundedGL.of_isUnitary hu hφ
  exact GlobalTheory.DiscChar.isSqInt_of_bounded (hn φ hφ) hC

theorem locCompGL_of_locComp {a : ℕ} {π : CuspSub E a} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)}
    {σ : SmIrr (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))} (hσ : π.IsLocComp w σ) : IsLocCompGL π.V w σ := by
  obtain ⟨f, h0, h1⟩ := hσ
  exact ⟨π.V.subtype.comp f, fun e => h0 (LinearMap.ext fun v => Subtype.ext (LinearMap.congr_fun e v)),
    fun v => (f v).2, fun g v => congrArg Subtype.val (h1 g v)⟩

theorem generic_unitarisable {a : ℕ} {π : CuspSub E a} (hu : π.IsUnitary) (hn : IsNormalised π)
    {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)} {σ : SmIrr (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))} (hσ : π.IsLocComp w σ) :
    GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsGeneric (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred).rep ∧
      GlobalTheory.SmGL.IsUnitarisable (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred).rep := by
  let e := GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.equivOf σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred
  have hg : GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsGeneric σ.ρ := GlobalTheory.CuspRep.Generic.isGeneric_of_isLocComp E a π w σ hσ
  have hv : GlobalTheory.SmGL.IsUnitarisable σ.ρ :=
    GlobalTheory.DiscreteUnit.isUnitarisable_of_isLocCompGL E a π.V (isDiscrete_of_cusp hu hn) w σ
      (locCompGL_of_locComp hσ)
  exact ⟨GlobalTheory.CompanionTheory.BZSeg.isGeneric_of_surjective e.symm.toIntertwiningMap e.symm.toLinearEquiv.surjective hg,
    GlobalTheory.GenericUnitary.isUnitarisable_of_equiv e hv⟩

end PositivityTheory.RSHS.LocalComponentGL

def BernsteinUnitaryProductIrreducible : Prop :=
  ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]
    (r t : ℕ) (V W : Type) [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W]
    (π₁ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin r) k) V) (π₂ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin t) k) W),
    IsSmooth π₁ → π₁.IsIrreducible → IsUnitarisable π₁ →
    IsSmooth π₂ → π₂.IsIrreducible → IsUnitarisable π₂ →
    (prod₂ π₁ π₂).IsIrreducible

import Mathlib
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.PublishedTheorems.GaloisCohomology₂.StandardCosocleGL
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.InertiaEigenvalues.ExistsEquivProdIrrTw
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.SatakeParameters.MultiProdCompCongr
import BuildAid.D3

noncomputable section

open GlobalTheory.PRep GlobalTheory.SmGL GlobalTheory.LocGL GlobalTheory.ProdReorder

namespace GlobalTheory.ProdFlatten

variable {k : Type} [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]

structure BR (k : Type) [Field k] (n : ℕ) : Type 1 where

  V : Type
  [ac : AddCommGroup V]
  [smul : Module ℂ V]

  ρ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) k) V

attribute [instance] BR.ac BR.smul

def one (w : IrrGL k) : BR k w.1 := ⟨_, w.rep⟩

def two {r t N : ℕ} (e : r + t = N) (A : BR k r) (C : BR k t) : BR k N :=
  ⟨_, ind₂ (stdSum e) (extTensor A.ρ C.ρ)⟩

def mp {d M : ℕ} (N : Fin d → ℕ) (hM : ∑ i, N i = M) (A : ∀ i, BR k (N i)) : BR k M :=
  ⟨_, multiProd (stdBlocks N hM) fun i => (A i).ρ⟩

def pr {a N : ℕ} (x : Fin a → IrrGL k) (hx : ∑ i, (x i).1 = N) : BR k N :=
  mp (fun i => (x i).1) hx fun i => one (x i)

def Iso {n : ℕ} (A C : BR k n) : Prop := Nonempty (A.ρ.Equiv C.ρ)

def Sm {n : ℕ} (A : BR k n) : Prop := IsSmooth A.ρ

theorem iso_refl {n : ℕ} (A : BR k n) : Iso A A := eqv_refl A.ρ

theorem iso_symm {n : ℕ} {A C : BR k n} (h : Iso A C) : Iso C A := eqv_symm (ρ := A.ρ) (σ := C.ρ) h

theorem iso_trans {n : ℕ} {A C D : BR k n} (h : Iso A C) (h' : Iso C D) : Iso A D :=
  eqv_trans (ρ := A.ρ) (σ := C.ρ) (τ := D.ρ) h h'

theorem sm_one (w : IrrGL k) : Sm (one w) := w.2.rep.smooth

theorem sm_pr {a N : ℕ} (x : Fin a → IrrGL k) (hx : ∑ i, (x i).1 = N) : Sm (pr x hx) := isSmooth_prodIrr x hx

theorem two_congr {r t N : ℕ} (e : r + t = N) {A A' : BR k r} {C C' : BR k t} (h : Iso A A') (h' : Iso C C') :
    Iso (two e A C) (two e A' C') :=
  ind₂_congr e (π₁ := A.ρ) (σ₁ := A'.ρ) (π₂ := C.ρ) (σ₂ := C'.ρ) h h'

theorem two_assoc {r s t M M' N : ℕ} (h₁ : r + s = M) (h₂ : M + t = N) (h₃ : s + t = M') (h₄ : r + M' = N)
    (A : BR k r) (C : BR k s) (D : BR k t) (s₁ : Sm A) (s₂ : Sm C) (s₃ : Sm D) :
    Iso (two h₂ (two h₁ A C) D) (two h₄ A (two h₃ C D)) :=
  ind₂_assoc' h₁ h₂ h₃ h₄ A.ρ C.ρ D.ρ s₁ s₂ s₃

theorem mp_head {d T M : ℕ} (N : Fin (d + 1) → ℕ) (hM : ∑ i, N i = M) (A : ∀ i, BR k (N i))
    (hT : ∑ i : Fin d, N i.succ = T) (e : N 0 + T = M) :
    Iso (mp N hM A) (two e (A 0) (mp (fun i : Fin d => N i.succ) hT fun i => A i.succ)) :=
  multiProd_head (d := N) (X := fun i => (A i).V) (fun i => (A i).ρ) (stdBlocks N hM) e
    (stdBlocks (fun i : Fin d => N i.succ) hT)

theorem mp_empty {M : ℕ} (N N' : Fin 0 → ℕ) (hM : ∑ i, N i = M) (hM' : ∑ i, N' i = M) (A : ∀ i, BR k (N i))
    (A' : ∀ i, BR k (N' i)) : Iso (mp N hM A) (mp N' hM' A') :=
  multiProd_empty (d := N) (d' := N') (X := fun i => (A i).V) (X' := fun i => (A' i).V) (fun i => (A i).ρ)
    (fun i => (A' i).ρ) (stdBlocks N hM) (stdBlocks N' hM')

theorem two_unit {r t : ℕ} (e : r + t = r) (A : BR k r) (hA : Sm A) (N : Fin 0 → ℕ) (hN : ∑ i, N i = t)
    (C : ∀ i, BR k (N i)) : Iso (two e A (mp N hN C)) A :=
  ind₂_unit_right e A.ρ hA (d := N) (X := fun i => (C i).V) (fun i => (C i).ρ) (stdBlocks N hN)

theorem two_unit_pr {r t : ℕ} (e : r + t = r) (A : BR k r) (hA : Sm A) (y : Fin 0 → IrrGL k)
    (hy : ∑ i, (y i).1 = t) : Iso (two e A (pr y hy)) A :=
  two_unit e A hA _ hy _

theorem mp_empty_pr {M : ℕ} (N : Fin 0 → ℕ) (hM : ∑ i, N i = M) (A : ∀ i, BR k (N i)) (y : Fin 0 → IrrGL k)
    (hy : ∑ i, (y i).1 = M) : Iso (mp N hM A) (pr y hy) :=
  mp_empty N _ hM hy A _

theorem pr_succ {a t N : ℕ} (z : Fin (a + 1) → IrrGL k)
    (hx : ∑ i : Fin a, (z i.succ).1 = t) (hz : ∑ i, (z i).1 = N) (e : (z 0).1 + t = N) :
    Iso (pr z hz) (two e (one (z 0)) (pr (fun i : Fin a => z i.succ) hx)) :=
  mp_head (fun i => (z i).1) hz (fun i => one (z i)) hx e

theorem of_iso {d h : ℕ} (y : Fin d → Fin h → IrrGL k) (N : Fin d → ℕ) {M c : ℕ} (hN : ∀ i, ∑ j, (y i j).1 = N i)
    (hM : ∑ i, N i = M) (z : Fin c → IrrGL k) (hz : ∑ p, (z p).1 = M)
    (H : Iso (mp N hM fun i => pr (y i) (hN i)) (pr z hz)) :
    Nonempty ((multiProd (stdBlocks N hM) fun i => prodIrr (y i) (hN i)).Equiv (prodIrr z hz)) := H

attribute [irreducible] one two mp pr Iso Sm

theorem pr_congr {a b N : ℕ} {x : Fin a → IrrGL k} {z : Fin b → IrrGL k} (h : List.ofFn x = List.ofFn z)
    (hx : ∑ i, (x i).1 = N) (hz : ∑ i, (z i).1 = N) : Iso (pr x hx) (pr z hz) := by
  obtain rfl : a = b := by simpa using congrArg List.length h
  obtain rfl := List.ofFn_injective h
  exact iso_refl _

theorem pr_cons {a b t N : ℕ} {w : IrrGL k} {x : Fin a → IrrGL k} {z : Fin b → IrrGL k}
    (h : List.ofFn z = w :: List.ofFn x) (hx : ∑ i, (x i).1 = t) (hz : ∑ i, (z i).1 = N) (e : w.1 + t = N) :
    Iso (pr z hz) (two e (one w) (pr x hx)) := by
  have hb : b = a + 1 := by simpa using congrArg List.length h
  subst hb
  have h' : z 0 :: List.ofFn (fun i : Fin a => z i.succ) = w :: List.ofFn x := List.ofFn_succ.symm.trans h
  have h₁ : z 0 = w := (List.cons.inj h').1
  have h₂ : (fun i : Fin a => z i.succ) = x := List.ofFn_injective (List.cons.inj h').2
  subst h₁ h₂
  exact pr_succ z hx hz e

theorem pr_append {a : ℕ} : ∀ {b c r t N : ℕ} (x : Fin a → IrrGL k) (y : Fin b → IrrGL k) (z : Fin c → IrrGL k)
    (_ : List.ofFn z = List.ofFn x ++ List.ofFn y) (_ : a ≠ 0 ∨ b = 0) (hx : ∑ i, (x i).1 = r)
    (hy : ∑ i, (y i).1 = t) (hz : ∑ i, (z i).1 = N) (e : r + t = N),
    Iso (pr z hz) (two e (pr x hx) (pr y hy)) := by
  induction a with
  | zero =>
    intro b c r t N x y z h hab hx hy hz e
    obtain rfl : b = 0 := by simpa using hab
    obtain rfl : t = 0 := by simpa using hy.symm
    have e0 : N = r := e.symm
    subst e0
    exact iso_trans (pr_congr (by simpa using h) hz hx) (iso_symm (two_unit_pr e _ (sm_pr x hx) y hy))
  | succ a ih =>
    intro b c r t N x y z h _ hx hy hz e
    have hx' : List.ofFn x = x 0 :: List.ofFn fun i : Fin a => x i.succ := List.ofFn_succ
    have hs : (x 0).1 + ∑ i : Fin a, (x i.succ).1 = r := (Fin.sum_univ_succ fun i => (x i).1).symm.trans hx
    have A := pr_cons hx' rfl hx hs
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos a with rfl | ha
    · obtain rfl : (x 0).1 = r := by simpa using hs
      have B := iso_trans A (two_unit_pr hs _ (sm_one (x 0)) (fun i : Fin 0 => x i.succ) rfl)
      refine iso_trans (pr_cons (w := x 0) (by simpa using h) hy hz e) ?_
      exact two_congr e (iso_symm B) (iso_refl _)
    · let L := (List.ofFn fun i : Fin a => x i.succ) ++ List.ofFn y
      have hL : List.ofFn L.get = L := List.ofFn_get L
      have hT : ∑ i, (L.get i).1 = ∑ i : Fin a, (x i.succ).1 + t := by
        have := congrArg (fun l : List (IrrGL k) => (l.map Sigma.fst).sum) hL
        simp only [L, List.map_append, List.sum_append, List.map_ofFn, List.sum_ofFn, Function.comp_def] at this
        rw [← hy]
        exact this
      have e' : (x 0).1 + (∑ i : Fin a, (x i.succ).1 + t) = N := by omega
      have B := pr_cons (w := x 0) (z := z) (x := L.get) (by rw [h, hx', hL]; rfl) hT hz e'
      have C := ih (fun i => x i.succ) y L.get hL (Or.inl ha.ne') rfl hy hT rfl
      have D := two_assoc hs e rfl e' (one (x 0)) (pr (fun i : Fin a => x i.succ) rfl) (pr y hy)
        (sm_one _) (sm_pr _ _) (sm_pr _ _)
      exact iso_trans B (iso_trans (two_congr e' (iso_refl _) C) (iso_trans (iso_symm D)
        (two_congr e (iso_symm A) (iso_refl _))))

theorem ofFn_lex {α : Type*} {d h : ℕ} (y : Fin d → Fin h → α) :
    List.ofFn (fun p : Fin (d * h) => y (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).1 (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2) =
      (List.ofFn fun i => List.ofFn (y i)).flatten := by
  rw [List.ofFn_mul]
  refine congrArg List.flatten (congrArg List.ofFn (funext fun i => congrArg List.ofFn (funext fun j => ?_)))
  have key : ∀ p : Fin (d * h), p = finProdFinEquiv (i, j) →
      y (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).1 (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2 = y i j := by
    rintro _ rfl
    rw [Equiv.symm_apply_apply]
  exact key _ (Fin.ext (by simp; ring))

theorem mp_pr_iso {h : ℕ} (d : ℕ) : ∀ (y : Fin d → Fin h → IrrGL k) (N : Fin d → ℕ) (M : ℕ)
    (hN : ∀ i, ∑ j, (y i j).1 = N i) (hM : ∑ i, N i = M)
    (hM' : ∑ p : Fin (d * h), (y (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).1 (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2).1 = M),
    Iso (mp N hM fun i => pr (y i) (hN i))
      (pr (fun p => y (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).1 (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2) hM') := by
  induction d with
  | zero =>
    intro y N M hN hM hM'
    obtain rfl : M = 0 := by simpa using hM.symm
    let z : Fin 0 → IrrGL k := fun i => i.elim0
    have hz : ∑ i, (z i).1 = 0 := by simp
    exact iso_trans (mp_empty_pr N hM _ z hz) (pr_congr (x := z) (by rw [ofFn_lex]; simp) hz hM')
  | succ d ih =>
    intro y N M hN hM hM'
    have e : N 0 + ∑ i : Fin d, N i.succ = M := (Fin.sum_univ_succ N).symm.trans hM
    have hT : ∑ p : Fin (d * h), (y (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).1.succ (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2).1 =
        ∑ i : Fin d, N i.succ := by
      rw [Equiv.sum_comp finProdFinEquiv.symm fun q : Fin d × Fin h => (y q.1.succ q.2).1, Fintype.sum_prod_type]
      exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => hN _
    have A := mp_head N hM (fun i => pr (y i) (hN i)) rfl e
    have B := ih (fun i => y i.succ) (fun i => N i.succ) _ (fun i => hN _) rfl hT
    have C := pr_append (y 0) _ _
      (by rw [ofFn_lex y, ofFn_lex fun i : Fin d => y i.succ, List.ofFn_succ, List.flatten_cons])
      (by rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos h with rfl | hh; exacts [Or.inr rfl, Or.inl hh.ne']) (hN 0) hT hM' e
    exact iso_trans A (iso_trans (two_congr e (iso_refl _) B) (iso_symm C))

theorem multiProd_prodIrr_equiv : ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k]
    [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]
    (d h : ℕ) (y : Fin d → Fin h → IrrGL k) (N : Fin d → ℕ) (M : ℕ) (hN : ∀ i, ∑ j, (y i j).1 = N i)
    (hM : ∑ i, N i = M)
    (hM' : ∑ p : Fin (d * h), (y (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).1 (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2).1 = M),
    Nonempty ((multiProd (stdBlocks N hM) fun i => prodIrr (y i) (hN i)).Equiv
      (prodIrr (fun p => y (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).1 (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2) hM')) :=
  fun _ _ _ _ _ d _ y N M hN hM hM' => of_iso y N hN hM _ hM' (mp_pr_iso d y N M hN hM hM')

end GlobalTheory.ProdFlatten

theorem exists_equiv_of_isSubquotient {σ : Representation ℂ G W} {ρ : Representation ℂ G V}
    (hσ : σ.IsIrreducible) (hρ : ρ.IsIrreducible) (h : GlobalTheory.SmGL.IsSubquotient σ ρ) :
    ∃ e : W ≃ₗ[ℂ] V, ∀ g x, e (σ g x) = ρ g (e x) := by
  obtain ⟨S, f, hf⟩ := h
  obtain ⟨hW, -⟩ := (GlobalTheory.CuspRep.isIrreducible_iff σ).1 hσ
  obtain ⟨-, hρ'⟩ := (GlobalTheory.CuspRep.isIrreducible_iff ρ).1 hρ
  have hS : S.toSubmodule = ⊤ := by
    rcases hρ' S.toSubmodule (fun g v hv => S.apply_mem_toSubmodule g hv) with e | e
    · exfalso
      obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := exists_ne (0 : W)
      obtain ⟨v, rfl⟩ := hf x
      have hv : v = 0 := by
        exact Subtype.ext ((Submodule.eq_bot_iff _).1 e v v.2)
      subst hv
      exact hx (map_zero f.toLinearMap)
    · exact e
  let g : V →ₗ[ℂ] W := f.toLinearMap ∘ₗ (LinearEquiv.ofTop S.toSubmodule hS).symm.toLinearMap
  have hg : ∀ x v, g (ρ x v) = σ x (g v) := fun x v => by
    have := LinearMap.congr_fun (f.isIntertwining' x) ((LinearEquiv.ofTop S.toSubmodule hS).symm v)
    exact this
  have gsurj : Function.Surjective g := fun y => by
    obtain ⟨v, rfl⟩ := hf y
    refine ⟨(v : V), ?_⟩
    change f ((LinearEquiv.ofTop S.toSubmodule hS).symm (v : V)) = f v
    congr 1
  have ginj : Function.Injective g := by
    rcases hρ' (LinearMap.ker g) (fun x v hv => by
        rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at hv ⊢
        rw [hg, hv, map_zero]) with e | e
    · exact LinearMap.ker_eq_bot.1 e
    · exfalso
      obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := exists_ne (0 : W)
      obtain ⟨v, rfl⟩ := gsurj y
      exact hy (LinearMap.mem_ker.1 (e ▸ Submodule.mem_top))
  let e0 := LinearEquiv.ofBijective g ⟨ginj, gsurj⟩
  refine ⟨e0.symm, fun x y => e0.injective ?_⟩
  rw [LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply]
  change σ x y = g (ρ x (e0.symm y))
  rw [hg]
  change σ x y = σ x (e0 (e0.symm y))
  rw [LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply]

variable {H : Type} [Group H]

theorem isSpherical_of_equiv {σ : Representation ℂ H W} {ρ : Representation ℂ H V} (e : W ≃ₗ[ℂ] V)
    (he : ∀ g x, e (σ g x) = ρ g (e x)) {K : Subgroup H} (h : GlobalTheory.LGrp.IsSpherical σ K) :
    GlobalTheory.LGrp.IsSpherical ρ K := by
  obtain ⟨x, hx, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 h
  exact (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨e x, GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.2 fun k hk => by rw [← he, GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.1 hx k hk],
    by simpa using h0⟩

theorem symm_equivariant {σ : Representation ℂ H W} {ρ : Representation ℂ H V} (e : W ≃ₗ[ℂ] V)
    (he : ∀ g x, e (σ g x) = ρ g (e x)) (g : H) (y : V) : e.symm (ρ g y) = σ g (e.symm y) :=
  e.injective (by rw [LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply, he, LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply])

end General

theorem hasSatGL_of_equiv {σ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) W}
    {ρ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) V} (e : W ≃ₗ[ℂ] V) (he : ∀ g x, e (σ g x) = ρ g (e x))
    {B : Multiset ℂˣ} (h : GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w σ B) : GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w ρ B := by
  refine ⟨isSpherical_of_equiv e he h.1, fun y hy h0 => ?_⟩
  have hx : e.symm y ∈ GlobalTheory.PRep.fixed σ (GlobalTheory.LFun.KGL w n) := GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.2 fun k hk => by
    rw [← symm_equivariant e he, GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.1 hy k hk]
  have h0' : e.symm y ≠ 0 := by simpa using h0
  have := (h.2 _ hx h0').map e.toLinearMap he (by simpa using h0)
  simpa using this

end Local

theorem isCompAlong_of_equiv {π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ)} {Gv : Subgroup (UA E n)} {H : Type} [Group H]
    {p : Gv →* H} {σ : Representation ℂ H W} {ρ : Representation ℂ H V} (e : W ≃ₗ[ℂ] V)
    (he : ∀ g x, e (σ g x) = ρ g (e x)) (hρ : ρ.IsIrreducible) (h : IsCompAlong π Gv p σ) :
    IsCompAlong π Gv p ρ := by
  obtain ⟨-, f, h0, hf, hfi⟩ := h
  refine ⟨hρ, f ∘ₗ e.symm.toLinearMap, fun h1 => h0 ?_, fun v => hf _, fun x v => ?_⟩
  · refine LinearMap.ext fun y => ?_
    simpa using LinearMap.congr_fun h1 (e y)
  · simp [symm_equivariant e he, hfi]

end CM

def forms (L : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (Af E))) {W : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W]
    (ρ : Representation ℂ (Kinf E a) W) (c : (E →+* ℂ) → ℂ → ℂ) : Set (Sm E a) :=
  {φ | IsAutForm φ ∧ (∀ k ∈ L, finRep E a k φ = φ) ∧
    (∀ g : GA E a, (fun k : Kinf E a => (cptRep E a k φ).1 g) ∈ AutFin.coeffSpan ρ) ∧
    ∀ τ u, cap τ u φ = c τ u • φ}

end AutFinGL

def AutomorphicFormsFiniteGL : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (a : ℕ) (L : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (Af E))),

    AutFin.IsContRep ρ →
  ∀ c : (E →+* ℂ) → ℂ → ℂ, FiniteDimensional ℂ (Submodule.span ℂ (AutFinGL.forms L ρ c))

def nullPart (ρ : Representation ℂ G V) (K : Subgroup G) : Submodule ℂ V := ⨅ g, (twKer ρ K 1).comap (ρ g)

theorem nullPart_act (ρ : Representation ℂ G V) (K : Subgroup G) (g : G) {x : V} (hx : x ∈ nullPart ρ K) :
    ρ g x ∈ nullPart ρ K := by
  simp only [nullPart, Submodule.mem_iInf, Submodule.mem_comap] at hx ⊢
  intro h
  rw [← Module.End.mul_apply, ← map_mul]
  exact hx _

theorem nullPart_comm (ρ : Representation ℂ G V) (K : Subgroup G) (T : Module.End ℂ V)
    (hT : ∀ g x, T (ρ g x) = ρ g (T x)) {x : V} (hx : x ∈ nullPart ρ K) : T x ∈ nullPart ρ K := by
  simp only [nullPart, Submodule.mem_iInf, Submodule.mem_comap] at hx ⊢
  intro h
  rw [← hT]
  have : twKer ρ K 1 ≤ (twKer ρ K 1).comap T := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
    rintro _ ⟨n, v, rfl⟩
    exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨n, T v, by rw [map_sub, map_smul, hT]⟩)
  exact this (hx h)

variable [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G]

theorem le_nullPart {ρ : Representation ℂ G V} (hρ : IsSmooth ρ) {K : Subgroup G} (hK : IsCompact (K : Set G))
    (W : Submodule ℂ V) (hW : ∀ g, ∀ v ∈ W, ρ g v ∈ W) (h : W ⊓ fixed ρ K = ⊥) : W ≤ nullPart ρ K := by
  intro x hx
  simp only [nullPart, Submodule.mem_iInf, Submodule.mem_comap]
  intro g
  rw [← LocGL.idem_eq_zero_iff hρ hK, ← Submodule.mem_bot ℂ, ← h]
  refine ⟨?_, LocGL.idem_mem hρ hK _⟩
  obtain ⟨U, -, _, hU⟩ := LocGL.exists_normal_fix hρ hK (ρ g x)
  letI := Fintype.ofFinite (K ⧸ U)
  rw [LocGL.idem_eq_cosetSum hρ hK U hU]
  exact W.smul_mem _ (W.sum_mem fun q _ => hW _ _ (hW _ _ hx))

theorem eq_zero_of_mem_nullPart {ρ : Representation ℂ G V} (hρ : IsSmooth ρ) {K : Subgroup G}
    (hK : IsCompact (K : Set G)) {x : V} (h1 : x ∈ fixed ρ K) (h2 : x ∈ nullPart ρ K) : x = 0 := by
  have h3 : ρ 1 x ∈ twKer ρ K 1 := (Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 h2 1
  rw [map_one] at h3
  exact Submodule.disjoint_def.1 (LocGL.isCompl_fixed_twKer hρ hK).disjoint x h1 h3

theorem exists_irred {ρ : Representation ℂ G V} (hρ : IsSmooth ρ) {K : Subgroup G} (hK : IsOpen (K : Set G))
    (h2 : ∀ W : Submodule ℂ V, (∀ g, ∀ v ∈ W, ρ g v ∈ W) → W ⊓ fixed ρ K = ⊥ → W = ⊥)
    (U : Submodule ℂ V) (hU : ∀ g, ∀ v ∈ U, ρ g v ∈ U) (h0 : U ≠ ⊥)
    (hfin : ∀ K' : Subgroup G, IsOpen (K' : Set G) → FiniteDimensional ℂ ↥(U ⊓ fixed ρ K')) :
    ∃ σ : SmIrr G, (∃ f : σ.V →ₗ[ℂ] V, f ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ g v, f (σ.ρ g v) = ρ g (f v)) ∧ IsAdmissible σ.ρ := by
  let S : Set (Submodule ℂ V) := {W | W ≤ U ∧ (∀ g, ∀ v ∈ W, ρ g v ∈ W) ∧ W ≠ ⊥}
  obtain ⟨W, ⟨hWU, hW, hW0⟩, hmin⟩ := (measure fun W : Submodule ℂ V => Module.finrank ℂ ↥(W ⊓ fixed ρ K)).wf.has_min S
    ⟨U, le_rfl, hU, h0⟩
  let X : Submodule ℂ V := InfSet.sInf {Y | (∀ g, ∀ v ∈ Y, ρ g v ∈ Y) ∧ W ⊓ fixed ρ K ≤ Y}
  have hX : ∀ g, ∀ v ∈ X, ρ g v ∈ X := fun g v hv =>
    Submodule.mem_sInf.2 fun Y hY => hY.1 g v (Submodule.mem_sInf.1 hv Y hY)
  have hXW : X ≤ W := sInf_le ⟨hW, inf_le_left⟩
  have hX0 : X ≠ ⊥ := fun e => hW0 (h2 W hW (le_bot_iff.1 (e ▸ le_sInf fun Y hY => hY.2)))
  have hsm : IsSmooth (sub ρ X hX) := fun v => by
    convert hρ v.1 using 1
    ext k
    exact Subtype.ext_iff
  have : Nontrivial X := Submodule.nontrivial_iff_ne_bot.2 hX0
  refine ⟨⟨X, sub ρ X hX, hsm, (isIrreducible_iff _).2 ⟨this, fun Y hY => ?_⟩⟩,
    ⟨X.subtype, fun e => hX0 ?_, fun _ _ => rfl⟩, hsm, fun K' hK' _ => ?_⟩
  · have hi := Submodule.map_injective_of_injective X.injective_subtype
    have hs : ∀ g, ∀ v ∈ Y.map X.subtype, ρ g v ∈ Y.map X.subtype := by
      rintro g _ ⟨v, hv, rfl⟩
      exact ⟨_, hY g v hv, rfl⟩
    refine or_iff_not_imp_left.2 fun hY0 => hi ?_
    have hle := (Submodule.map_subtype_le X Y).trans hXW
    have h3 := hmin _ ⟨hle.trans hWU, hs, fun e => hY0 (hi (e.trans (Submodule.map_bot _).symm))⟩
    have := hfin K hK
    have : FiniteDimensional ℂ ↥(W ⊓ fixed ρ K) := Submodule.finiteDimensional_of_le (inf_le_inf_right _ hWU)
    have h4 := Submodule.eq_of_le_of_finrank_le (inf_le_inf_right (fixed ρ K) hle) (not_lt.1 h3)
    rw [Submodule.map_subtype_top]
    exact le_antisymm (Submodule.map_subtype_le X Y) (sInf_le ⟨hs, h4 ▸ inf_le_left⟩)
  · rw [← X.range_subtype, e, LinearMap.range_zero]
  · have := hfin K' hK'
    refine FiniteDimensional.of_injective ((X.subtype ∘ₗ (fixed (sub ρ X hX) K').subtype).codRestrict
      (U ⊓ fixed ρ K') fun v => ⟨hWU (hXW v.1.2), mem_fix.2 fun k hk => congrArg Subtype.val (mem_fix.1 v.2 k hk)⟩)
      fun v v' e => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (by have e' := congrArg Subtype.val e; exact e'))

end Rep

theorem eq_bot_of_fixed (π : CuspSub E a) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))
    {K : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))} (hK : IsCompact (K : Set (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))))
    {φ : π.V} (h0 : φ ≠ 0) (hφ : φ ∈ fixed (π.locRep w) K) (W : Submodule ℂ π.V)
    (hW : ∀ g, ∀ v ∈ W, π.locRep w g v ∈ W) (h : W ⊓ fixed (π.locRep w) K = ⊥) : W = ⊥ := by
  have hi := Submodule.map_injective_of_injective π.V.injective_subtype
  rcases π.irred ((nullPart (π.locRep w) K).map π.V.subtype) (Submodule.map_subtype_le _ _) (by
    rintro o _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨_, nullPart_op π w K o hx, rfl⟩) with h1 | h1
  · have h2 : nullPart (π.locRep w) K = ⊥ := hi (h1.trans (Submodule.map_bot _).symm)
    exact le_bot_iff.1 (h2 ▸ le_nullPart (π.isSmooth_locRep w) hK W hW h)
  · have h2 : nullPart (π.locRep w) K = ⊤ := hi (h1.trans (Submodule.map_subtype_top _).symm)
    exact absurd (eq_zero_of_mem_nullPart (π.isSmooth_locRep w) hK hφ (h2 ▸ Submodule.mem_top)) h0

def orbSpan (φ : Sm E a) : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a) := Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range fun k => cptRep E a k φ)

theorem orbSpan_stable (φ : Sm E a) (k : Kinf E a) (x : Sm E a) (hx : x ∈ orbSpan φ) : cptRep E a k x ∈ orbSpan φ := by
  have : orbSpan φ ≤ (orbSpan φ).comap (cptRep E a k) := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
    rintro _ ⟨k', rfl⟩
    exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨k * k', by simp⟩)
  exact this hx

theorem isContRep_sub (S : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)) [FiniteDimensional ℂ S] (hS : ∀ k, ∀ x ∈ S, cptRep E a k x ∈ S) :
    AutFin.IsContRep (sub (cptRep E a) S hS) := by
  intro ℓ x
  have h : Submodule.span ℂ (Set.range fun g : GA E a =>
      ((LinearMap.proj g).comp ((Sm E a).subtype.comp S.subtype) : S →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ)) = ⊤ := by
    refine Submodule.span_eq_top_of_ne_zero fun z hz => ?_
    by_contra hc
    push Not at hc
    exact hz (Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (funext fun g => hc _ ⟨g, rfl⟩)))
  have hl : ℓ ∈ (⊤ : Submodule ℂ (S →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ)) := Submodule.mem_top
  rw [← h] at hl
  induction hl using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem y hy =>
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := hy
    have h1 : Continuous fun k : Kinf E a => chart x.1.1 g (toL (Unitary.toUnits k).val) :=
      (x.1.2 g).continuousOn.comp_continuous (f := fun k : Kinf E a => toL (Unitary.toUnits k).val)
        continuous_subtype_val fun k => val_mem_invSet _
    simp only [chart_val] at h1
    exact h1
  | zero => exact continuous_const
  | add y z _ _ hy hz => exact hy.add hz
  | smul c y _ hy => exact hy.const_smul c

def typePart (π : CuspSub E a) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (L : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (Af E)))
    {W : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] (ρ : Representation ℂ (Kinf E a) W) : Submodule ℂ π.V where
  carrier := {ψ | (∀ k ∈ L, GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w) k = 1 → π.finPart k ψ = ψ) ∧
    ∀ g : GA E a, (fun k : Kinf E a => (cptRep E a k ψ.1).1 g) ∈ AutFin.coeffSpan ρ}
  add_mem' := fun hx hy => ⟨fun k h1 h2 => by rw [map_add, hx.1 k h1 h2, hy.1 k h1 h2],
    fun g => add_mem (hx.2 g) (hy.2 g)⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨fun _ _ _ => map_zero _, fun _ => zero_mem _⟩
  smul_mem' := fun c _ hx => ⟨fun k h1 h2 => by rw [map_smul, hx.1 k h1 h2],
    fun g => Submodule.smul_mem _ c (hx.2 g)⟩

theorem typePart_act (π : CuspSub E a) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (L : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (Af E)))
    {W : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] (ρ : Representation ℂ (Kinf E a) W) (g : GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))
    (x : π.V) (hx : x ∈ typePart π w L ρ) : π.locRep w g x ∈ typePart π w L ρ := by
  refine ⟨fun k h1 h2 => ?_, fun y => ?_⟩
  · rw [CuspSub.locRep, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← Module.End.mul_apply, ← map_mul, LocUniq.comm_locGL w h2, map_mul,
      Module.End.mul_apply, hx.1 k h1 h2]
  · convert hx.2 (y * finGL E a (locGL E a w g)) using 2 with k
    change x.1.1 (y * infGL E a _ * finGL E a _) = x.1.1 (y * finGL E a _ * infGL E a _)
    rw [mul_assoc, mul_assoc, infGL_comm]

theorem finiteDimensional_typePart (h : AutomorphicFormsFiniteGL) (π : CuspSub E a) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))
    {L : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (Af E))} (hL : IsOpen (L : Set (GL (Fin a) (Af E))))
    {W : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] [FiniteDimensional ℂ W] {ρ : Representation ℂ (Kinf E a) W}
    (hρ : AutFin.IsContRep ρ) (K : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)))
    (hK : IsOpen (K : Set (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)))) :
    FiniteDimensional ℂ ↥(typePart π w L ρ ⊓ fixed (π.locRep w) K) := by
  have hc : ∀ τ u, ∃ c : ℂ, ∀ φ ∈ π.V, cap τ u φ = c • φ := fun τ u =>
    π.exists_scalar (z := cap τ u) (Algebra.subset_adjoin ⟨τ, u, rfl⟩) fun o => by
      cases o with
      | fin k => exact commute_cap_finRep τ u k
      | cpt k => exact GlobalTheory.CapAdInv.cap_commute_rt_infGL E a τ u _
      | lie X => exact commute_cap_lie τ u X
  choose c hc using hc
  have := h E a (L ⊓ (K ⊓ L.comap (locGL E a w)).comap (GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w)))
    (hL.inter ((hK.inter (hL.preimage (CuspSub.continuous_locGL w))).preimage (continuous_map_pLoc w))) W ρ hρ c
  refine FiniteDimensional.of_injective ((π.V.subtype ∘ₗ (typePart π w L ρ ⊓ fixed (π.locRep w) K).subtype).codRestrict
    (Submodule.span ℂ (AutFinGL.forms
      (L ⊓ (K ⊓ L.comap (locGL E a w)).comap (GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w))) ρ c))
    fun v => Submodule.subset_span ⟨(π.cusp _ v.1.2).1, fun k hk => ?_, v.2.1.2, fun τ u => hc τ u _ v.1.2⟩)
    fun v v' e => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (by have e' := congrArg Subtype.val e; exact e'))
  have h1 := v.2.1.1 _ (L.mul_mem (L.inv_mem hk.2.2) hk.1) (map_pLoc_inv_mul w k)
  have h2 := mem_fix.1 v.2.2 _ hk.2.1
  have h3 : π.finPart k v.1 = v.1 := by
    rw [← mul_inv_cancel_left (locGL E a w (GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w) k)) k, map_mul, Module.End.mul_apply, h1]
    exact h2
  exact congrArg Subtype.val h3

theorem exists_isLocComp (h : AutomorphicFormsFiniteGL) : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E]
    (a : ℕ) (π : CuspSub E a) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)),
    ∃ σ : SmIrr (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)), π.IsLocComp w σ ∧ IsAdmissible σ.ρ := by
  intro E _ _ _ a π w
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 π.ne_bot
  have h0' : (⟨φ, hφ⟩ : π.V) ≠ 0 := fun e => h0 (congrArg Subtype.val e)
  have hL : IsOpen (stab π.finPart ⟨φ, hφ⟩ : Set (GL (Fin a) (Af E))) := π.isSmooth_finPart _
  obtain ⟨K, ho, hc, hK⟩ := exists_compact_open w _ hL
  have : FiniteDimensional ℂ (orbSpan φ) := (π.cusp φ hφ).1.kfin
  refine exists_irred (π.isSmooth_locRep w) ho (eq_bot_of_fixed π w hc h0' (mem_fix.2 hK))
    (typePart π w (stab π.finPart ⟨φ, hφ⟩) (sub (cptRep E a) (orbSpan φ) (orbSpan_stable φ))) (typePart_act π w _ _)
    ((Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨⟨φ, hφ⟩, ⟨fun k hk _ => hk, ?_⟩, h0'⟩)
    (finiteDimensional_typePart h π w hL (isContRep_sub _ _))
  exact AutFinGL.type_of_map (sub (cptRep E a) (orbSpan φ) (orbSpan_stable φ)) (orbSpan φ).subtype (fun _ _ => rfl)
    ⟨φ, Submodule.subset_span ⟨1, by simp⟩⟩

end GlobalTheory.CuspRep.LocEx

open GlobalTheory.CuspRep.LocEx

namespace GlobalTheory.DiscLocEx

variable {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] {a : ℕ}

def opD {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsStable V) (o : Op E a) : Module.End ℂ V := (act o).restrict (hV o)

def finPartD {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsStable V) : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin a) (Af E)) V :=
  GlobalTheory.PRep.sub (finRep E a) V fun k => hV (.fin k)

def locRepD {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsStable V) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) :
    Representation ℂ (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)) V :=
  (finPartD hV).comp (locGL E a w)

theorem isSmooth_finPartD {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsStable V) (ha : ∀ φ ∈ V, IsAutForm φ) :
    IsSmooth (finPartD hV) := fun φ => by
  convert (ha _ φ.2).level using 1
  ext k
  exact Subtype.ext_iff

theorem isSmooth_locRepD {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsStable V) (ha : ∀ φ ∈ V, IsAutForm φ)
    (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) : IsSmooth (locRepD hV w) := fun φ =>
  (isSmooth_finPartD hV ha φ).preimage (CuspSub.continuous_locGL w)

theorem exists_scalarD {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsDiscrete V) {z : Module.End ℂ (Sm E a)} (hz : z ∈ Zalg E a)
    (hc : ∀ o : Op E a, Commute z (act o)) : ∃ c : ℂ, ∀ φ ∈ V, z φ = c • φ := by
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 hV.ne_bot
  set U := Submodule.span ℂ ((fun z : Module.End ℂ (Sm E a) => z φ) '' Zalg E a) with hU
  have hfin : FiniteDimensional ℂ U := (hV.aut φ hφ).zfin
  have hle : U ≤ V := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
    rintro _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
    exact Zalg_stable (fun X => hV.stable (.lie X)) hy φ hφ)
  have hst : ∀ x ∈ U, z x ∈ U := fun x hx => by
    have : U ≤ U.comap z := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
      rintro _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨z * y, mul_mem hz hy, rfl⟩)
    exact this hx
  have hφU : φ ∈ U := Submodule.subset_span ⟨1, one_mem _, rfl⟩
  have : Nontrivial U := ⟨⟨φ, hφU⟩, 0, fun h => h0 (congrArg Subtype.val h)⟩
  obtain ⟨c, hc'⟩ := Module.End.exists_eigenvalue (z.restrict hst)
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hc'.exists_hasEigenvector
  have hv1 : z v.1 = c • v.1 := congrArg Subtype.val hv.apply_eq_smul
  refine ⟨c, fun ψ hψ => ?_⟩
  rcases hV.irred (V ⊓ z.eigenspace c) inf_le_left (fun o x hx => ⟨hV.stable o x hx.1, by
    have h1 : z x = c • x := Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.1 hx.2
    refine Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.2 ?_
    rw [← map_smul, ← h1]
    exact LinearMap.congr_fun (hc o).eq x⟩) with h | h
  · have : v.1 ∈ V ⊓ z.eigenspace c := ⟨hle v.2, Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.2 hv1⟩
    rw [h] at this
    exact absurd (Subtype.ext ((Submodule.mem_bot ℂ).1 this)) hv.2
  · rw [← h] at hψ
    exact Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.1 hψ.2

theorem nullPart_opD {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsStable V) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))
    (K : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)))
    (o : Op E a) {x : V} (hx : x ∈ nullPart (locRepD hV w) K) : opD hV o x ∈ nullPart (locRepD hV w) K := by
  cases o with
  | fin k =>
    have h1 := nullPart_act _ K (GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w) k) (nullPart_comm _ K
      (finPartD hV ((locGL E a w (GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w) k))⁻¹ * k)) (fun g' y => by
      simp only [locRepD, MonoidHom.comp_apply]
      rw [← Module.End.mul_apply, ← Module.End.mul_apply, ← map_mul, ← map_mul,
        LocUniq.comm_locGL w (map_pLoc_inv_mul w k)]) hx)
    rwa [locRepD, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← Module.End.mul_apply, ← map_mul, mul_inv_cancel_left] at h1
  | cpt k =>
    refine nullPart_comm _ K _ (fun g y => Subtype.ext ?_) hx
    change (rt E a (infGL E a (Unitary.toUnits k)) * rt E a (finGL E a (locGL E a w g))) y.1 =
      (rt E a (finGL E a (locGL E a w g)) * rt E a (infGL E a (Unitary.toUnits k))) y.1
    rw [← map_mul, ← map_mul, infGL_comm]
  | lie X =>
    refine nullPart_comm _ K _ (fun g y => Subtype.ext ?_) hx
    exact LinearMap.congr_fun (lie_rt_fin X (locGL E a w g)) y.1

theorem eq_bot_of_fixedD {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsDiscrete V) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))
    {K : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))} (hK : IsCompact (K : Set (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))))
    {φ : V} (h0 : φ ≠ 0) (hφ : φ ∈ fixed (locRepD hV.stable w) K) (W : Submodule ℂ V)
    (hW : ∀ g, ∀ v ∈ W, locRepD hV.stable w g v ∈ W) (h : W ⊓ fixed (locRepD hV.stable w) K = ⊥) : W = ⊥ := by
  have hi := Submodule.map_injective_of_injective V.injective_subtype
  rcases hV.irred ((nullPart (locRepD hV.stable w) K).map V.subtype) (Submodule.map_subtype_le _ _) (by
    rintro o _ ⟨x, hx, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨_, nullPart_opD hV.stable w K o hx, rfl⟩) with h1 | h1
  · have h2 : nullPart (locRepD hV.stable w) K = ⊥ := hi (h1.trans (Submodule.map_bot _).symm)
    exact le_bot_iff.1 (h2 ▸ le_nullPart (isSmooth_locRepD hV.stable hV.aut w) hK W hW h)
  · have h2 : nullPart (locRepD hV.stable w) K = ⊤ := hi (h1.trans (Submodule.map_subtype_top _).symm)
    exact absurd (eq_zero_of_mem_nullPart (isSmooth_locRepD hV.stable hV.aut w) hK hφ (h2 ▸ Submodule.mem_top)) h0

def typePartD {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsStable V) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))
    (L : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (Af E)))
    {W : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] (ρ : Representation ℂ (Kinf E a) W) : Submodule ℂ V where
  carrier := {ψ | (∀ k ∈ L, GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w) k = 1 → finPartD hV k ψ = ψ) ∧
    ∀ g : GA E a, (fun k : Kinf E a => (cptRep E a k ψ.1).1 g) ∈ AutFin.coeffSpan ρ}
  add_mem' := fun hx hy => ⟨fun k h1 h2 => by rw [map_add, hx.1 k h1 h2, hy.1 k h1 h2],
    fun g => add_mem (hx.2 g) (hy.2 g)⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨fun _ _ _ => map_zero _, fun _ => zero_mem _⟩
  smul_mem' := fun c _ hx => ⟨fun k h1 h2 => by rw [map_smul, hx.1 k h1 h2],
    fun g => Submodule.smul_mem _ c (hx.2 g)⟩

theorem typePartD_act {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsStable V) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))
    (L : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (Af E)))
    {W : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] (ρ : Representation ℂ (Kinf E a) W) (g : GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))
    (x : V) (hx : x ∈ typePartD hV w L ρ) : locRepD hV w g x ∈ typePartD hV w L ρ := by
  refine ⟨fun k h1 h2 => ?_, fun y => ?_⟩
  · rw [locRepD, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← Module.End.mul_apply, ← map_mul, LocUniq.comm_locGL w h2, map_mul,
      Module.End.mul_apply, hx.1 k h1 h2]
  · convert hx.2 (y * finGL E a (locGL E a w g)) using 2 with k
    change x.1.1 (y * infGL E a _ * finGL E a _) = x.1.1 (y * finGL E a _ * infGL E a _)
    rw [mul_assoc, mul_assoc, infGL_comm]

theorem finiteDimensional_typePartD (h : AutomorphicFormsFiniteGL) {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsDiscrete V)
    (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E))
    {L : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (Af E))} (hL : IsOpen (L : Set (GL (Fin a) (Af E))))
    {W : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] [FiniteDimensional ℂ W] {ρ : Representation ℂ (Kinf E a) W}
    (hρ : AutFin.IsContRep ρ) (K : Subgroup (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)))
    (hK : IsOpen (K : Set (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)))) :
    FiniteDimensional ℂ ↥(typePartD hV.stable w L ρ ⊓ fixed (locRepD hV.stable w) K) := by
  have hc : ∀ τ u, ∃ c : ℂ, ∀ φ ∈ V, cap τ u φ = c • φ := fun τ u =>
    exists_scalarD hV (z := cap τ u) (Algebra.subset_adjoin ⟨τ, u, rfl⟩) fun o => by
      cases o with
      | fin k => exact commute_cap_finRep τ u k
      | cpt k => exact GlobalTheory.CapAdInv.cap_commute_rt_infGL E a τ u _
      | lie X => exact commute_cap_lie τ u X
  choose c hc using hc
  have := h E a (L ⊓ (K ⊓ L.comap (locGL E a w)).comap (GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w)))
    (hL.inter ((hK.inter (hL.preimage (CuspSub.continuous_locGL w))).preimage (continuous_map_pLoc w))) W ρ hρ c
  refine FiniteDimensional.of_injective ((V.subtype ∘ₗ (typePartD hV.stable w L ρ ⊓
    fixed (locRepD hV.stable w) K).subtype).codRestrict
    (Submodule.span ℂ (AutFinGL.forms
      (L ⊓ (K ⊓ L.comap (locGL E a w)).comap (GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w))) ρ c))
    fun v => Submodule.subset_span ⟨hV.aut _ v.1.2, fun k hk => ?_, v.2.1.2, fun τ u => hc τ u _ v.1.2⟩)
    fun v v' e => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (by have e' := congrArg Subtype.val e; exact e'))
  have h1 := v.2.1.1 _ (L.mul_mem (L.inv_mem hk.2.2) hk.1) (map_pLoc_inv_mul w k)
  have h2 := mem_fix.1 v.2.2 _ hk.2.1
  have h3 : finPartD hV.stable k v.1 = v.1 := by
    rw [← mul_inv_cancel_left (locGL E a w (GeneralLinearGroup.map (pLoc E w) k)) k, map_mul, Module.End.mul_apply, h1]
    exact h2
  exact congrArg Subtype.val h3

theorem exists_irred_sub (h : AutomorphicFormsFiniteGL) {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E a)} (hV : IsDiscrete V)
    (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) :
    ∃ σ : SmIrr (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)), (∃ f : σ.V →ₗ[ℂ] V, f ≠ 0 ∧
      ∀ g v, f (σ.ρ g v) = locRepD hV.stable w g (f v)) ∧ IsAdmissible σ.ρ := by
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 hV.ne_bot
  have h0' : (⟨φ, hφ⟩ : V) ≠ 0 := fun e => h0 (congrArg Subtype.val e)
  have hL : IsOpen (stab (finPartD hV.stable) ⟨φ, hφ⟩ : Set (GL (Fin a) (Af E))) :=
    isSmooth_finPartD hV.stable hV.aut _
  obtain ⟨K, ho, hc, hK⟩ := exists_compact_open w _ hL
  have : FiniteDimensional ℂ (orbSpan φ) := (hV.aut φ hφ).kfin
  refine exists_irred (isSmooth_locRepD hV.stable hV.aut w) ho (eq_bot_of_fixedD hV w hc h0' (mem_fix.2 hK))
    (typePartD hV.stable w (stab (finPartD hV.stable) ⟨φ, hφ⟩) (sub (cptRep E a) (orbSpan φ) (orbSpan_stable φ)))
    (typePartD_act hV.stable w _ _)
    ((Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨⟨φ, hφ⟩, ⟨fun k hk _ => hk, ?_⟩, h0'⟩)
    (finiteDimensional_typePartD h hV w hL (isContRep_sub _ _))
  exact AutFinGL.type_of_map (sub (cptRep E a) (orbSpan φ) (orbSpan_stable φ)) (orbSpan φ).subtype (fun _ _ => rfl)
    ⟨φ, Submodule.subset_span ⟨1, by simp⟩⟩

end GlobalTheory.DiscLocEx

open GlobalTheory.DiscSpec GlobalTheory.SmGL in

theorem GlobalTheory.DiscLocEx.exists_isLocCompGL : AutomorphicFormsFiniteGL → ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E]
    [IsTotallyComplex E] (N : ℕ), ∀ (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)), IsDiscrete V →
    ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), ∃ σ' , IsLocCompGL V w σ' := by
  intro h E _ _ _ N V hV w
  obtain ⟨σ, ⟨f, hf0, hf⟩, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.DiscLocEx.exists_irred_sub h hV w
  refine ⟨σ, V.subtype ∘ₗ f, fun e => hf0 (LinearMap.ext fun v => Subtype.ext ?_), fun v => (f v).2,
    fun g v => congrArg Subtype.val (hf g v)⟩
  exact LinearMap.congr_fun e v

    GlobalTheory.CapelliTop.subdet_empty]
  simp only [R, eval_add, eval_finset_sum, eval_mul, eval_C, hQe, LinearMap.add_apply, LinearMap.sum_apply,
    LinearMap.smul_apply, map_add, map_sum, map_smul, Module.End.one_apply, hf, smul_eq_mul, mul_one]
  congr 1
  exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun S _ => mul_comm _ _

variable {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] {a : ℕ}

theorem CuspSub.exists_scalar (π : CuspSub E a) {z : Module.End ℂ (Sm E a)} (hz : z ∈ Zalg E a)
    (hc : ∀ o : Op E a, Commute z (act o)) : ∃ c : ℂ, ∀ φ ∈ π.V, z φ = c • φ := by
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 π.ne_bot
  set U := Submodule.span ℂ ((fun z : Module.End ℂ (Sm E a) => z φ) '' Zalg E a) with hU
  have hfin : FiniteDimensional ℂ U := (π.cusp φ hφ).1.zfin
  have hle : U ≤ π.V := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
    rintro _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
    exact Zalg_stable (fun X => π.stable (.lie X)) hy φ hφ)
  have hst : ∀ x ∈ U, z x ∈ U := fun x hx => by
    have : U ≤ U.comap z := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
      rintro _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨z * y, mul_mem hz hy, rfl⟩)
    exact this hx
  have hφU : φ ∈ U := Submodule.subset_span ⟨1, one_mem _, rfl⟩
  have : Nontrivial U := ⟨⟨φ, hφU⟩, 0, fun h => h0 (congrArg Subtype.val h)⟩
  obtain ⟨c, hc'⟩ := Module.End.exists_eigenvalue (z.restrict hst)
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := hc'.exists_hasEigenvector
  have hv1 : z v.1 = c • v.1 := congrArg Subtype.val hv.apply_eq_smul
  refine ⟨c, fun ψ hψ => ?_⟩
  rcases π.irred (π.V ⊓ z.eigenspace c) inf_le_left (fun o x hx => ⟨π.stable o x hx.1, by
    have h1 : z x = c • x := Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.1 hx.2
    refine Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.2 ?_
    rw [← map_smul, ← h1]
    exact LinearMap.congr_fun (hc o).eq x⟩) with h | h
  · have : v.1 ∈ π.V ⊓ z.eigenspace c := ⟨hle v.2, Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.2 hv1⟩
    rw [h] at this
    exact absurd (Subtype.ext ((Submodule.mem_bot ℂ).1 this)) hv.2
  · rw [← h] at hψ
    exact Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.1 hψ.2

theorem hasInfChar_infChar_of_commute : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (a : ℕ), (∀ (τ : E →+* ℂ) (u : ℂ) (o : Op E a), Commute (cap (a := a) τ u) (act o)) → ∀ (π : CG E a) (τ : E →+* ℂ), π.HasInfChar τ (π.infChar τ) := by
  intro E _ _ _ a hc π τ
  suffices h : ∃ m, π.HasInfChar τ m by
    obtain ⟨m, h⟩ := h
    rwa [h.infChar_eq]
  induction π using Quotient.inductionOn with
  | h π =>
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 π.ne_bot
  obtain ⟨f, hf⟩ := Module.Projective.exists_dual_eq_one ℂ h0
  obtain ⟨m, hm, h⟩ := exists_multiset_qdet (Eop (a := a) τ) (((a : ℂ) - 1) / 2) φ f hf
  refine ⟨m, hm, fun u ψ hψ => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨c, h1⟩ := π.exists_scalar (z := cap τ u) (Algebra.subset_adjoin ⟨τ, u, rfl⟩) (hc τ u)
  have h2 : f (cap τ u φ) = _ := h u
  rw [h1 φ hφ, map_smul, hf, smul_eq_mul, mul_one] at h2
  rw [h1 ψ hψ, h2]

end GlobalTheory.CuspRep

    prodMap S φ = fun m => ∏ i, (φ i).1 (m i) := rfl

theorem span_le_iSup {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] {G Γ X A : ι → Type} [∀ i, Group (G i)] [∀ i, Group (Γ i)]
    [∀ i, AddCommGroup (X i)] [∀ i, Module ℂ (X i)] (S : ∀ i, Submodule ℂ (G i → ℂ)) (V : ∀ i, Submodule ℂ (S i))
    (j : ∀ i, Γ i → G i) (ρ : ∀ i, Representation ℂ (Γ i) (X i)) (f : ∀ i, A i → X i →ₗ[ℂ] S i)
    (hf : ∀ i a v, f i a v ∈ V i) (he : ∀ i a g v y, (f i a (ρ i g v)).1 y = (f i a v).1 (y * j i g))
    (hV : ∀ i, V i ≤ ⨆ a, LinearMap.range (f i a)) (T : Submodule ℂ ((∀ i, G i) → ℂ))
    (hT : T = Submodule.span ℂ {F | ∃ φ : ∀ i, V i, F = fun m => ∏ i, (φ i).1.1 (m i)}) :
    T ≤ ⨆ Φ : {Φ : (PiTensorProduct ℂ X) →ₗ[ℂ] ((∀ i, G i) → ℂ) // (∀ v, Φ v ∈ T) ∧
      ∀ g v, Φ (piTensor ρ g v) = fun m => Φ v fun i => m i * j i (g i)}, LinearMap.range Φ.1 := by
  refine hT.le.trans (Submodule.span_le.2 ?_)
  rintro _ ⟨φ, rfl⟩
  refine multilinear_mem_of_span (prodMap S) (fun i => {y | ∃ a v, f i a v = y}) _ (fun x hx => ?_)
    (fun i => (φ i).1) fun i => ?_
  · choose a v hx using hx
    let Φ : (PiTensorProduct ℂ X) →ₗ[ℂ] ((∀ i, G i) → ℂ) :=
      PiTensorProduct.lift ((prodMap S).compLinearMap fun i => f i (a i))
    have hΦ : ∀ u : ∀ i, X i, Φ (PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ u) = fun m => ∏ i, (f i (a i) (u i)).1 (m i) := fun u => by
      simp only [Φ, PiTensorProduct.lift.tprod]
      rfl
    refine Submodule.mem_iSup_of_mem ⟨Φ, fun t => ?_, fun g t => ?_⟩ ⟨PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ v, ?_⟩
    · induction t using PiTensorProduct.induction_on with
      | smul_tprod r u =>
        rw [map_smul, hΦ, hT]
        exact Submodule.smul_mem _ _ (Submodule.subset_span ⟨fun i => ⟨f i (a i) (u i), hf i _ _⟩, rfl⟩)
      | add x y h1 h2 => rw [map_add]; exact add_mem h1 h2
    · induction t using PiTensorProduct.induction_on with
      | smul_tprod r u =>
        rw [map_smul, map_smul, map_smul, piTensor_tprod, hΦ, hΦ]
        funext m
        simp only [Pi.smul_apply, he]
      | add x y h1 h2 => rw [map_add, map_add, map_add, h1, h2]; rfl
    · change Φ _ = _
      rw [hΦ, prodMap_apply]
      simp only [hx]
  · refine (hV i).trans (iSup_le fun a => ?_) (φ i).2
    rintro _ ⟨v, rfl⟩
    exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨a, v, rfl⟩

theorem hl_of_good {m : ℕ} {f : GA E a → ℂ} (hf : Good ψ m f) {k : ℕ} (hk : k ≤ m) (c : UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a k k → E) (g : GA E a) :
    f (ratGL E a (blkU c) * g) = f g := by
  refine hf.left _ (fun i j h => isMir_blkU c i j ?_) g
  omega

theorem colT_add (hψ : StdPsi ψ) {m : ℕ} {f f' : GA E a → ℂ} (hf : Good ψ (m + 1) f) (hf' : Good ψ (m + 1) f') :
    colT ψ (m + 1) (fun g => f g + f' g) = fun g => colT ψ (m + 1) f g + colT ψ (m + 1) f' g := by
  funext g
  have := GlobalTheory.T.quotientAverage_add_of_periodic _ (ratV E (UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a (m + 1) (m + 1))) (isCoLat_ratV E _) _ _
    (per_coef hψ (eLast a (m + 1)) hf.cont (hl_of_good hf le_rfl) g)
    (per_coef hψ (eLast a (m + 1)) hf'.cont (hl_of_good hf' le_rfl) g)
  simp only [colT, coefF, mul_add]
  exact this

theorem colT_smul (m : ℕ) (c : ℂ) (f : GA E a → ℂ) :
    colT ψ (m + 1) (fun g => c * f g) = fun g => c * colT ψ (m + 1) f g := by
  funext g
  simp only [colT, coefF]
  rw [← avg_const_mul]
  congr 1
  funext x
  ring

theorem whit_good (hψ : StdPsi ψ) (m : ℕ) : (m < a ∨ m = 0) → ∀ {f : GA E a → ℂ}, Good ψ m f →
    Good ψ 0 (whit ψ m f) ∧ (f ≠ 0 → whit ψ m f ≠ 0) := by
  induction m with
  | zero => exact fun _ f hf => ⟨hf, id⟩
  | succ m ih =>
    intro h f hf
    have h1 : m + 1 < a := by omega
    obtain ⟨h2, h3⟩ := ih (Or.inl (by omega)) (good_colT hψ h1 hf)
    exact ⟨h2, fun h0 => h3 (GlobalTheory.T.unipotentColumnWhittakerTransform_ne_zero E a ψ hψ m f h1 hf h0)⟩

theorem whit_add (hψ : StdPsi ψ) (m : ℕ) : (m < a ∨ m = 0) → ∀ {f f' : GA E a → ℂ}, Good ψ m f → Good ψ m f' →
    whit ψ m (fun g => f g + f' g) = fun g => whit ψ m f g + whit ψ m f' g := by
  induction m with
  | zero => exact fun _ f f' _ _ => rfl
  | succ m ih =>
    intro h f f' hf hf'
    have h1 : m + 1 < a := by omega
    rw [whit_succ, whit_succ, whit_succ, colT_add hψ hf hf']
    exact ih (Or.inl (by omega)) (good_colT hψ h1 hf) (good_colT hψ h1 hf')

theorem whit_smul (m : ℕ) (c : ℂ) (f : GA E a → ℂ) : whit ψ m (fun g => c * f g) = fun g => c * whit ψ m f g := by
  induction m generalizing f with
  | zero => rfl
  | succ m ih => rw [whit_succ, whit_succ, colT_smul, ih]

theorem whit_rt (m : ℕ) (f : GA E a → ℂ) (h g : GA E a) : whit ψ m (fun x => f (x * h)) g = whit ψ m f (g * h) := by
  induction m generalizing f g with
  | zero => rfl
  | succ m ih =>
    rw [whit_succ, whit_succ, ← ih]
    congr 1
    funext x
    simp only [colT, coefF, mul_assoc]

def locPsi (hψ : StdPsi ψ) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) : AddChar (w.adicCompletion E) Circle where
  toFun y := ⟨ψ (sFin E (sLoc E w y)), by simpa [Submonoid.unitSphere] using hψ.norm _⟩
  map_zero_eq_one' := by
    apply Circle.ext
    simp
  map_add_eq_mul' x y := by
    apply Circle.ext
    change ψ (sFin E (sLoc E w (x + y))) = ψ (sFin E (sLoc E w x)) * ψ (sFin E (sLoc E w y))
    rw [map_add, map_add, AddChar.map_add_eq_mul]

theorem coe_locPsi (hψ : StdPsi ψ) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (y : w.adicCompletion E) :
    (locPsi hψ w y : ℂ) = ψ (sFin E (sLoc E w y)) := rfl

theorem isGeneric_of_isLocComp : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (a : ℕ) (P : CuspSub E a)
    (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (σ : SmIrr (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E))), P.IsLocComp w σ →
    GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsGeneric σ.ρ := by

  have hg : ∀ v, Good ψ (a - 1) (f v).1.1 := fun v => GlobalTheory.T.isMirabolicWhittakerStage_of_isCuspForm E a ψ (f v).1 (P.cusp _ (f v).2)
  have h0 : a - 1 < a ∨ a - 1 = 0 := by omega

    by_contra h
    push Not at h
    exact hf0 (LinearMap.ext h)

      whit ψ (a - 1) (f v).1.1 (x * finGL E a (locGL E a w u)) := fun u v x => by
    rw [hf u v]
    exact whit_rt _ _ _ _
  let l : σ.V →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ :=
    { toFun := fun v => whit ψ (a - 1) (f v).1.1 h
      map_add' := fun v v' => by
        rw [map_add f]
        exact congrFun (whit_add hψ (a - 1) h0 (hg v) (hg v')) h
      map_smul' := fun c v => by
        rw [map_smul f]
        exact congrFun (whit_smul (a - 1) c (f v).1.1) h }
  have hl : ∀ v, l v = whit ψ (a - 1) (f v).1.1 h := fun _ => rfl
  have hne : l (σ.ρ gw v₀) ≠ 0 := by
    rw [hl, hrt, ← e]
    exact hg₀
  have hlaw : ∀ (u : GlobalTheory.SmGL.unipB (w.adicCompletion E) (Fin a)) v,
      l (σ.ρ u v) = (locPsi hψ w (∑ p : Fin a × Fin a with (p.2 : ℕ) = p.1 + 1, (u : GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)).val p.1 p.2) : ℂ) * l v := by
    intro u v
    obtain ⟨u1, u2⟩ := (GlobalTheory.SmGL.mem_unipB _).1 u.2
    obtain ⟨t1, t2, t3⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.localUpperUnipotent_adelic_upperUnipotent E a w u u1 u2
    have hlw := (whit_good hψ (a - 1) h0 (hg v)).1.law
    have := GlobalTheory.T.whittakerLaw_upperTriangularUnipotent_adelic E a ψ _ (fun k hk => hlw k hk) _ t1 t2 h
    rw [hl, hrt, hc, this, t3]
    rfl
  refine ⟨locPsi hψ w, ?_, fun h1 => ?_, ?_⟩
  · refine Continuous.subtype_mk (hψ.cont.comp ?_) _
    exact (continuous_const.prodMk continuous_id : Continuous fun x : Af E => ((0, x) : InfiniteAdeleRing E × Af E)).comp
      (CuspSub.continuous_sLoc w)
  · obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := hψ.loc w
    apply hy
    have := congrArg (fun c : Circle => (c : ℂ)) (DFunLike.congr_fun h1 y)
    simpa [coe_locPsi] using this
  · refine Submodule.Quotient.nontrivial_iff.2 fun htop => hne ?_
    have hle : GlobalTheory.PRep.twKer σ.ρ (GlobalTheory.SmGL.unipB (w.adicCompletion E) (Fin a)) (GlobalTheory.LocGL.whitChar (locPsi hψ w) a) ≤
        LinearMap.ker l := by

      rintro _ ⟨u, v, rfl⟩
      simp only [SetLike.mem_coe, LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, map_smul, hlaw, smul_eq_mul]

theorem colT_ne_zero_of_good : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] (a : ℕ) (ψ : AddChar (Ad E) ℂ), StdPsi ψ →
    ∀ (m : ℕ) (f : GA E a → ℂ), m + 1 < a → Good ψ (m + 1) f → f ≠ 0 → colT ψ (m + 1) f ≠ 0 := by
  intro E _ _ a ψ hψ m f hma hG hf
  obtain ⟨g₀, hg₀⟩ : ∃ g₀, f g₀ ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra h
    push_neg at h
    exact hf (funext h)
  have hPer : Per (ratV E (UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a (m + 1) (m + 1))) (fun x => f (blkU x * g₀)) :=
    per_blk hG.cont (fun c g => hG.left _ (isMir_blkU c) g) g₀
  have hΦ0 : (fun x : UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a (m + 1) (m + 1) → Ad E => f (blkU x * g₀)) ≠ 0 := by
    intro h
    have h1 := congrFun h 0
    simp only [Pi.zero_apply] at h1
    apply hg₀
    have e : (blkU (0 : UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a (m + 1) (m + 1) → Ad E) : GA E a) = 1 := posU_zero _
    rw [e, one_mul] at h1
    exact h1
  obtain ⟨ξ, hξ⟩ : ∃ ξ : UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a (m + 1) (m + 1) → E, coefF ψ (m + 1) ξ f g₀ ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra h
    push_neg at h
    exact hΦ0 (GlobalTheory.T.periodic_eq_zero_of_fourier_coefficients_zero E _ ψ hψ _ hPer h)
  have hξ0 : ξ ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    apply hξ
    have hv := hG.van (m + 1) (Nat.succ_pos m) le_rfl g₀
    have e : ∀ x : UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a (m + 1) (m + 1) → Ad E, ψ (-dot (0 : UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a (m + 1) (m + 1) → E) x) = 1 := by
      intro x
      simp [dot]
    simp only [coefF, e, one_mul]
    exact hv
  obtain ⟨γ, hγ, hγξ⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_rationalMirabolic_moving_row_to_last E a m hma ξ hξ0
  intro h
  apply hξ
  have h1 := congrFun h (ratGL E a γ * g₀)
  simp only [Pi.zero_apply, colT] at h1
  rw [GlobalTheory.T.whittakerCoefficient_rationalMirabolic_mul E a ψ hψ m f hG.cont hG.left γ hγ (eLast a (m + 1)) g₀] at h1
  have e : (fun t : UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a (m + 1) (m + 1) => ∑ s : UnipotentBlockColumnIndex a (m + 1) (m + 1), eLast a (m + 1) s * γ.val s.1.1 t.1.1) = ξ :=
    funext hγξ
  rw [e] at h1
  exact h1

variable (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]

def IrreducibleAdmissible : Prop :=
  ∀ (n : Type) [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (V : Type) [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V]
    (π : Representation ℂ (GL n k) V), IsSmooth π → π.IsIrreducible → IsAdmissible π

def JacquetFiniteLength : Prop :=
  ∀ (α n : Type) (m : α → Type) [Fintype α] [LinearOrder α] [∀ a, Fintype (m a)]
    [∀ a, DecidableEq (m a)] [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (e : (Σ a, m a) ≃ n)
    (V : Type) [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] (π : Representation ℂ (GL n k) V),
    IsSmooth π → HasFiniteLength π → HasFiniteLength (jac e π)

def InductionFiniteLength : Prop :=
  ∀ (α n : Type) (m : α → Type) [Fintype α] [LinearOrder α] [∀ a, Fintype (m a)]
    [∀ a, DecidableEq (m a)] [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (e : (Σ a, m a) ≃ n)
    (W : Type) [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] (ρ : Representation ℂ (Levi k m) W),
    IsSmooth ρ → HasFiniteLength ρ → HasFiniteLength (parInd e ρ)

def InductionContragredient : Prop :=
  ∀ (α n : Type) (m : α → Type) [Fintype α] [LinearOrder α] [∀ a, Fintype (m a)]
    [∀ a, DecidableEq (m a)] [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] (e : (Σ a, m a) ≃ n)
    [MeasurableSpace (GL n k)] [BorelSpace (GL n k)] (μ : Measure (GL n k)) [μ.IsHaarMeasure]
    (W : Type) [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] (ρ : Representation ℂ (Levi k m) W),
    IsSmooth ρ → ∃ Φ : (parInd e (contra ρ)).Equiv (contra (parInd e ρ)),
      ∀ (f' : IndSp e (contra ρ)) (f : IndSp e ρ),
        (Φ f' : Module.Dual ℂ (IndSp e ρ)) f =
          ∫ x in (KO k n : Set (GL n k)), (f'.1.1 x : Module.Dual ℂ W) (f.1.1 x) ∂μ

end SmGL

namespace Published

def BernsteinZelevinskyFiniteness : Prop :=
  ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k],
    IrreducibleAdmissible k ∧ JacquetFiniteLength k ∧ InductionFiniteLength k ∧
      InductionContragredient k

theorem basis (N : ℕ) : ∀ U ∈ nhds (1 : GL (Fin N) k), ∃ K : Subgroup (GL (Fin N) k),
    IsOpen (K : Set (GL (Fin N) k)) ∧ IsCompact (K : Set (GL (Fin N) k)) ∧ (K : Set (GL (Fin N) k)) ⊆ U :=
  fun U hU => by
    obtain ⟨J, h1, h2, h3, -⟩ := exists_open_normalised_subgroup_subset k (Fin N) U hU
    exact ⟨J, h1, GlobalTheory.SplitFlip.isCompact_KO.of_isClosed_subset (J.isClosed_of_isOpen h1) h3, h2⟩

theorem contraMap_surjective {N : ℕ} {X Y : Type} [AddCommGroup X] [Module ℂ X] [AddCommGroup Y] [Module ℂ Y]
    {σ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) X} {τ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) Y} (hσ : IsSmooth σ)
    (hτ : IsSmooth τ) (f : σ.IntertwiningMap τ) (hf : Function.Injective f) :
    Function.Surjective (GlobalTheory.ContraRefl.contraMap f) := by
  intro l
  obtain ⟨K, ho, hc, hKs⟩ := basis N _ ((isSmooth_contra σ l).mem_nhds (by simp))
  have hlK : l ∈ fixed (contra σ) K := GlobalTheory.ContraRefl.mem_fix.2 fun h hh => hKs hh
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := LinearMap.exists_leftInverse_of_injective f.toLinearMap (LinearMap.ker_eq_bot.2 hf)
  obtain ⟨l', h1, h2⟩ := GlobalTheory.ContraRefl.exists_fixed_contra hτ ho hc
    (((l : Module.Dual ℂ X) ∘ₗ g) ∘ₗ (fixed τ K).subtype)
  have hl'K : l' ∈ fixed (contra τ) K := GlobalTheory.ContraRefl.mem_fix.2 h1
  refine ⟨l', Subtype.ext (GlobalTheory.ContraRefl.form_ext hσ hc (fun h hh w => ?_) (fun h hh w => ?_) fun w hw => ?_)⟩
  · show (l' : Module.Dual ℂ Y) (f (σ h w)) = (l' : Module.Dual ℂ Y) (f w)
    rw [f.isIntertwining]
    exact GlobalTheory.ContraRefl.invariant_of_fixed ⟨l', hl'K⟩ h hh (f w)
  · exact GlobalTheory.ContraRefl.invariant_of_fixed ⟨l, hlK⟩ h hh w
  · have hfw : f w ∈ fixed τ K := GlobalTheory.ContraRefl.mem_fix.2 fun h hh => by
      rw [← f.isIntertwining]
      exact congrArg f (GlobalTheory.ContraRefl.mem_fix.1 hw h hh)
    show (l' : Module.Dual ℂ Y) (f w) = (l : Module.Dual ℂ X) w
    have e1 := h2 ⟨f w, hfw⟩
    have e2 := LinearMap.congr_fun hg w
    simp only [LinearMap.comp_apply, LinearMap.id_apply] at e2
    rw [show f w = ((⟨f w, hfw⟩ : fixed τ K) : Y) from rfl, e1]
    simp only [LinearMap.comp_apply, Submodule.subtype_apply]
    exact congrArg _ e2

def LocSub : Prop := ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (N a d : ℕ) (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) (π : CuspSub E a), 1 ≤ N → IsDiscrete V →
  AscEmb V π d → ∀ (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (σ : SmIrr (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)))
    (σ' : SmIrr (GL (Fin N) (w.adicCompletion E))), π.IsLocComp w σ → IsLocCompGL V w σ' →
    ∃ hd : ∑ i : Fin d, (GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) d i.rev).1 = N,
      ∃ f : σ'.ρ.IntertwiningMap (GlobalTheory.LocGL.prodIrr
        (fun i : Fin d => GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) d i.rev) hd), f ≠ 0

def LocDual : Prop := ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]
  [CharZero k] (N d : ℕ) (x : GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL k) (σ' : SmIrr (GL (Fin N) k)), IsUnitarisable x.rep → IsUnitarisable σ'.ρ →
  ∀ (hd : ∑ i : Fin d, (GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor x d i.rev).1 = N)
    (f : σ'.ρ.IntertwiningMap (GlobalTheory.LocGL.prodIrr (fun i : Fin d => GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor x d i.rev) hd)), f ≠ 0 →
    ∃ hd' : ∑ i, (GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor x d i).1 = N,
      ∃ g : (GlobalTheory.LocGL.prodIrr (GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor x d) hd').IntertwiningMap σ'.ρ, Function.Surjective g

def Clause3Asc : Prop := ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (N a d : ℕ) (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) (π : CuspSub E a), 1 ≤ N → IsDiscrete V →
  π.IsUnitary → IsNormalised π → AscEmb V π d → ∀ w σ σ', π.IsLocComp w σ → IsLocCompGL V w σ' →
    GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsSpeh (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) d σ'.ρ

def AscSeed : Prop := AscAll ∧ Clause3Asc

end PositivityTheory.Rg.Rs

def Emb (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) (P : Sm E N → Prop) : Prop :=
  ∃ f : Sm E N →ₗ[ℂ] Sm E N, (∀ φ ∈ V, P (f φ)) ∧ (∀ φ ∈ V, f φ = 0 → φ = 0) ∧ ∀ o, ∀ φ ∈ V, f (act o φ) = act o (f φ)

def AscEmb (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) (π : CuspSub E a) (d : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (h : ∑ _ : Fin d, a = N) (ν : Fin d → HChar E), (∀ i, (ν i).IsAbsPow (-spehExp d i + a * spehExp d i)) ∧
    Emb V (· ∈ indForms h fun i => π.twist (ν i))

def RegInt (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) (τ : E →+* ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∃ m, HasInfCharGL V τ m ∧ m.Nodup ∧ ∀ x ∈ m, ∀ y ∈ m, ∃ n : ℤ, x - y = n

def indForms (h : ∑ i, n i = N) (π : ∀ i, CuspSub E (n i)) : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N) where
  carrier := {φ | (∀ u ∈ unipRad (Ad E) (stdBlocks n h), ∀ g, φ.1 (u * g) = φ.1 g) ∧
    φ ∈ GlobalTheory.PRep.smoothPart (finRep E N) ∧ φ ∈ finVec (cptRep E N) ∧
    ∀ (k : Kinf E N) (g : GL (Fin N) (Af E)),
      (fun m => φ.1 (leviEmb (Ad E) (stdBlocks n h) m * (infGL E N (Unitary.toUnits k) * finGL E N g))) ∈ tensorForms π}
  add_mem' := fun h1 h2 => ⟨fun u hu g => congrArg₂ (· + ·) (h1.1 u hu g) (h2.1 u hu g),
    add_mem h1.2.1 h2.2.1, add_mem h1.2.2.1 h2.2.2.1, fun k g => add_mem (h1.2.2.2 k g) (h2.2.2.2 k g)⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨fun _ _ _ => rfl, zero_mem _, zero_mem _, fun _ _ => zero_mem _⟩
  smul_mem' z _ h1 := ⟨fun u hu g => congrArg (z • ·) (h1.1 u hu g), Submodule.smul_mem _ z h1.2.1,
    Submodule.smul_mem _ z h1.2.2.1, fun k g => Submodule.smul_mem _ z (h1.2.2.2 k g)⟩

def IsQuotOf (V I : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) : Prop :=
  ∃ f : Sm E N →ₗ[ℂ] Sm E N, I.map f = V ∧ ∀ o, ∀ φ ∈ I, f (act o φ) = act o (f φ)

def IsIsoGL (V V' : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) : Prop :=
  ∃ T : V ≃ₗ[ℂ] V', ∀ (o : Op E N) (φ ψ : V), ψ.1 = act o φ.1 → (T ψ).1 = act o (T φ).1

def spehExp (d : ℕ) (i : Fin d) : ℝ := ((d : ℝ) - 1) / 2 - (i : ℕ)

def IsSpehOf (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) (π : CuspSub E a) (d : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (h : ∑ _ : Fin d, a = N) (ν : Fin d → HChar E), (∀ i, (ν i).IsAbsPow (spehExp d i + a * spehExp d i)) ∧
    IsQuotOf V (indForms h fun i => π.twist (ν i))

def IsNormalised (π : CuspSub E a) : Prop := ∀ φ ∈ π.V, ∀ t g, φ.1 (splitCentre E a t * g) = φ.1 g

attribute [local instance] GlobalTheory.LocalData.complRel GlobalTheory.LocalData.isLocalField in

structure HasSpehComp (V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)) (π : CuspSub E a) (d : ℕ) : Prop where
  exLoc : ∀ w, ∃ σ σ', π.IsLocComp w σ ∧ IsLocCompGL V w σ'
  loc : ∀ w σ σ', π.IsLocComp w σ → IsLocCompGL V w σ' →
    GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsSpeh (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) d σ'.ρ
  inf : ∀ τ, ∃ m, π.HasInfChar τ m ∧ HasInfCharGL V τ (GlobalTheory.SphGal.spehInf d m)

theorem IsSpehOf.mul_eq {V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N)} {π : CuspSub E a} (h : IsSpehOf V π d) : d * a = N := by
  obtain ⟨h, -⟩ := h
  simpa using h

  have h2 : Finset.univ.filter (fun b => b < i) = Finset.Iio i := by ext; simp

    ((-spehExp d i + a * spehExp d i : ℝ) : ℂ) =
      ((modExp (fun _ : Fin d => Fin a) i : ℤ) : ℂ) / 2 + (((d : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - ((i.rev : ℕ) : ℂ)) := by

theorem gen (hF : FrobRecip) (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]
    (N a d : ℕ) (h : ∑ _ : Fin d, a = N) (σ : SmIrr (GL (Fin a) k)) (σ' : SmIrr (GL (Fin N) k))
    (ψ : Fin d → GL (Fin a) k →* ℂˣ)
    (hψ : ∀ i, ψ i = detChar (Fin a) (absPow k ((-spehExp d i + a * spehExp d i : ℝ) : ℂ)))
    (ℓ : σ'.V →ₗ[ℂ] PiTensorProduct ℂ fun _ : Fin d => σ.V) (h0 : ℓ ≠ 0)
    (hℓ : ∀ (p : parab k (stdBlocks (fun _ : Fin d => a) h)) (v : σ'.V), ℓ (σ'.ρ p v) =
      piTensor (fun i => GlobalTheory.SmGL.twist σ.ρ (ψ i)) (leviProj k (stdBlocks (fun _ : Fin d => a) h) p) (ℓ v)) :
    ∃ hd : ∑ i : Fin d, (spehFactor (IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) d i.rev).1 = N,
      ∃ f : σ'.ρ.IntertwiningMap (prodIrr (fun i : Fin d => spehFactor (IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) d i.rev) hd),
        f ≠ 0 := by
  have hx := hF k N d (fun _ => a) h σ' (PiTensorProduct ℂ fun _ : Fin d => σ.V)
    (piTensor (Γ := fun _ : Fin d => GL (Fin a) k) (X := fun _ : Fin d => σ.V) fun i => GlobalTheory.SmGL.twist σ.ρ (ψ i)) ℓ h0 hℓ
  have hy := PositivityTheory.RSHS.LocalComponentTwist.levi_equiv σ.ρ (fun i : Fin d => ((d : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - ((i.rev : ℕ) : ℂ))
    (fun i => ((-spehExp d i + a * spehExp d i : ℝ) : ℂ)) (hst_rev a d) ψ hψ
  refine hx.elim fun g hg => hy.elim fun φ => ?_
  let T : ∀ i : Fin d, (GlobalTheory.SmGL.twist σ.ρ (detChar (Fin a) (absPow k (((d : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - ((i.rev : ℕ) : ℂ))))).Equiv
      (spehFactor (IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) d i.rev).rep := fun i =>
    (twistEquiv (IrrGL.equivOf σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) _).trans
      ((IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred).twEquiv _ (isSmoothChar_absPow _))
  let M := multiProdEquiv (stdBlocks (fun _ : Fin d => a) h) T
  exact ⟨h, comp_ne g hg ((GlobalTheory.LocGL.parIndEquiv (stdBlocks (fun _ : Fin d => a) h) φ).symm.trans M)⟩

theorem locSub_of (hR : RestrictAtPoint) (hP : ExistsProj) (hF : FrobRecip) : LocSub :=
  fun E _ _ _ N a d V π _ _ hA w σ σ' hσ hσ' =>
    hA.elim fun h hA => hA.elim fun ν hA =>
      (step1 hR hP E N d (fun _ => a) h (fun i => π.twist (ν i)) w
        (fun i => σ.twist (CuspSub.locDet a (ν i) w) (CuspSub.isOpen_ker_locDet (ν i) w))
        (fun i => PositivityTheory.RSHS.LocalComponentTwist.isLocComp_twist π (ν i) hσ) V hA.2 σ' hσ').elim fun ℓ hℓ =>
      gen hF (w.adicCompletion E) N a d h σ σ' (fun i => CuspSub.locDet a (ν i) w)
        (fun i => congrArg (fun χ : (w.adicCompletion E)ˣ →* ℂˣ => χ.comp Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det)
          (PositivityTheory.RSHS.LocalComponentTwist.loc_eq_absPow (hA.1 i) w)) ℓ hℓ.1 hℓ.2

end PositivityTheory.Rg.Rs.LsTop
