def GenUnitCC : Prop :=
  ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k] [CharZero k]
    (N : ℕ) (V : Type) [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] (π : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) V),
    IsSmooth π → π.IsIrreducible → IsUnitarisable π → IsGeneric π →
    ∃ (h : ℕ) (δ : Fin h → IrrGL k) (Δ : Fin h → Segment k) (s : Fin h → ℝ),
      (∀ j, IsLSeg (Δ j) (δ j).rep) ∧ (∀ j, UOrder.UnitCC (δ j).rep) ∧ (∀ j, |s j| < 1 / 2) ∧
      ∃ hd : ∑ j, ((δ j).nu (s j)).1 = N, Nonempty (π.Equiv (prodIrr (fun j => (δ j).nu (s j)) hd))

def StandardCosocleGL : Prop :=
  ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k] [CharZero k]
    (𝔪 : Multiset (Segment k)) (h N : ℕ) (x : Fin h → IrrGL k) (hd : ∑ a, (x a).1 = N), LFactors 𝔪 x →
    ∃ π : IrrRep (GL (Fin N) k), IsCosocle π.ρ (prodIrr x hd)

def IsSocle (σ : Representation ℂ G W) (π : Representation ℂ G V) : Prop :=
  σ.IsIrreducible ∧ ∃ f : σ.IntertwiningMap π, Function.Injective f ∧
    ∀ S : Subrepresentation π, IsAtom S → S = f.range

def IsCosocle (σ : Representation ℂ G W) (π : Representation ℂ G V) : Prop :=
  σ.IsIrreducible ∧ ∃ f : π.IntertwiningMap σ, Function.Surjective f ∧
    ∀ S : Subrepresentation π, IsCoatom S → S = f.ker

theorem IsSocle.embeds {σ : Representation ℂ G W} {π : Representation ℂ G V} (h : IsSocle σ π) : Embeds σ π :=

SHA256 eb811edd5b01f69aab6b433f7358976bcbf55ce6505b41a6f8c50c1e09be7ca7

def IsZSeg (Δ : Segment k) (π : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) V) : Prop :=
  ∃ hd : ∑ a, (Δ.term a).1 = N, IsSocle π (prodIrr Δ.term hd)

def IsLSeg (Δ : Segment k) (π : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) V) : Prop :=
  ∃ hd : ∑ a, (Δ.term a).1 = N, IsCosocle π (prodIrr Δ.term hd)

def ZFactors (𝔪 : Multiset (Segment k)) (x : Fin h → IrrGL k) : Prop :=
  ∃ Δ : Fin h → Segment k, IsOrderedForm Δ 𝔪 ∧ ∀ i, IsZSeg (Δ i) (x i).rep

def LFactors (𝔪 : Multiset (Segment k)) (x : Fin h → IrrGL k) : Prop :=
  ∃ Δ : Fin h → Segment k, IsOrderedForm Δ 𝔪 ∧ ∀ i, IsLSeg (Δ i) (x i).rep

def IsZ (𝔪 : Multiset (Segment k)) (π : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) V) : Prop :=
  ∃ (h : ℕ) (x : Fin h → IrrGL k) (hd : ∑ a, (x a).1 = N), ZFactors 𝔪 x ∧ IsSocle π (prodIrr x hd)

def IsL (𝔪 : Multiset (Segment k)) (π : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) V) : Prop :=
  ∃ (h : ℕ) (x : Fin h → IrrGL k) (hd : ∑ a, (x a).1 = N), LFactors 𝔪 x ∧ IsCosocle π (prodIrr x hd)

def spehFactor (x : IrrGL k) (d : ℕ) (i : Fin d) : IrrGL k := x.nu (((d : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (i : ℕ))

def IsSpeh (x : IrrGL k) (d : ℕ) (π : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) V) : Prop :=
  ∃ hd : ∑ a, (spehFactor x d a).1 = N, IsCosocle π (prodIrr (spehFactor x d) hd)

theorem hasCentralChar_nu (x : IrrGL k) {ω : kˣ →* ℂˣ} (h : HasCentralChar x.rep ω) (s : ℂ) :
    HasCentralChar (x.nu s).rep (ω * absPow k s ^ x.1) := by
  have h1 := hasCentralChar_twist h (absPow k s)
  rw [Fintype.card_fin] at h1
  exact hasCentralChar_of_equiv (x.twEquiv _ _) h1

theorem norm_eq_one_of_isUnitarisable {G X : Type} [Group G] [AddCommGroup X] [Module ℂ X] [Nontrivial X]
    {ρ : Representation ℂ G X} (hU : IsUnitarisable ρ) {g : G} {c : ℂ} (hc : ∀ v, ρ g v = c • v) : ‖c‖ = 1 := by
  obtain ⟨B, -, hp, hi⟩ := hU
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := exists_ne (0 : X)
  have h := hi g v v
  have h0 : B v v ≠ 0 := fun e => by simpa [e] using hp v hv
  rw [hc, B.map_smul, LinearMap.smul_apply, LinearMap.map_smulₛₗ, smul_eq_mul, smul_eq_mul, ← mul_assoc,
    Complex.mul_conj, mul_eq_right₀ h0] at h
  have h2 : ‖c‖ ^ 2 = 1 := by
    rw [← Complex.normSq_eq_norm_sq]; exact_mod_cast h
  exact (pow_eq_one_iff_of_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) two_ne_zero).1 h2

theorem log_norm_mul_absPow (ω : kˣ →* ℂˣ) (t : ℝ) (d : ℕ) (z : kˣ) :
    Real.log ‖(((ω * absPow k (t : ℂ) ^ d) z : ℂˣ) : ℂ)‖ =
      Real.log ‖(ω z : ℂ)‖ + d * t * Real.log (nabs k z) := by
  have hz := nabs_pos' k z
  have hω : ‖(ω z : ℂ)‖ ≠ 0 := norm_ne_zero_iff.2 (ω z).ne_zero
  rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.pow_apply, Units.val_mul, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, val_absPow_ofReal,
    norm_mul, norm_pow, Complex.norm_real, Real.norm_of_nonneg (Real.rpow_nonneg hz.le _),
    Real.log_mul hω (pow_ne_zero _ (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hz _).ne'), Real.log_pow, Real.log_rpow hz]
  ring

theorem sum_range_mul_two (l : ℕ) : (∑ i ∈ Finset.range l, (i : ℝ)) * 2 = l * ((l : ℝ) - 1) := by
  induction l with
  | zero => simp
  | succ n ih => rw [Finset.sum_range_succ, add_mul, ih]; push_cast; ring

theorem log_norm_centralChar {δ : IrrGL k} {Δ : Segment k} (hL : IsLSeg Δ δ.rep) (hU : IsUnitarisable δ.rep)
    {ω : kˣ →* ℂˣ} (hω : HasCentralChar Δ.first.rep ω) (z : kˣ) :
    Real.log ‖(ω z : ℂ)‖ = -((Δ.d : ℝ) * ((Δ.len : ℝ) - 1) / 2) * Real.log (nabs k z) := by
  obtain ⟨hd, hi, f, hf, -⟩ := hL
  have hterm : ∀ a : Fin Δ.len,
      HasCentralChar (Δ.term a).rep (ω * absPow k (((a : ℕ) : ℝ) : ℂ) ^ Δ.d) := fun a => by
    have h := hasCentralChar_nu Δ.first hω ((a : ℕ) : ℂ)
    rw [Complex.ofReal_natCast]
    exact h
  have hsc : ∀ v, δ.rep (GeneralLinearGroup.scalar (Fin δ.1) z) v =
      (∏ a : Fin Δ.len, (((ω * absPow k (((a : ℕ) : ℝ) : ℂ) ^ Δ.d) z : ℂˣ) : ℂ)) • v := by
    intro v
    obtain ⟨w, rfl⟩ := hf v
    have h1 := GlobalTheory.BoundaryMaps.multiProd_scalar (stdBlocks (fun a => (Δ.term a).1) hd)
      (fun a => (Δ.term a).rep) _ hterm z w
    rw [← f.isIntertwining]
    exact (congrArg f h1).trans (map_smul f _ _)
  haveI := Model.nontrivial_of_isIrreducible hi
  have hc := congrArg Real.log (norm_eq_one_of_isUnitarisable hU hsc)
  rw [norm_prod, Real.log_one, Real.log_prod fun a _ => norm_ne_zero_iff.2 (Units.ne_zero _)] at hc
  simp only [log_norm_mul_absPow] at hc
  rw [Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, nsmul_eq_mul,
    ← Finset.sum_mul, ← Finset.mul_sum, Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun i => (i : ℝ)) Δ.len] at hc
  have hS := sum_range_mul_two Δ.len
  have hl : (Δ.len : ℝ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 Δ.len_pos.ne'
  apply mul_left_cancel₀ hl
  linear_combination hc - ((Δ.d : ℝ) * Real.log (nabs k z) / 2) * hS

theorem add_one_le_of_precedes (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]
    (δ δ' : IrrGL k) (Δ Δ' : Segment k) (hL : IsLSeg Δ δ.rep) (hU : IsUnitarisable δ.rep) (hL' : IsLSeg Δ' δ'.rep)
    (hU' : IsUnitarisable δ'.rep) (x y : ℝ) (hP : (Δ.nu x).Precedes (Δ'.nu y)) : x + 1 ≤ y := by
  obtain ⟨⟨-, -, hnsub⟩, j, hj, hfirst⟩ := hP
  have hf : Δ'.first.nu (y : ℂ) = (Δ.first.nu (x : ℂ)).nu (j : ℂ) := hfirst
  have hρ' : Δ'.first = Δ.first.nu (((x + j - y : ℝ)) : ℂ) := by
    have h1 := congrArg (fun r => r.nu (-(y : ℂ))) hf
    simp only [IrrGL.nu_nu, add_neg_cancel, IrrGL.nu_zero] at h1
    rw [h1]
    congr 1
    push_cast
    ring
  have hd : Δ'.d = Δ.d := congrArg Sigma.fst hρ'
  obtain ⟨ω, -, hω⟩ := exists_smooth_hasCentralChar Δ.first.2.rep.smooth Δ.first.2.rep.irr
  have hω' : HasCentralChar Δ'.first.rep (ω * absPow k (((x + j - y : ℝ)) : ℂ) ^ Δ.first.1) := by
    rw [hρ']
    exact hasCentralChar_nu _ hω _
  obtain ⟨ϖ, hϖ⟩ := exists_uniformizer k
  have hne : nabs k ϖ ≠ 1 := fun e => uniformizer_lt_one.ne (hϖ ▸ (nabs_eq_one_iff ϖ).1 e)
  have hL0 : Real.log (nabs k ϖ) ≠ 0 := Real.log_ne_zero_of_pos_of_ne_one (nabs_pos' k ϖ) hne
  have hA := log_norm_centralChar hL hU hω ϖ
  have hA' := log_norm_centralChar hL' hU' hω' ϖ
  rw [log_norm_mul_absPow, hd] at hA'
  have hdL : (Δ.d : ℝ) * Real.log (nabs k ϖ) ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero (Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 Δ.d_pos.ne') hL0
  have ht : x + j - y = ((Δ.len : ℝ) - Δ'.len) / 2 := by
    apply mul_left_cancel₀ hdL
    have e1 : (Δ.first.1 : ℝ) = Δ.d := rfl
    rw [e1] at hA'
    linear_combination hA' - hA
  have hlen : Δ.len < j + Δ'.len := by
    by_contra hcon
    push_neg at hcon
    apply hnsub
    intro w hw
    obtain ⟨a, ha, rfl⟩ := Segment.mem_carrier.1 hw
    refine Segment.mem_carrier.2 ⟨j + a, ?_, ?_⟩
    · have ha' : a < Δ'.len := ha
      show j + a < Δ.len
      omega
    · rw [hfirst, IrrGL.nu_nu]
      push_cast
      rfl
  have hj1 : (1 : ℝ) ≤ j := by exact_mod_cast hj
  have hl1 : (Δ.len : ℝ) + 1 ≤ j + Δ'.len := by exact_mod_cast hlen
  linarith

end GlobalTheory.LocGL.UnitaryOrder

theorem spehCosocle_uc (hG : PositivityTheory.RgUd.Exp.GenUnitCC) (hSC : GlobalTheory.Published.StandardCosocleGL)
    [CharZero k] (x : GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL k) (hg : GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsGeneric x.rep)
    (hu : GlobalTheory.SmGL.IsUnitarisable x.rep) (d : ℕ) (hd : ∑ i, (GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor x d i).1 = N) :
    ∃ (W : Type) (_ : AddCommGroup W) (_ : Module ℂ W) (τ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) W),
      GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsCosocle τ (GlobalTheory.LocGL.prodIrr (GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor x d) hd) := by
  obtain ⟨m, δ, Δ, s, hΔ, hδu, hs, hd0, ⟨φ⟩⟩ := hG k _ _ x.rep x.2.rep.smooth x.2.rep.irr hu hg
  let y : Fin d → Fin m → IrrGL k := fun i j => ((δ j).nu (s j)).nu (((d : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (i : ℕ))
  have hN : ∀ i, ∑ j, (y i j).1 = (spehFactor x d i).1 := fun _ => hd0
  let Y : Fin (d * m) → IrrGL k := fun p => y (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).1 (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2
  have hM' : ∑ p, (Y p).1 = N := by
    rw [← hd, ← finProdFinEquiv.sum_comp, Fintype.sum_prod_type]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
    simp only [Y, Equiv.symm_apply_apply]
    exact hN i
  let e1 : ∀ i : Fin d, (spehFactor x d i).rep.Equiv (prodIrr (y i) (hN i)) := fun i =>
    (x.twEquiv _ (isSmoothChar_absPow _)).symm.trans
      ((twistEquiv φ _).trans (prodIrrTwistEquiv _ hd0 _ (isSmoothChar_absPow _)))
  obtain ⟨e2⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.parabolicInduction_in_stages_product_of_products k d m y (fun i => (spehFactor x d i).1) N hN hd hM'
  let E := (multiProdEquiv _ e1).trans e2
  let Γs : Fin (d * m) → Segment k := fun p =>
    ((Δ (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2).nu (s (finProdFinEquiv.symm p).2)).nu
      ((((d : ℝ) - 1) / 2 - ((finProdFinEquiv.symm p).1 : ℕ) : ℝ) : ℂ)
  have hcast : ∀ i : ℕ, (((((d : ℝ) - 1) / 2 - i : ℝ)) : ℂ) = ((d : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (i : ℕ) := fun i => by
    push_cast; ring
  have hL : ∀ p, IsLSeg (Γs p) (Y p).rep := fun p => by
    simp only [Γs, Y, y, hcast]
    exact GlobalTheory.isLSeg_nu (GlobalTheory.isLSeg_nu (hΔ _) _) _
  have hord : ∀ p p' : Fin (d * m), p < p' → ¬ (Γs p).Precedes (Γs p') := by
    intro p p' hpp' hP
    obtain ⟨⟨i, j⟩, rfl⟩ := finProdFinEquiv.surjective p
    obtain ⟨⟨i', j'⟩, rfl⟩ := finProdFinEquiv.surjective p'
    have hii : (i : ℕ) ≤ i' := by
      by_contra hlt
      have h1 : (i' : ℕ) + 1 ≤ i := by omega
      have h2 := Nat.mul_le_mul_left m h1
      have h3 := Fin.lt_def.1 hpp'
      simp only [finProdFinEquiv_apply_val] at h3
      have := j.2
      have := j'.2
      nlinarith
    simp only [Γs, Equiv.symm_apply_apply, GlobalTheory.segment_nu_nu] at hP
    have key := PositivityTheory.RgUd.UOrder.add_one_le_of_precedes_u k (δ j) (δ j') (Δ j) (Δ j') (hΔ j) (hδu j) (hΔ j')
      (hδu j') (s j + (((d : ℝ) - 1) / 2 - (i : ℕ))) (s j' + (((d : ℝ) - 1) / 2 - (i' : ℕ)))
      (by push_cast at hP ⊢; exact hP)
    have hi : ((i : ℕ) : ℝ) ≤ (i' : ℕ) := by exact_mod_cast hii
    have := abs_lt.1 (hs j)
    have := abs_lt.1 (hs j')
    linarith
  have hLF : LFactors (↑((List.range d).flatMap fun i : ℕ =>
      (List.ofFn fun j => (Δ j).nu (s j)).map fun Γ => Γ.nu ((((d : ℝ) - 1) / 2 - i : ℝ) : ℂ))) Y :=
    ⟨Γs, ⟨GlobalTheory.univ_map_speh_segments d m Δ s, hord⟩, hL⟩
  obtain ⟨π, hπ⟩ := hSC k _ (d * m) N Y hM' hLF
  exact ⟨_, _, _, π.ρ, hπ.of_equiv (.refl _) E.symm⟩

end PositivityTheory.Rg.Rs.C3.UC

theorem isSpeh_of_quot {k : Type} [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k] {N : ℕ}
    {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] {ρ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) V}
    (hi : ρ.IsIrreducible) (x : GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL k) (d : ℕ)
    (hd : ∑ i, (GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor x d i).1 = N)
    (hc : ∃ (W : Type) (_ : AddCommGroup W) (_ : Module ℂ W) (τ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin N) k) W),
      GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsCosocle τ (GlobalTheory.LocGL.prodIrr (GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor x d) hd))
    (f : (GlobalTheory.LocGL.prodIrr (GlobalTheory.LocGL.spehFactor x d) hd).IntertwiningMap ρ) (hf : Function.Surjective f) :
    GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsSpeh x d ρ := by
  obtain ⟨W, _, _, τ, hτ⟩ := hc
  obtain ⟨e⟩ := hτ.equiv_of_surjective hi f hf
  exact ⟨hd, hτ.of_equiv e.symm (.refl _)⟩

theorem charZero_completion {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) :
    CharZero (w.adicCompletion E) :=
  charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap E (w.adicCompletion E)).injective

theorem clause3Asc_of (hL : LocSub) (hD : LocDual) (hG : PositivityTheory.RgUd.Exp.GenUnitCC)
    (hSC : GlobalTheory.Published.StandardCosocleGL) : Clause3Asc := by
  intro E _ _ _ N a d V π hN hV hu hn hasc w σ σ' hσ hσ'
  obtain ⟨hd, f, hf⟩ := hL E N a d V π hN hV hasc w σ σ' hσ hσ'
  obtain ⟨hg, hun⟩ := PositivityTheory.RSHS.LocalComponentGL.generic_unitarisable hu hn hσ
  have hU' : IsUnitarisable σ'.ρ := GlobalTheory.DiscreteUnit.isUnitarisable_of_isLocCompGL E N V hV w σ' hσ'
  have := charZero_completion (E := E) w
  obtain ⟨hd', g, hg'⟩ := hD _ N d _ σ' hun hU' hd f hf
  exact isSpeh_of_quot σ'.irred _ d hd' (UC.spehCosocle_uc hG hSC _ hg hun d hd') g hg'

theorem clause3Asc_holds (hL : LocSub) (hD : LocDual) : Clause3Asc :=
  have hZ := PositivityTheory.RgGu.segmentRepresentation
  clause3Asc_of hL hD

variable {A : Type} [Ring A] [Algebra ℂ A]

def upperSpan {r m : ℕ} (X : Fin (r + m) → Fin (r + m) → A) : Submodule ℂ A :=
  Submodule.span ℂ {y | ∃ (p : Fin r) (q : Fin m) (w : A), y = X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.natAdd r q) * w}

theorem span_mul_right_mem {S : Set A} (hS : ∀ y ∈ S, ∀ a : A, y * a ∈ S) {x : A}
    (hx : x ∈ Submodule.span ℂ S) (a : A) : x * a ∈ Submodule.span ℂ S := by
  induction hx using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem y hy => exact Submodule.subset_span (hS y hy a)
  | zero => simp
  | add x y _ _ hx hy => rw [add_mul]; exact add_mem hx hy
  | smul c x _ hx => rw [smul_mul_assoc]; exact Submodule.smul_mem _ c hx

theorem levi_mul_mem {r m : ℕ} {X : Fin (r + m) → Fin (r + m) → A} (hX : GlRel X) (s t : Fin m)
    {y : A} (hy : y ∈ upperSpan X) : X (Fin.natAdd r s) (Fin.natAdd r t) * y ∈ upperSpan X := by
  induction hy using Submodule.span_induction with
  | mem y hy =>
    obtain ⟨p, q, w, rfl⟩ := hy
    have h := hX (Fin.natAdd r s) (Fin.natAdd r t) (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.natAdd r q)
    have hne : Fin.natAdd r t ≠ Fin.castAdd m p := by
      intro h; have := congrArg Fin.val h; simp at this; omega
    rw [if_neg hne] at h
    have h' : X (Fin.natAdd r s) (Fin.natAdd r t) * X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.natAdd r q) =
        X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.natAdd r q) * X (Fin.natAdd r s) (Fin.natAdd r t) +
          (0 - if Fin.natAdd r q = Fin.natAdd r s then X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.natAdd r t) else 0) := by
      rw [← h]; abel
    rw [← mul_assoc, h', add_mul, mul_assoc]
    refine add_mem (Submodule.subset_span ⟨p, q, _, rfl⟩) ?_
    rw [zero_sub, neg_mul]
    refine neg_mem ?_
    split_ifs
    · exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨p, t, w, rfl⟩
    · rw [zero_mul]; exact zero_mem _
  | zero => rw [mul_zero]; exact zero_mem _
  | add x y _ _ hx hy => rw [mul_add]; exact add_mem hx hy
  | smul c x _ hx => rw [mul_smul_comm]; exact Submodule.smul_mem _ c hx

theorem prod_mul_mem (J : Submodule ℂ A) : ∀ (k : ℕ) (g : Fin k → A),
    (∀ i, (∀ y ∈ J, g i * y ∈ J) ∨ (∀ y, g i * y ∈ J)) → (∃ i, ∀ y, g i * y ∈ J) →
    ∀ z, (List.ofFn g).prod * z ∈ J := by
  intro k
  induction k with
  | zero => intro g _ h; obtain ⟨i, _⟩ := h; exact i.elim0
  | succ k ih =>
    intro g hg h z
    obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := h
    rw [List.ofFn_succ, List.prod_cons, mul_assoc]
    by_cases h0 : ∀ y, g 0 * y ∈ J
    · exact h0 _
    · have hp : ∀ y ∈ J, g 0 * y ∈ J := (hg 0).resolve_right h0
      apply hp
      refine ih (fun j => g j.succ) (fun j => hg _) ?_ z
      cases i using Fin.cases with
      | zero => exact absurd hi h0
      | succ j => exact ⟨j, hi⟩

theorem qminor_cast {n t t' : ℕ} (X : Fin n → Fin n → A) (h : t' = t) (a b : Fin t → Fin n) (u : ℂ) :
    qminor X (a ∘ finCongr h) (b ∘ finCongr h) u = qminor X a b u := by
  subst h; rfl

theorem Tm_restrict {n k : ℕ} (X : Fin n → Fin n → A) (f : Fin k → Fin n) (hf : Function.Injective f)
    (w : ℂ) (p q : Fin k) : Tm (fun p q => X (f p) (f q)) w p q = Tm X w (f p) (f q) := by
  unfold Tm; simp [hf.eq_iff]

theorem glRel_restrict {n k : ℕ} {X : Fin n → Fin n → A} (hX : GlRel X) (f : Fin k → Fin n)
    (hf : Function.Injective f) : GlRel (fun p q => X (f p) (f q)) := by
  intro a b c d
  have := hX (f a) (f b) (f c) (f d)
  simpa [hf.eq_iff] using this

theorem qdet_eq_flip {r m : ℕ} (X : Fin (r + m) → Fin (r + m) → A) (hX : GlRel X) (u : ℂ) :
    qdet X u = qminor X (finAddFlip : Fin (m + r) ≃ Fin (r + m)) finAddFlip u := by
  have hc := qminor_cast X (add_comm m r) (id : Fin (r + m) → Fin (r + m)) id u
  set c : Fin (m + r) ≃ Fin (r + m) := finCongr (add_comm m r) with hcdef
  set τ : Equiv.Perm (Fin (m + r)) := finAddFlip.trans (finCongr (add_comm r m)) with hτ
  have he : (⇑(finAddFlip : Fin (m + r) ≃ Fin (r + m))) = ⇑c ∘ ⇑τ := by
    funext k; apply Fin.ext; simp [c, τ]
  rw [he, quantumMinorRowSkew_holds A (r + m) (m + r) X (⇑c) (⇑c ∘ ⇑τ) τ u,
    quantumMinorColSkew_holds A (r + m) (m + r) X hX ⇑c ⇑c τ u, ← mul_assoc, ← Int.cast_mul,
    ← Units.val_mul, Int.units_mul_self, Units.val_one, Int.cast_one, one_mul]
  exact hc.symm

theorem Tm_levi_mem {r m : ℕ} {X : Fin (r + m) → Fin (r + m) → A} (hX : GlRel X) (w : ℂ) (s t : Fin m)
    {y : A} (hy : y ∈ upperSpan X) : Tm X w (Fin.natAdd r s) (Fin.natAdd r t) * y ∈ upperSpan X := by
  unfold Tm
  rw [add_mul]
  refine add_mem ?_ (levi_mul_mem hX s t hy)
  split_ifs
  · rw [← Algebra.smul_def]; exact Submodule.smul_mem _ w hy
  · rw [zero_mul]; exact zero_mem _

theorem Tm_upper_mem {r m : ℕ} (X : Fin (r + m) → Fin (r + m) → A) (w : ℂ) (p : Fin r) (q : Fin m)
    (y : A) : Tm X w (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.natAdd r q) * y ∈ upperSpan X := by
  unfold Tm
  have hne : Fin.castAdd m p ≠ Fin.natAdd r q := by
    intro h; have := congrArg Fin.val h; simp at this; omega
  rw [if_neg hne, zero_add]
  exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨p, q, y, rfl⟩

theorem two_block {r m : ℕ} (X : Fin (r + m) → Fin (r + m) → A) (hX : GlRel X) (u : ℂ) :
    qdet X u - qdet (fun p q : Fin m => X (Fin.natAdd r p) (Fin.natAdd r q)) u *
      qdet (fun p q : Fin r => X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.castAdd m q)) (u - m) ∈ upperSpan X := by
  classical
  rw [qdet_eq_flip X hX u]
  set G : Equiv.Perm (Fin (m + r)) → A := fun σ => ((Equiv.Perm.sign σ : ℤ) : A) *
    (List.ofFn fun k : Fin (m + r) =>
      Tm X (u - (k : ℂ)) ((finAddFlip : Fin (m + r) ≃ Fin (r + m)) (σ k)) (finAddFlip k)).prod with hG
  have h1 : qminor X (finAddFlip : Fin (m + r) ≃ Fin (r + m)) finAddFlip u = ∑ σ, G σ := rfl
  rw [h1, ← Equiv.sum_comp (Equiv.permCongr finSumFinEquiv) G]
  set S := (Finset.univ : Finset (Equiv.Perm (Fin m) × Equiv.Perm (Fin r))).image
    (fun p => Equiv.Perm.sumCongr p.1 p.2) with hS
  rw [← Finset.sum_add_sum_compl S]
  have hblock : ∑ π ∈ S, G (Equiv.permCongr finSumFinEquiv π) =
      qdet (fun p q : Fin m => X (Fin.natAdd r p) (Fin.natAdd r q)) u *
        qdet (fun p q : Fin r => X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.castAdd m q)) (u - m) := by
    have hinj : Set.InjOn (fun p : Equiv.Perm (Fin m) × Equiv.Perm (Fin r) => Equiv.Perm.sumCongr p.1 p.2)
        (Finset.univ : Finset (Equiv.Perm (Fin m) × Equiv.Perm (Fin r))) := by
      intro x _ y _ hxy
      exact Equiv.Perm.sumCongrHom_injective (α := Fin m) (β := Fin r) hxy
    rw [hS, Finset.sum_image hinj, Fintype.sum_prod_type]
    unfold qdet qminor
    rw [Finset.sum_mul_sum]
    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun α _ => Finset.sum_congr rfl fun β _ => ?_
    simp only [hG, Equiv.permCongr_def, Equiv.Perm.sign_symm_trans_trans, Equiv.Perm.sign_sumCongr,
      Units.val_mul, Int.cast_mul, List.ofFn_add, List.prod_append]
    rw [show ∀ (a b : ℤ) (P Q : A), (a : A) * b * (P * Q) = a * P * (b * Q) by
      intro a b P Q; rw [mul_assoc, ← mul_assoc (b : A), Int.cast_comm b P, mul_assoc, mul_assoc]]
    congr 3
    · refine congrArg List.ofFn (funext fun i => ?_)
      have e : (Fin.castLE (by omega) i : Fin (m + r)) = Fin.castAdd r i := rfl
      simp only [e, Equiv.trans_apply, finSumFinEquiv_symm_apply_castAdd, Equiv.sumCongr_apply,
        Sum.map_inl, finSumFinEquiv_apply_left, finAddFlip_apply_castAdd, Fin.val_castAdd, id]
      exact (Tm_restrict X (Fin.natAdd r) (fun a b h => Fin.ext (by simpa using congrArg Fin.val h)) _ _ _).symm
    · refine congrArg List.ofFn (funext fun i => ?_)
      simp only [Equiv.trans_apply, finSumFinEquiv_symm_apply_natAdd, Equiv.sumCongr_apply,
        Sum.map_inr, finSumFinEquiv_apply_right, finAddFlip_apply_natAdd, Fin.val_natAdd, id]
      rw [Tm_restrict X (Fin.castAdd m) (fun a b h => Fin.ext (by simpa using congrArg Fin.val h))]
      congr 1
      push_cast; ring
  rw [hblock, add_sub_cancel_left]
  refine Submodule.sum_mem _ fun π hπ => ?_
  have hπ' : π ∉ S := Finset.mem_compl.1 hπ
  have hmaps : ¬ Set.MapsTo π (Set.range Sum.inl) (Set.range Sum.inl) := by
    intro hm
    obtain ⟨⟨α, β⟩, hαβ⟩ := Equiv.Perm.mem_sumCongrHom_range_of_perm_mapsTo_inl hm
    exact hπ' (Finset.mem_image.2 ⟨(α, β), Finset.mem_univ _, hαβ⟩)
  obtain ⟨i₀, l₀, hl₀⟩ : ∃ i₀ : Fin m, ∃ l : Fin r, π (Sum.inl i₀) = Sum.inr l := by
    by_contra hc
    apply hmaps
    rintro _ ⟨i, rfl⟩
    cases h : π (Sum.inl i) with
    | inl j => exact ⟨j, rfl⟩
    | inr l => exact absurd ⟨i, l, h⟩ hc
  show ((Equiv.Perm.sign _ : ℤ) : A) * _ ∈ _
  rw [← zsmul_eq_mul]
  refine zsmul_mem ?_ _
  rw [List.ofFn_add, List.prod_append]
  refine prod_mul_mem _ m _ ?_ ⟨i₀, ?_⟩ _
  · intro i
    have e : (Fin.castLE (by omega) i : Fin (m + r)) = Fin.castAdd r i := rfl
    simp only [e, Equiv.permCongr_apply, finSumFinEquiv_symm_apply_castAdd, finAddFlip_apply_castAdd]
    cases h : π (Sum.inl i) with
    | inl j =>
      left
      intro y hy
      simp only [finSumFinEquiv_apply_left, finAddFlip_apply_castAdd]
      exact Tm_levi_mem hX _ _ _ hy
    | inr l =>
      right
      intro y
      simp only [finSumFinEquiv_apply_right, finAddFlip_apply_natAdd]
      exact Tm_upper_mem X _ _ _ y
  · intro y
    have e : (Fin.castLE (by omega) i₀ : Fin (m + r)) = Fin.castAdd r i₀ := rfl
    simp only [e, Equiv.permCongr_apply, finSumFinEquiv_symm_apply_castAdd, finAddFlip_apply_castAdd,
      hl₀, finSumFinEquiv_apply_right, finAddFlip_apply_natAdd]
    exact Tm_upper_mem X _ _ _ y

theorem entry_comm {r m : ℕ} {X : Fin (r + m) → Fin (r + m) → A} (hX : GlRel X) (s t : Fin m)
    (p q : Fin r) :
    Commute (X (Fin.natAdd r s) (Fin.natAdd r t)) (X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.castAdd m q)) := by
  have h := hX (Fin.natAdd r s) (Fin.natAdd r t) (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.castAdd m q)
  have h1 : Fin.natAdd r t ≠ Fin.castAdd m p := by
    intro h; have := congrArg Fin.val h; simp at this; omega
  have h2 : Fin.castAdd m q ≠ Fin.natAdd r s := by
    intro h; have := congrArg Fin.val h; simp at this; omega
  rw [if_neg h1, if_neg h2, sub_zero] at h
  exact sub_eq_zero.1 h

theorem ite_algebraMap_comm (P : Prop) [Decidable P] (c : ℂ) (z : A) :
    Commute (if P then algebraMap ℂ A c else 0) z := by
  split_ifs
  · exact Algebra.commute_algebraMap_left c z
  · exact Commute.zero_left z

theorem qdet_comm {r m : ℕ} {X : Fin (r + m) → Fin (r + m) → A} (hX : GlRel X) (v w : ℂ) :
    Commute (qdet (fun p q : Fin m => X (Fin.natAdd r p) (Fin.natAdd r q)) v)
      (qdet (fun p q : Fin r => X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.castAdd m q)) w) := by
  unfold qdet qminor
  refine Commute.sum_left _ _ _ fun α _ => Commute.sum_right _ _ _ fun β _ => ?_
  refine Commute.mul_left (Int.cast_commute _ _) (Commute.mul_right (Int.cast_commute _ _).symm ?_)
  refine Commute.list_prod_left _ _ fun x hx => Commute.list_prod_right _ _ fun y hy => ?_
  obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := List.mem_ofFn.1 hx
  obtain ⟨j, rfl⟩ := List.mem_ofFn.1 hy
  unfold Tm
  exact Commute.add_left (ite_algebraMap_comm _ _ _)
    (Commute.add_right (ite_algebraMap_comm _ _ _).symm (entry_comm hX _ _ _ _))

theorem modExp_castSucc {c : ℕ} (n : Fin (c + 1) → ℕ) (j : Fin c) :
    modExp (fun i => Fin (n i)) (Fin.castSucc j) =
      modExp (fun i => Fin (n (Fin.castSucc i))) j + n (Fin.last c) := by
  unfold modExp
  simp only [Fintype.card_fin, Finset.sum_filter, Fin.sum_univ_castSucc, Fin.castSucc_lt_castSucc_iff,
    Fin.castSucc_lt_last, if_true]
  have : ¬ Fin.last c < Fin.castSucc j := not_lt.2 (Fin.castSucc_lt_last j).le
  simp only [this, if_false]
  ring

theorem modExp_last {c : ℕ} (n : Fin (c + 1) → ℕ) :
    modExp (fun i => Fin (n i)) (Fin.last c) = -((∑ i : Fin c, n (Fin.castSucc i) : ℕ) : ℤ) := by
  unfold modExp
  simp only [Fintype.card_fin, Finset.sum_filter, Fin.sum_univ_castSucc, Fin.castSucc_lt_last, if_true,
    lt_irrefl, if_false]
  have : ∀ i : Fin c, ¬ Fin.last c < Fin.castSucc i := fun i => not_lt.2 (Fin.castSucc_lt_last i).le
  simp only [this, if_false, Finset.sum_const_zero]
  push_cast
  ring

theorem qdet_blocks : ∀ (c : ℕ) {N : ℕ} (n : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, n i = N) (X : Fin N → Fin N → A),
    GlRel X → ∀ u : ℂ, qdet X (u + ((N : ℂ) - 1) / 2) - (List.ofFn fun i : Fin c =>
      qdet (fun p q : Fin (n i) => X (stdBlocks n h ⟨i, p⟩) (stdBlocks n h ⟨i, q⟩))
        (u + ((n i : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (modExp (fun i => Fin (n i)) i : ℂ) / 2)).prod ∈
      Submodule.span ℂ {y : A | ∃ (p q : Fin N) (w : A),
        blk (stdBlocks n h) p < blk (stdBlocks n h) q ∧ y = X p q * w} := by
  intro c
  induction c with
  | zero =>
    intro N n h X hX u
    have hN : N = 0 := by rw [← h]; simp
    subst hN
    have h1 : qdet X (u + (((0 : ℕ) : ℂ) - 1) / 2) = 1 := by unfold qdet qminor; simp
    rw [h1]
    simp
  | succ c ih =>
    intro N n h X hX u
    set r := ∑ i : Fin c, n (Fin.castSucc i) with hr
    set m := n (Fin.last c) with hm
    have hN : r + m = N := by rw [← h, Fin.sum_univ_castSucc]
    subst hN
    set n' : Fin c → ℕ := fun i => n (Fin.castSucc i) with hn'
    have hr' : ∑ i, n' i = r := rfl
    have hL1 : ∀ (i : Fin c) (p : Fin (n (Fin.castSucc i))),
        stdBlocks n h ⟨Fin.castSucc i, p⟩ = Fin.castAdd m (stdBlocks n' hr' ⟨i, p⟩) := by
      intro i p; apply Fin.ext; rw [stdBlocks_val, Fin.val_castAdd, stdBlocks_val]; rfl
    have hL2 : ∀ q : Fin m, stdBlocks n h ⟨Fin.last c, q⟩ = Fin.natAdd r q := by
      intro q; apply Fin.ext; rw [stdBlocks_val, Fin.val_natAdd]; rfl
    have hblkF : ∀ p : Fin r, blk (stdBlocks n h) (Fin.castAdd m p) =
        Fin.castSucc (blk (stdBlocks n' hr') p) := by
      intro p
      obtain ⟨⟨i, p0⟩, rfl⟩ := (stdBlocks n' hr').surjective p
      rw [← hL1, blk_apply, blk_apply]
    have hblkS : ∀ q : Fin m, blk (stdBlocks n h) (Fin.natAdd r q) = Fin.last c := by
      intro q; rw [← hL2, blk_apply]
    have hS : ∀ y ∈ {y : A | ∃ (p q : Fin (r + m)) (w : A),
        blk (stdBlocks n h) p < blk (stdBlocks n h) q ∧ y = X p q * w}, ∀ a : A,
        y * a ∈ {y : A | ∃ (p q : Fin (r + m)) (w : A),
          blk (stdBlocks n h) p < blk (stdBlocks n h) q ∧ y = X p q * w} := by
      rintro _ ⟨p, q, w, hpq, rfl⟩ a
      exact ⟨p, q, w * a, hpq, mul_assoc _ _ _⟩
    have hJ1 : upperSpan X ≤ Submodule.span ℂ {y : A | ∃ (p q : Fin (r + m)) (w : A),
        blk (stdBlocks n h) p < blk (stdBlocks n h) q ∧ y = X p q * w} := by
      refine Submodule.span_le.2 ?_
      rintro _ ⟨p, q, w, rfl⟩
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨_, _, w, by rw [hblkF, hblkS]; exact Fin.castSucc_lt_last _, rfl⟩
    have hJ2 : Submodule.span ℂ {y : A | ∃ (p q : Fin r) (w : A),
        blk (stdBlocks n' hr') p < blk (stdBlocks n' hr') q ∧
          y = X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.castAdd m q) * w} ≤
        Submodule.span ℂ {y : A | ∃ (p q : Fin (r + m)) (w : A),
          blk (stdBlocks n h) p < blk (stdBlocks n h) q ∧ y = X p q * w} := by
      refine Submodule.span_le.2 ?_
      rintro _ ⟨p, q, w, hpq, rfl⟩
      exact Submodule.subset_span ⟨_, _, w, by
        rw [hblkF, hblkF]; exact Fin.castSucc_lt_castSucc_iff.2 hpq, rfl⟩
    have injC : Function.Injective (Fin.castAdd m : Fin r → Fin (r + m)) := (fun a b h => Fin.ext (by simpa using congrArg Fin.val h))
    have h3 := ih n' hr' (fun p q : Fin r => X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.castAdd m q))
      (glRel_restrict hX _ injC) (u - (m : ℂ) / 2)
    have h2 := two_block X hX (u + (((r + m : ℕ) : ℂ) - 1) / 2)
    rw [(qdet_comm hX _ _).eq] at h2
    have hv : u - (m : ℂ) / 2 + ((r : ℂ) - 1) / 2 = u + (((r + m : ℕ) : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - m := by
      push_cast; ring
    rw [hv] at h3
    rw [List.ofFn_succ', List.prod_concat]
    have hF : (List.ofFn fun i : Fin c =>
        qdet (fun p q : Fin (n (Fin.castSucc i)) =>
          X (stdBlocks n h ⟨Fin.castSucc i, p⟩) (stdBlocks n h ⟨Fin.castSucc i, q⟩))
          (u + ((n (Fin.castSucc i) : ℂ) - 1) / 2 -
            (modExp (fun i => Fin (n i)) (Fin.castSucc i) : ℂ) / 2)) =
        List.ofFn fun i : Fin c =>
          qdet (fun p q : Fin (n' i) =>
            X (Fin.castAdd m (stdBlocks n' hr' ⟨i, p⟩)) (Fin.castAdd m (stdBlocks n' hr' ⟨i, q⟩)))
            (u - (m : ℂ) / 2 + ((n' i : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (modExp (fun i => Fin (n' i)) i : ℂ) / 2) := by
      refine congrArg List.ofFn (funext fun i => ?_)
      simp only [hL1]
      congr 1
      rw [modExp_castSucc]
      push_cast
      ring
    have hSb : qdet (fun p q : Fin m => X (stdBlocks n h ⟨Fin.last c, p⟩) (stdBlocks n h ⟨Fin.last c, q⟩))
        (u + ((m : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (modExp (fun i => Fin (n i)) (Fin.last c) : ℂ) / 2) =
        qdet (fun p q : Fin m => X (Fin.natAdd r p) (Fin.natAdd r q))
          (u + (((r + m : ℕ) : ℂ) - 1) / 2) := by
      simp only [hL2]
      congr 1
      rw [modExp_last, ← hr]
      push_cast
      ring
    rw [hF, hSb]
    refine (show ∀ (a b c d : A), a - c * d = (a - b * d) + (b - c) * d by intros; noncomm_ring)
      _ (qdet (fun p q : Fin r => X (Fin.castAdd m p) (Fin.castAdd m q))
        (u + (((r + m : ℕ) : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - m)) _ _ ▸ ?_
    exact add_mem (hJ1 h2) (span_mul_right_mem hS (hJ2 h3) _)

end GlobalTheory.CapHC

theorem GlobalTheory.CapHC.qdet_blocks_congr : ∀ (A : Type) [Ring A] [Algebra ℂ A] {c N : ℕ} (n : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, n i = N) (X : Fin N → Fin N → A), Formalism.REAL.GlRel X → ∀ u : ℂ, Formalism.REAL.qdet X (u + ((N : ℂ) - 1) / 2) - (List.ofFn fun i : Fin c => Formalism.REAL.qdet (fun p q : Fin (n i) => X (stdBlocks n h ⟨i, p⟩) (stdBlocks n h ⟨i, q⟩)) (u + ((n i : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (modExp (fun i => Fin (n i)) i : ℂ) / 2)).prod ∈ Submodule.span ℂ {y : A | ∃ (p q : Fin N) (w : A), blk (stdBlocks n h) p < blk (stdBlocks n h) q ∧ y = X p q * w} := by
  intro A _ _ c N n h X hX u
  exact GlobalTheory.CapHC.qdet_blocks c n h X hX u

theorem lie_leftInv {N : ℕ} (U : Subgroup (GA E N)) (X : Lie E N) {ψ : Sm E N}
    (hψ : ∀ u ∈ U, ∀ g, ψ.1 (u * g) = ψ.1 g) : ∀ u ∈ U, ∀ g, (lie E N X ψ).1 (u * g) = (lie E N X ψ).1 g := by
  intro u hu g
  have e : chart ψ.1 (u * g) = chart ψ.1 g := by
    funext x
    unfold chart
    split_ifs with hx
    · rw [mul_assoc, hψ u hu]
    · rfl
  simp only [lie_apply, lieD, e]

theorem list_prod_along {ι : Type} {N : ℕ} (ℓ : ι → GA E N) (T : Submodule ℂ (ι → ℂ))
    (l : List (Module.End ℂ (Sm E N) × ℂ))
    (hl : ∀ x ∈ l, ∀ ψ : Sm E N, (fun m => ψ.1 (ℓ m)) ∈ T → ∀ m, (x.1 ψ).1 (ℓ m) = x.2 * ψ.1 (ℓ m)) :
    ∀ ψ : Sm E N, (fun m => ψ.1 (ℓ m)) ∈ T → ∀ m,
      ((l.map Prod.fst).prod ψ).1 (ℓ m) = (l.map Prod.snd).prod * ψ.1 (ℓ m) := by
  induction l with
  | nil => intro ψ _ m; simp
  | cons x l ih =>
    intro ψ hψ m
    have ih' := ih fun y hy => hl y (List.mem_cons_of_mem _ hy)
    have h2 : (fun m => ((l.map Prod.fst).prod ψ).1 (ℓ m)) ∈ T := by
      have : (fun m => ((l.map Prod.fst).prod ψ).1 (ℓ m)) = (l.map Prod.snd).prod • (fun m => ψ.1 (ℓ m)) :=
        funext fun m => by rw [ih' ψ hψ m]; rfl
      rw [this]
      exact T.smul_mem _ hψ
    simp only [List.map_cons, List.prod_cons, Module.End.mul_apply]
    rw [hl x (by simp) _ h2 m, ih' ψ hψ m, mul_assoc]

theorem lie_mem_smoothPart {N : ℕ} (X : Lie E N) {φ : Sm E N} (hφ : φ ∈ GlobalTheory.PRep.smoothPart (finRep E N)) :
    lie E N X φ ∈ GlobalTheory.PRep.smoothPart (finRep E N) := by
  have ho : IsOpen {k | finRep E N k φ = φ} := hφ
  refine isOpen_stab_of_mem_nhds _ _ (Filter.mem_of_superset (ho.mem_nhds (by simp)) fun k hk => ?_)
  have hk' : finRep E N k φ = φ := hk
  change finRep E N k (lie E N X φ) = lie E N X φ
  have e := congrArg (fun z => z φ) (lie_rt_fin X k)
  simp only [Module.End.mul_apply] at e
  change lie E N X (finRep E N k φ) = finRep E N k (lie E N X φ) at e
  rw [← e, hk']

theorem lie_mem_indForms {c N : ℕ} (n : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, n i = N) (π : ∀ i, CuspSub E (n i)) (X : Lie E N)
    (φ : Sm E N) (hφ : φ ∈ indForms h π) : lie E N X φ ∈ indForms h π :=
  ⟨lie_leftInv _ X hφ.1, lie_mem_smoothPart X hφ.2.1, lie_mem_finVec E X φ hφ.2.2.1,
    lie_tensorForms E n h π X φ hφ⟩

theorem cap_indForms {c N : ℕ} (n : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, n i = N) (π : ∀ i, CuspSub E (n i)) (τ : E →+* ℂ)
    (M : Fin c → Multiset ℂ) (hM : ∀ i, (π i).HasInfChar τ (M i)) (u : ℂ) (φ : Sm E N) (hφ : φ ∈ indForms h π) :
    cap τ u φ = (∏ i, ((M i).map fun x => u + x - (modExp (fun i => Fin (n i)) i : ℂ) / 2).prod) • φ := by
  set e := stdBlocks n h with he
  set mE : Fin c → ℂ := fun i => (modExp (fun i => Fin (n i)) i : ℂ) / 2 with hmE
  let S : Subalgebra ℂ (Module.End ℂ (Sm E N)) :=
    Algebra.adjoin ℂ (Set.range fun pq : Fin N × Fin N => Eop τ pq.1 pq.2)
  have hSE : ∀ p q, Eop τ p q ∈ S := fun p q => Algebra.subset_adjoin ⟨(p, q), rfl⟩
  have hSL : S ≤ leftInvAlg (unipRad (Ad E) e) :=
    Algebra.adjoin_le (by rintro _ ⟨pq, rfl⟩; exact Eop_mem_leftInvAlg _ τ pq.1 pq.2)
  let X : Fin N → Fin N → S := fun p q => ⟨Eop τ p q, hSE p q⟩
  have hX : Formalism.REAL.GlRel X := fun a b c' d => Subtype.ext (by
    have := glRel_Eop (a := N) τ a b c' d
    split_ifs at this ⊢ <;> simpa [X] using this)
  have key := qdet_sub_blocks_mem S n h X hX u

  let B : Fin c → Module.End ℂ (Sm E N) := fun i =>
    Formalism.REAL.qdet (fun p q : Fin (n i) => Eop (a := N) τ (e ⟨i, p⟩) (e ⟨i, q⟩)) (u + ((n i : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - mE i)
  let sc : Fin c → ℂ := fun i => ((M i).map fun x => u + x - mE i).prod
  have hval : S.val (List.ofFn fun i : Fin c => Formalism.REAL.qdet (fun p q : Fin (n i) => X (e ⟨i, p⟩) (e ⟨i, q⟩))
      (u + ((n i : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - mE i)).prod = (List.ofFn B).prod := by
    rw [map_list_prod, List.map_ofFn]
    congr 1
    refine List.ofFn_inj.2 (funext fun i => ?_)
    simp only [Function.comp_apply, B]
    rw [map_qdet]
    rfl
  have hcap : S.val (Formalism.REAL.qdet X (u + ((N : ℂ) - 1) / 2)) = cap τ u := by
    rw [map_qdet]
    rfl

  have hlevi : ∀ ψ : Sm E N, (∀ v ∈ unipRad (Ad E) e, ∀ g, ψ.1 (v * g) = ψ.1 g) →
      (fun m => ψ.1 (leviEmb (Ad E) e m)) ∈ tensorForms π → ∀ m,
      (cap τ u ψ).1 (leviEmb (Ad E) e m) = (∏ i, sc i) * ψ.1 (leviEmb (Ad E) e m) := by
    intro ψ hψ hT m
    let L : S →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ :=
      { toFun := fun w => ((w : Module.End ℂ (Sm E N)) ψ).1 (leviEmb (Ad E) e m)
        map_add' := fun _ _ => rfl
        map_smul' := fun _ _ => rfl }
    have hker : Submodule.span ℂ {y : S | ∃ (p q : Fin N) (w : S), blk e p < blk e q ∧ y = X p q * w} ≤
        LinearMap.ker L := by
      refine Submodule.span_le.2 ?_
      rintro _ ⟨p, q, w, hpq, rfl⟩
      change (Eop τ p q ((w : Module.End ℂ (Sm E N)) ψ)).1 (leviEmb (Ad E) e m) = 0
      exact Eop_leviEmb_eq_zero E n h _ (hSL w.2 ψ hψ) τ p q hpq m
    have h1 := hker key
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h1

theorem prod_neg (d : ℕ) (g : ℂ → ℂ) :

theorem infAsc_holds : InfAsc := by
  intro E _ _ _ N a d V π hV hemb τ m hm
  obtain ⟨hs, ν, hν, f, hfm, hfi, hfe⟩ := hemb
  have hm0 : π.HasInfChar τ ((CG.mk π).infChar τ) :=
    hasInfChar_infChar_of_commute E a (fun τ u o => cap_commute_act τ u o) (CG.mk π) τ
  set m₀ := (CG.mk π).infChar τ with hm₀
  refine ⟨m₀, hm0, GlobalTheory.BcSpehProof.hasInfCharGL_unique hV.ne_bot hm ⟨?_, fun u φ hφ => ?_⟩⟩
  · rw [← hs, Multiset.card_sum]
    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => by rw [Multiset.card_map, hm₀, CG.card_infChar]
  have hI : ∀ X, ∀ φ ∈ indForms hs (fun i => π.twist (ν i)), lie E N X φ ∈ indForms hs (fun i => π.twist (ν i)) :=
    fun X φ hφ => lie_mem_indForms _ hs _ X φ hφ
  have hIV : ∀ X, ∀ φ ∈ V, lie E N X φ ∈ V := fun X φ hφ => hV.stable (.lie X) φ hφ
  have hexpo : ∀ i : Fin d, expo (ν i) τ = ((-spehExp d i + a * spehExp d i : ℝ) : ℂ) := fun i =>
    (GlobalTheory.CuspRep.InfType.expo_of_infType E (ν i)).2.1 _ (hν i) τ
  have hmod : ∀ i : Fin d, ((modExp (fun _ : Fin d => Fin a) i : ℤ) : ℂ) / 2 = ((a * spehExp d i : ℝ) : ℂ) := fun i => by
    rw [mul_spehExp]
    push_cast
    rfl
  have hc := cap_indForms (fun _ : Fin d => a) hs (fun i => π.twist (ν i)) τ (fun i => m₀.map (· + expo (ν i) τ))
    (fun i => hm0.twist (ν i)) u (f φ) (hfm φ hφ)
  have e1 : f (cap τ u φ) = cap τ u (f φ) := cap_comm_quot hIV f hfe τ u φ hφ
  set Q := ((∑ i : Fin d, m₀.map (· + (spehExp d i : ℂ))).map (u + ·)).prod with hQ
  have hQ' : (∏ i : Fin d, ((m₀.map (· + expo (ν i) τ)).map fun x =>
      u + x - (modExp (fun _ : Fin d => Fin a) i : ℂ) / 2).prod) = Q := by
    rw [hQ, prod_map_finset_sum]
    have e : ∀ i : Fin d, ((m₀.map (· + expo (ν i) τ)).map fun x =>
        u + x - (modExp (fun _ : Fin d => Fin a) i : ℂ) / 2).prod =
        (m₀.map fun x => u + x + -(spehExp d i : ℂ)).prod := fun i => by
      rw [Multiset.map_map]
      congr 1
      refine Multiset.map_congr rfl fun x _ => ?_
      simp only [Function.comp_apply]
      rw [hexpo i, hmod i]
      push_cast
      ring
    rw [Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => e i, prod_neg d (fun t => (m₀.map fun x => u + x + t).prod)]
    refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
    rw [Multiset.map_map]
    congr 1
    refine Multiset.map_congr rfl fun x _ => ?_
    simp only [Function.comp_apply]
    ring
  have hmem : cap τ u φ - Q • φ ∈ V := by
    rw [hm.2 u φ hφ]
    exact V.sub_mem (V.smul_mem _ hφ) (V.smul_mem _ hφ)
  have h0 : f (cap τ u φ - Q • φ) = 0 := by
    rw [map_sub, e1, hc, map_smul, hQ']
    exact sub_self _
  exact sub_eq_zero.1 (hfi _ hmem h0)

end PositivityTheory.Rg.Rs

def satEig (q n : ℕ) (B : Multiset ℂˣ) (j : ℕ) : ℂ :=
  (q : ℂ) ^ (((j : ℂ) * ((n : ℂ) - j)) / 2) * (B.map fun b : ℂˣ => (b : ℂ)).esymm j

theorem eq_of_satEig_eq {q n : ℕ} (hq : q ≠ 0) {B B' : Multiset ℂˣ} (hB : B.card = n) (hB' : B'.card = n)
    (h : ∀ j ≤ n, satEig q n B j = satEig q n B' j) : B = B' := by
  have h1 : ∀ j ≤ n, (B.map fun b : ℂˣ => (b : ℂ)).esymm j = (B'.map fun b : ℂˣ => (b : ℂ)).esymm j := fun j hj =>
    mul_left_cancel₀ (fun h0 => hq (by exact_mod_cast ((Complex.cpow_eq_zero_iff _ _).1 h0).1)) (h j hj)
  have h2 : ((B.map fun b : ℂˣ => (b : ℂ)).map fun r => Polynomial.X + Polynomial.C r).prod =
      ((B'.map fun b : ℂˣ => (b : ℂ)).map fun r => Polynomial.X + Polynomial.C r).prod := by
    rw [Multiset.prod_X_add_C_eq_sum_esymm, Multiset.prod_X_add_C_eq_sum_esymm, Multiset.card_map, Multiset.card_map,
      hB, hB']
    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun j hj => by rw [h1 j (Nat.lt_succ_iff.1 (Finset.mem_range.1 hj))]
  have h3 : ∀ A : Multiset ℂˣ, ((A.map fun b : ℂˣ => (b : ℂ)).map fun r => Polynomial.X + Polynomial.C r).prod.roots =
      A.map fun b : ℂˣ => -(b : ℂ) := fun A => by
    rw [← Polynomial.roots_multiset_prod_X_sub_C (A.map fun b : ℂˣ => -(b : ℂ))]
    simp [Multiset.map_map]
  have h4 := congrArg Polynomial.roots h2
  rw [h3, h3] at h4
  exact Multiset.map_injective (fun a b e => Units.ext (neg_injective e)) h4

section Linear

variable {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) {n : ℕ}
  {V W : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W]

variable (n) in

abbrev KGL : Subgroup (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) := GLint (Fin n) (Ov E w)

def IsSatVecGL (ρ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) V) (x : V) (B : Multiset ℂˣ) : Prop :=
  x ≠ 0 ∧ x ∈ fixed ρ (KGL w n) ∧ B.card = n ∧
    ∀ ϖ, IsUnifV E w ϖ → ∀ j ≤ n, heckeVec ρ (KGL w n) (Tj ℂ n (Ov E w) ϖ j) x = satEig (qV E w) n B j • x

def HasSatGL (ρ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) V) (B : Multiset ℂˣ) : Prop :=
  GlobalTheory.LGrp.IsSpherical ρ (KGL w n) ∧ ∀ x ∈ fixed ρ (KGL w n), x ≠ 0 → IsSatVecGL w ρ x B

theorem exists_isUnifV : ∃ ϖ, IsUnifV E w ϖ := ⟨_, Formalism.L2.Host.unif_spec w⟩

theorem qV_ne_zero : qV E w ≠ 0 := (one_lt_absNorm w).ne_bot

variable {w}

theorem mem_Ov_of_isUnifV {ϖ : w.adicCompletion E} (h : IsUnifV E w ϖ) : ϖ ∈ Ov E w := by
  change Valued.v ϖ ≤ 1
  rw [h]
  exact (by decide : ((Multiplicative.ofAdd (-1 : ℤ) : Multiplicative ℤ) : WithZero (Multiplicative ℤ)) ≤ 1)

theorem Tj_mem_hecke {ϖ : w.adicCompletion E} (h : IsUnifV E w ϖ) (j : ℕ) :
    Tj ℂ n (Ov E w) ϖ j ∈ Hecke ℂ (KGL w n) :=
  Final.TjMem _ _ ϖ ℂ n j fun h0 => by simp [IsUnifV, h0] at h

variable {ρ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) V} {x : V} {B B' : Multiset ℂˣ}

theorem IsSatVecGL.unique (h : IsSatVecGL w ρ x B) (h' : IsSatVecGL w ρ x B') : B = B' := by
  obtain ⟨ϖ, hϖ⟩ := exists_isUnifV w
  exact eq_of_satEig_eq (qV_ne_zero w) h.2.2.1 h'.2.2.1 fun j hj =>
    smul_left_injective ℂ h.1 ((h.2.2.2 ϖ hϖ j hj).symm.trans (h'.2.2.2 ϖ hϖ j hj))

theorem HasSatGL.exists_vec (h : HasSatGL w ρ B) : ∃ x, IsSatVecGL w ρ x B := by
  obtain ⟨x, hx, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 h.1
  exact ⟨x, h.2 x hx h0⟩

theorem HasSatGL.unique (h : HasSatGL w ρ B) (h' : HasSatGL w ρ B') : B = B' := by
  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := h.exists_vec
  exact hx.unique (h'.2 x hx.2.1 hx.1)

theorem HasSatGL.card (h : HasSatGL w ρ B) : B.card = n := by
  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := h.exists_vec
  exact hx.2.2.1

theorem IsSatVecGL.map (h : IsSatVecGL w ρ x B) {ρ' : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) W}
    (f : V →ₗ[ℂ] W) (hf : ∀ g y, f (ρ g y) = ρ' g (f y)) (h0 : f x ≠ 0) : IsSatVecGL w ρ' (f x) B :=
  ⟨h0, GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.2 fun k hk => by rw [← hf, GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.1 h.2.1 k hk], h.2.2.1, fun ϖ hϖ j hj => by

theorem map_idem_of_fixed (hs : ∀ a, IsSmooth (ρ a)) (hc : ∀ a, IsCompact (U a : Set (Γ a))) {v : ⨂[ℂ] a, X a}
    (hv : ∀ g : ∀ a, Γ a, (∀ a, g a ∈ U a) → piTensor ρ g v = v) :
    PiTensorProduct.map (fun a => idem (hs a) (hc a)) v = v := by
  classical
  have hC : IsCompact (Subgroup.pi Set.univ U : Set (∀ a, Γ a)) := isCompact_univ_pi hc
  have hS := isSmooth_piTensor hs
  have h1 : ∀ (x : ∀ a, X a) (i : α) (y : X i),
      idem hS hC (PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ (Function.update x i (idem (hs i) (hc i) y))) =
        idem hS hC (PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ (Function.update x i y)) := fun x i y => by
    let l : X i →ₗ[ℂ] ⨂[ℂ] a, X a := idem hS hC ∘ₗ (PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ).toLinearMap x i
    have hk : twKer (ρ i) (U i) 1 ≤ LinearMap.ker l := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
      rintro _ ⟨u, z, rfl⟩
      show l _ = 0
      rw [map_sub, MonoidHom.one_apply, Units.val_one, one_smul, sub_eq_zero]
      have hu : Pi.mulSingle i (u : Γ i) ∈ Subgroup.pi Set.univ U := fun a _ => by
        show (Pi.mulSingle i (u : Γ i) : ∀ a, Γ a) a ∈ U a
        by_cases e : a = i
        · subst e; simp
        · rw [Pi.mulSingle_eq_of_ne e]; exact one_mem _
      refine Eq.trans ?_ (idem_apply_mem hS hC hu (PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ (Function.update x i z)))
      rw [piTensor_tprod]
      show idem hS hC (PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ _) = _
      congr 2
      funext a
      by_cases e : a = i
      · subst e; simp
      · simp [e])
    have h2 : l (idem (hs i) (hc i) y - y) = 0 := hk ((idem_eq_zero_iff (hs i) (hc i)).1 (by
      rw [map_sub, idem_of_mem _ _ (idem_mem _ _ _), sub_self]))
    rwa [map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h2
  have h2 : ∀ (x : ∀ a, X a) (s : Finset α),
      idem hS hC (PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ (s.piecewise (fun a => idem (hs a) (hc a) (x a)) x)) =
        idem hS hC (PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ x) := fun x s => by
    induction s using Finset.induction_on with
    | empty => simp
    | insert i s hi ih =>
      rw [Finset.piecewise_insert, h1, ← ih, ← Finset.piecewise_eq_of_notMem s (fun a => idem (hs a) (hc a) (x a)) x hi,
        Function.update_eq_self]
  have h3 : ∀ u, PiTensorProduct.map (fun a => idem (hs a) (hc a)) u = idem hS hC u := fun u => by
    induction u using PiTensorProduct.induction_on with
    | smul_tprod r x =>
      rw [map_smul, map_smul, PiTensorProduct.map_tprod, ← h2 x Finset.univ, Finset.piecewise_univ, idem_of_mem]
      refine GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.2 fun g hg => ?_
      rw [piTensor_tprod]
      congr 1
      funext a
      exact GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.1 (idem_mem _ _ _) _ (hg a trivial)
    | add x y hx hy => rw [map_add, map_add, hx, hy]
  rw [h3, idem_of_mem hS hC (GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.2 fun g hg => hv g fun a => hg a trivial)]

theorem deltaHalf_eq_one {g : Levi k m} (hg : ∀ a, g a ∈ KO k (m a)) : deltaHalf k m g = 1 := by
  rw [deltaHalf_apply]
  exact Finset.prod_eq_one fun a _ => Units.ext (by simp [val_absPow, LeviInt.nabs_det_eq_one (hg a)])

theorem apply_mul_of_fixed {f : IndSp e ρ} (hf : f ∈ fixed (parInd e ρ) (KO k n)) (g : GL n k) {x : GL n k}
    (hx : x ∈ KO k n) : f.1.1 (g * x) = f.1.1 g :=
  congrFun (congrArg (fun φ : IndSp e ρ => φ.1.1) (GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.1 hf x hx)) g

theorem eq_zero_of_apply_one {f : IndSp e ρ} (hf : f ∈ fixed (parInd e ρ) (KO k n)) (h : f.1.1 1 = 0) : f = 0 := by
  refine Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (funext fun g => ?_))
  obtain ⟨p, hp, x, hx, rfl⟩ := (LeviInt.levi_integral_facts k α n m e).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 g
  have := parInd_law e ρ f ⟨p, hp⟩ 1
  rw [mul_one, h, map_zero, smul_zero] at this
  exact (apply_mul_of_fixed e hf p hx).trans this

theorem apply_one_fixed {f : IndSp e ρ} (hf : f ∈ fixed (parInd e ρ) (KO k n)) (g : Levi k m)
    (hg : ∀ a, g a ∈ KO k (m a)) : ρ g (f.1.1 1) = f.1.1 1 := by
  have h1 := parInd_law e ρ f ⟨_, leviEmb_mem_parab k e g⟩ 1
  have h2 := ((LeviInt.levi_integral_facts k α n m e).1 g 1 (one_mem _)).2 ⟨hg, one_mem _⟩
  rw [leviProj_leviEmb, deltaHalf_eq_one hg, Units.val_one, one_smul] at h1
  rw [← h1, ← apply_mul_of_fixed e hf 1 h2, one_mul]

theorem exists_fixed_apply_one {x : W} (hx : ∀ g : Levi k m, (∀ a, g a ∈ KO k (m a)) → ρ g x = x) :
    ∃ f ∈ fixed (parInd e ρ) (KO k n), f.1.1 1 = x := by

    have : (⟨p (q * y), hp _⟩ : parab k e) = q * (q⁻¹ * ⟨p (q * y), hp _⟩) := by group

    rw [e1]
    exact mul_mem hy (inv_mem (hu _))
  have h5 : ∀ y ∈ KO k n, ∀ g, τ ⟨p (g * y), hp _⟩ x = τ ⟨p g, hp _⟩ x := fun y hy g => by
    have := h4 ⟨p g, hp g⟩ (u g * y) (mul_mem (hu g) hy)

    have := h4 (q * ⟨p g, hp g⟩) (u g) (hu g)

theorem dosetInd_eq_Tj {ϖ : w.adicCompletion E} (h0 : ϖ ≠ 0) (hu : IsUnifV E w ϖ) {g : GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)}
    (h1 : IntM g) (h2 : IntM (tor h0 (fun _ => 1) * g⁻¹)) :
    ∃ j ≤ n, dosetInd ℂ (KGL w n) g = Tj ℂ n (Ov E w) ϖ j := by
  obtain ⟨a, ha, c, hc, d, hd, rfl⟩ := cartan h0 hu g
  have a0 : ∀ i, 0 ≤ a i := (intM_tor h0 hu a).1 (intM_doset h1 ⟨c⁻¹, inv_mem hc, d⁻¹, inv_mem hd, by group⟩)
  have a1 : ∀ i, 0 ≤ 1 - a i := (intM_tor h0 hu (fun _ => 1 - a _)).1 (intM_doset h2 ⟨d, hd, c, hc, by
    have e : tor h0 (fun i => 1 - a i) * tor h0 a = tor h0 fun _ => 1 := by
      rw [← tor_add]; congr 1; funext i; simp
    rw [← mul_assoc, ← tor_const_comm, ← e]; group⟩)
  set j := (Finset.univ.filter fun i => 0 < a i).card
  have hj : ∀ i : Fin n, 0 < a i ↔ (i : ℕ) < j := fun i =>
    (Fin.lt_card_filter_univ_iff_apply_of_imp (fun i => 0 < a i) fun x y hxy hx => lt_of_lt_of_le hx (ha hxy)).symm
  refine ⟨j, by simpa using Finset.card_filter_le (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin n)) fun i => 0 < a i, ?_⟩
  rw [dosetInd_congr ⟨c, hc, d, hd, rfl⟩, Tj_eq h0]
  congr 2
  funext i
  have := hj i
  have := a0 i
  have := a1 i
  split_ifs <;> omega

theorem intM_of_Tj {ϖ : w.adicCompletion E} (h0 : ϖ ≠ 0) (hu : IsUnifV E w ϖ) {g : GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)}
    {j : ℕ} (h : Tj ℂ n (Ov E w) ϖ j g ≠ 0) : IntM g ∧ IntM (tor h0 (fun _ => 1) * g⁻¹) := by
  rw [Tj_eq h0] at h

  refine ⟨intM_doset ((intM_tor h0 hu _).2 fun i => by split_ifs <;> simp) ⟨c, hc, d, hd, rfl⟩,
    intM_doset ((intM_tor h0 hu fun i => if (i : ℕ) < j then 0 else 1).2 fun i => by split_ifs <;> simp)
      ⟨d⁻¹, inv_mem hd, c⁻¹, inv_mem hc, ?_⟩⟩
  have e : (tor h0 fun i : Fin n => if (i : ℕ) < j then 0 else 1) * (tor h0 fun i => if (i : ℕ) < j then 1 else 0) =
      tor h0 fun _ => 1 := by

  rw [mul_inv_rev, mul_inv_rev, ← mul_assoc, tor_const_comm, ← e]; group

    b ∈ parab (w.adicCompletion E) (stdBlocks N h) := by

      exact hb.elim (fun e => e ▸ hxy) fun e => e.trans hxy

theorem chiDelta_eq {β : ∀ a, Fin (N a) → ℂ} {γ : Fin n → ℂ} (hγ : ∀ a i, γ (stdBlocks N h ⟨a, i⟩) = β a i)
    {b : parab (w.adicCompletion E) (stdBlocks N h)} (hb : IsUpperB E w b.1) :
    chiDelta E w γ b.1 = (deltaHalf _ _ (leviProj _ _ b) : ℂ) * ∏ a, chiDelta E w (β a) (leviProj _ _ b a) := by
  rw [chiDelta, ← (stdBlocks N h).prod_comp, Fintype.prod_sigma, deltaHalf_apply, Units.coe_prod,
    ← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun a _ => ?_
  have hd : (leviProj _ _ b a).val.det = ∏ i, (leviProj _ _ b a).val i i :=
    Matrix.det_of_isUpperTriangular fun i j hij => isUpperB_leviProj h hb a i j hij
  have h0 : ∀ i, (leviProj _ _ b a).val i i ≠ 0 := fun i e =>
    (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det (leviProj _ _ b a)).ne_zero (hd.trans (Finset.prod_eq_zero (Finset.mem_univ i) e))
  have hd' : Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det (leviProj _ _ b a) = ∏ i, Units.mk0 _ (h0 i) :=
    Units.ext (by rw [Units.coe_prod]; exact hd)
  rw [hd', map_prod, Units.coe_prod, chiDelta, ← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
  have hq : (qV E w : ℂ) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 (qV_ne_zero w)
  rw [val_absPow_completion, hγ, mul_left_comm, ← Complex.cpow_add _ _ hq, val_stdBlocks]
  have hn : n = ∑ b with b < a, N b + N a + ∑ b with a < b, N b := by
    have : ∀ b, N b = (if b < a then N b else 0) + (if b = a then N b else 0) + if a < b then N b else 0 := fun b => by
      rcases lt_trichotomy b a with e | e | e
      · simp [e, e.ne, e.not_gt]
      · simp [e]
      · simp [e, e.ne', e.not_gt]
    rw [← h, Finset.sum_congr rfl fun b _ => this b, Finset.sum_add_distrib, Finset.sum_add_distrib,
      Finset.sum_filter, Finset.sum_filter]
    simp
  have hn' := congrArg (Nat.cast : ℕ → ℂ) hn
  simp only [modExp, Fintype.card_fin, Units.val_mk0, leviProj_apply]
  push_cast at hn' ⊢
  rw [hn']
  congr 2
  ring

omit h in

theorem exists_model {S : Multiset ℂˣ} (hS : S.card = n) :
    ∃ (X : Type) (_ : AddCommGroup X) (_ : Module ℂ X) (R : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) X)
      (β : Fin n → ℂˣ) (l : X →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ) (y : X), IsSmooth R ∧ Finset.univ.val.map β = S ∧ IsSatVecGL w R y S ∧ l y = 1 ∧
        ∀ g, IsUpperB E w g → ∀ z, l (R g z) = chiDelta E w (fun i => (β i : ℂ)) g * l z := by
  obtain ⟨β, rfl⟩ : ∃ β : Fin n → ℂˣ, Finset.univ.val.map β = S := by
    obtain ⟨l, rfl⟩ := Quot.exists_rep S
    obtain rfl : l.length = n := hS
    exact ⟨l.get, by rw [Fin.univ_val_map, List.ofFn_get]; rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, hφ1, -⟩ := Final.SphericalExists E w n (fun i => (β i : ℂ)) fun i => (β i).ne_zero
  obtain ⟨y, -, rfl⟩ := (SphCompletion.isSpherical_iff E w n β φ).1 hφ
  refine ⟨_, _, _, prinSeries fun i => unrChar (w.adicCompletion E) (β i), β,
    LinearMap.proj 1 ∘ₗ Submodule.subtype _ ∘ₗ Submodule.subtype _, y, isSmooth_parInd _ _, rfl, ?_, hφ1,
    fun g hg z => ?_⟩
  · exact IsSatVecGL.of_map (ρ' := rightReg ℂ _ ℂ) ((Submodule.subtype _).comp (Submodule.subtype _))
      (fun _ _ => rfl) (fun u v e => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext e))
      (isSatVecGL_of_isSpherical hφ fun e => one_ne_zero (hφ1.symm.trans (congrFun e 1)))
  · have := (parInd_law _ _ z ⟨g, (mem_borel _).2 hg⟩ 1).trans
      (SphCompletion.law_completion w n β ⟨g, (mem_borel _).2 hg⟩ _)
    rw [mul_one] at this
    exact (congrArg z.1.1 (one_mul _)).trans this

theorem exists_map_isSatVecGL {X : Fin c → Type} [∀ a, AddCommGroup (X a)] [∀ a, Module ℂ (X a)]
    {R : ∀ a, Representation ℂ (GL (Fin (N a)) (w.adicCompletion E)) (X a)} {β : ∀ a, Fin (N a) → ℂˣ}
    {l : ∀ a, X a →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ} {y : ∀ a, X a} (h1 : ∀ a, l a (y a) = 1)
    (hl : ∀ a g, IsUpperB E w g → ∀ z, l a (R a g z) = chiDelta E w (fun i => (β a i : ℂ)) g * l a z)
    {F : IndSp (stdBlocks N h) (piTensor R)} (hF : F ∈ fixed (multiProd (stdBlocks N h) R) (KO _ (Fin n)))
    (hF1 : F.1.1 1 = PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ y) :
    ∃ Y : IndSp (stdBlocks N h) (piTensor R) →ₗ[ℂ] (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E) → ℂ),
      (∀ g z, Y (multiProd (stdBlocks N h) R g z) = rightReg ℂ _ ℂ g (Y z)) ∧
        IsSatVecGL w (rightReg ℂ _ ℂ) (Y F) (∑ a, Finset.univ.val.map (β a)) := by
  obtain ⟨ev, hev⟩ : ∃ ev : (⨂[ℂ] a, X a) →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ, ∀ u, ev (PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ u) = ∏ a, l a (u a) :=
    ⟨PiTensorProduct.lift ((MultilinearMap.mkPiAlgebra ℂ (Fin c) ℂ).compLinearMap l), fun u => by simp⟩
  have hlaw : ∀ g : ∀ a, GL (Fin (N a)) (w.adicCompletion E), (∀ a, IsUpperB E w (g a)) → ∀ u,
      ev (piTensor R g u) = (∏ a, chiDelta E w (fun i => (β a i : ℂ)) (g a)) * ev u := fun g hg u => by
    induction u using PiTensorProduct.induction_on with
    | smul_tprod t z =>
      rw [map_smul, map_smul, map_smul, piTensor_tprod, hev, hev, smul_eq_mul, smul_eq_mul, mul_left_comm,
        ← Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_congr rfl fun a _ => hl a _ (hg a) _]
    | add z z' hz hz' => rw [map_add, map_add, map_add, hz, hz', mul_add]
  let γ : Fin n → ℂˣ := (fun s : Σ a, Fin (N a) => β s.1 s.2) ∘ (stdBlocks N h).symm
  have hγ : Finset.univ.val.map γ = ∑ a, Finset.univ.val.map (β a) := by
    rw [← Multiset.map_map, Multiset.map_univ_val_equiv, ← Finset.univ_sigma_univ]
    simp only [Finset.sigma, Multiset.sigma, Multiset.map_bind, Multiset.map_map]
    rfl
  let Y : IndSp (stdBlocks N h) (piTensor R) →ₗ[ℂ] (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E) → ℂ) :=
    ev.compLeft _ ∘ₗ Submodule.subtype _ ∘ₗ Submodule.subtype _
  have hY1 : Y F 1 = 1 := by
    show ev (F.1.1 1) = 1
    rw [hF1, hev]
    exact Finset.prod_eq_one fun a _ => h1 a
  refine ⟨Y, fun _ _ => rfl, ?_⟩
  rw [← hγ]
  refine isSatVecGL_of_isSpherical (fun b g u hb hu => ?_) fun e => one_ne_zero (hY1.symm.trans (congrFun e 1))
  show ev (F.1.1 (b * g * u)) = _ * ev (F.1.1 g)
  rw [← SphCompletion.KO_completion w n] at hu
  rw [apply_mul_of_fixed _ hF _ hu, parInd_law _ _ F ⟨b, mem_parab_of_isUpperB h hb⟩ g, map_smul,
    hlaw _ (isUpperB_leviProj h hb), smul_eq_mul, ← mul_assoc,
    ← chiDelta_eq h (β := fun a i => (β a i : ℂ)) (γ := fun i => (γ i : ℂ)) (b := ⟨b, mem_parab_of_isUpperB h hb⟩)
      (fun a i => congrArg (fun s : Σ a, Fin (N a) => (β s.1 s.2 : ℂ)) ((stdBlocks N h).symm_apply_apply ⟨a, i⟩)) hb]

theorem exists_sat_multiProd {X : Fin c → Type} [∀ a, AddCommGroup (X a)] [∀ a, Module ℂ (X a)]
    {π : ∀ a, Representation ℂ (GL (Fin (N a)) (w.adicCompletion E)) (X a)} (hs : ∀ a, IsSmooth (π a))
    (hi : ∀ a, (π a).IsIrreducible) {f : IndSp (stdBlocks N h) (piTensor π)}
    (hf : f ∈ fixed (multiProd (stdBlocks N h) π) (KGL w n)) (h0 : f ≠ 0) :
    ∃ S : Fin c → Multiset ℂˣ, (∀ a, HasSatGL w (π a) (S a)) ∧
      IsSatVecGL w (multiProd (stdBlocks N h) π) f (∑ a, S a) := by
  have hKO : ∀ m, KGL w m = KO (w.adicCompletion E) (Fin m) := fun m => (SphCompletion.KO_completion w m).symm
  have hf' := hf
  rw [hKO] at hf'
  have hv := map_idem_of_fixed hs (fun _ => GlobalTheory.SplitFlip.isCompact_KO) (apply_one_fixed _ hf')
  have hsph : ∀ a, GlobalTheory.LGrp.IsSpherical (π a) (KGL w (N a)) := fun a e => by
    rw [hKO] at e
    refine h0 (eq_zero_of_apply_one _ hf' (hv.symm.trans ?_))
    generalize f.1.1 1 = u
    induction u using PiTensorProduct.induction_on with
    | smul_tprod r x =>
      rw [map_smul, PiTensorProduct.map_tprod, MultilinearMap.map_coord_zero _ a, smul_zero]
      have := idem_mem (hs a) GlobalTheory.SplitFlip.isCompact_KO (x a)
      rwa [e] at this
    | add x y hx hy => rw [map_add, hx, hy, add_zero]
  choose S hS using fun a => exists_hasSatGL_of_isIrreducible (hs a) (hi a) (hsph a)
  choose x hx using fun a => (hS a).exists_vec
  have hr : ∀ u, ∃ r : ℂ, PiTensorProduct.map (fun a => idem (hs a) (H := KO _ _) GlobalTheory.SplitFlip.isCompact_KO) u =
      r • PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ x := fun u => by
    induction u using PiTensorProduct.induction_on with
    | smul_tprod r y =>
      choose t ht using fun a => exists_idem_eq_smul (hs a) (hi a) GlobalTheory.SplitFlip.isCompact_KO
        (hKO _ ▸ Final.GLintComm E w (N a)) (hKO _ ▸ exists_scalar_GL (hs a) (hi a)) (hKO _ ▸ (hx a).2.1) (hx a).1 (y a)
      exact ⟨r * ∏ a, t a, by
        rw [map_smul, PiTensorProduct.map_tprod, funext ht, MultilinearMap.map_smul_univ, mul_smul]⟩
    | add y z hy hz =>
      obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := hy
      obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hz
      exact ⟨r + t, by rw [map_add, hr, ht, add_smul]⟩
  obtain ⟨r, hr⟩ := hr (f.1.1 1)
  rw [hv] at hr
  choose Z iZ mZ R β l y hR hβ hyS h1 hl using fun a => exists_model (w := w) (hS a).card
  have hy : ∀ a, y a ∈ fixed (R a) (KO _ _) := fun a => hKO _ ▸ (hyS a).2.1
  obtain ⟨F, hF, hF1⟩ := exists_fixed_apply_one (stdBlocks N h) (ρ := piTensor R) (x := PiTensorProduct.tprod ℂ y)
    fun g hg => by
      rw [piTensor_tprod]
      congr 1
      exact funext fun a => GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.1 (hy a) _ (hg a)
  obtain ⟨Y, hYe, hY⟩ := exists_map_isSatVecGL h h1 hl hF hF1
  rw [funext hβ] at hY
  refine ⟨S, hS, h0, hf, hY.2.2.1, fun ϖ hϖ j hj => ?_⟩
  have hϖ0 : ϖ ≠ 0 := fun e => by simp [IsUnifV, e] at hϖ
  have hT := Tj_mem_hecke (n := n) hϖ j
  have hK : IsCommensurated (KGL w n) := Final.GLintComm E w n
  have e1 := (map_heckeVec (ρ := multiProd (stdBlocks N h) R) hK hT Y hYe F).trans (hY.2.2.2 ϖ hϖ j hj)
  rw [hKO] at hT hK e1 ⊢
  have hp : ∀ q : GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E) ⧸ KO (w.adicCompletion E) (Fin n),
      ∃ p : parab (w.adicCompletion E) (stdBlocks N h), ((p : GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) : _ ⧸ KO _ _) = q :=
    fun q => by
      obtain ⟨p, hp, u, hu, e⟩ := (LeviInt.levi_integral_facts _ _ _ _ (stdBlocks N h)).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 q.out
      exact ⟨⟨p, hp⟩, (QuotientGroup.eq.2 (by simpa [e] using hu)).trans q.out_eq'⟩
  choose p hp using hp
  have hω : ∀ q a, ∃ t : ℂ, Tj ℂ n (Ov E w) ϖ j (p q) ≠ 0 →
      idem (hs a) GlobalTheory.SplitFlip.isCompact_KO (π a (leviProj _ _ (p q) a) (x a)) = t • x a ∧
      idem (hR a) GlobalTheory.SplitFlip.isCompact_KO (R a (leviProj _ _ (p q) a) (y a)) = t • y a := fun q a => by
    by_cases hq : Tj ℂ n (Ov E w) ϖ j (p q) = 0
    · exact ⟨0, fun e => absurd hq e⟩
    obtain ⟨i1, i2⟩ := intM_of_Tj hϖ0 hϖ hq
    obtain ⟨l, hl, e⟩ := dosetInd_eq_Tj hϖ0 hϖ (g := leviProj _ _ (p q) a) (fun s t => i1 _ _) fun s t => by
      have := i2 (stdBlocks N h ⟨a, s⟩) (stdBlocks N h ⟨a, t⟩)
      rw [← Pi.inv_apply, ← map_inv]
      rw [Units.val_mul, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_mul] at this ⊢
      exact this
    exact ⟨_, fun _ => ⟨idem_of_isSatVecGL (hs a) (hx a) hϖ hl e, idem_of_isSatVecGL (hR a) (hyS a) hϖ hl e⟩⟩
  choose ω hω using hω
  rw [heckeVec_multiProd _ hK hT hR hF (hF1.trans (one_smul ℂ _).symm) p hp ω (fun q hq a => (hω q a hq).2),
    map_smul] at e1

theorem absPow_det_of_mem (s : ℂ) {g : GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)} (hg : g ∈ KGL w n) :
    absPow (w.adicCompletion E) s (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det g) = 1 :=
  Units.ext (by
    rw [KGL, ← SphCompletion.KO_completion w n] at hg
    simp [val_absPow, LeviInt.nabs_det_eq_one hg])

theorem mem_fixed_twist (T : (twist ρ (detChar (Fin n) (absPow _ s))).Equiv ρ') (x : V) :
    T x ∈ fixed ρ' (KGL w n) ↔ x ∈ fixed ρ (KGL w n) := by
  simp only [GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed]
  refine forall₂_congr fun g hg => ?_
  have := T.toIntertwiningMap.isIntertwining _ _ g x
  rw [twist_apply, detChar_apply, absPow_det_of_mem s hg, Units.val_one, one_smul] at this
  exact (Eq.congr_left this.symm).trans T.toLinearEquiv.injective.eq_iff

theorem hasSatGL_of_twist (T : (twist ρ (detChar (Fin n) (absPow _ s))).Equiv ρ') {B : Multiset ℂˣ}
    (hB : HasSatGL w ρ B) {z : ℂˣ} (hz : (z : ℂ) = (qV E w : ℂ) ^ (-s)) : HasSatGL w ρ' (B.map (· * z)) := by
  constructor
  · obtain ⟨x, hx, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 hB.1
    exact (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨T x, (mem_fixed_twist T x).2 hx,
      (map_ne_zero_iff T.toLinearEquiv T.toLinearEquiv.injective).2 h0⟩
  · intro y hy h0
    obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := T.toLinearEquiv.surjective y
    have hx := hB.2 x ((mem_fixed_twist T x).1 hy) ((map_ne_zero_iff _ T.toLinearEquiv.injective).1 h0)
    refine ⟨h0, hy, by rw [Multiset.card_map, hx.2.2.1], fun ϖ hϖ j hj => ?_⟩
    rw [GlobalTheory.SphGal.satEig_map_mul, mul_smul, ← map_smul T.toLinearEquiv, ← hx.2.2.2 ϖ hϖ j hj, heckeVec, heckeVec]
    have e := T.toLinearEquiv.toAddEquiv.map_finsum
      (fun c : _ ⧸ KGL w n => Tj ℂ n (Ov E w) ϖ j c.out • ρ c.out x)
    rw [show T.toLinearEquiv _ = _ from e, smul_finsum]
    refine finsum_congr fun c => ?_
    by_cases hc : Tj ℂ n (Ov E w) ϖ j c.out = 0
    · simp [hc]
    obtain ⟨a, ha, b, hb, e⟩ := Set.mem_of_indicator_ne_zero hc
    have hϖ0 : ϖ ≠ 0 := fun h0 => by simp [IsUnifV, h0] at hϖ
    have e2 : Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det c.out =
        Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det a * Units.mk0 ϖ hϖ0 ^ j * Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det b := by
      apply Units.ext
      change c.out.val.det = a.val.det * _ * b.val.det
      rw [e, Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_mul, GlobalTheory.SphGal.det_diagJ ϖ hj]
      simp
    have e3 : absPow (w.adicCompletion E) s (Units.mk0 ϖ hϖ0) = z := Units.ext (by
      have : ordV E w ϖ = 1 := by
        rw [GlobalTheory.LGrp.BorelCompletion.ordV_eq, show Valued.v ϖ = _ from hϖ]; rfl
      rw [val_absPow_completion, hz, Units.val_mk0, this]
      simp)
    have := T.toIntertwiningMap.isIntertwining _ _ c.out x
    rw [twist_apply, detChar_apply, e2, map_mul, map_mul, absPow_det_of_mem s ha, absPow_det_of_mem s hb, map_pow, e3,
      one_mul, mul_one, map_smul] at this
    refine (congrArg (Tj ℂ n (Ov E w) ϖ j c.out • ·) this.symm).trans ?_
    rw [smul_comm, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val]
    congr 1
    exact (map_smul T.toLinearEquiv _ _).symm

end Twist

theorem exists_sat_of_isSpeh {a d : ℕ} (hd : 1 ≤ d) {ρ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin a) (w.adicCompletion E)) V}
    (hs : IsSmooth ρ) (hi : ρ.IsIrreducible) {σ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E)) W}
    (hσ : IsSpeh (IrrGL.of ρ hs hi) d σ) {B : Multiset ℂˣ} (hB : HasSatGL w σ B) :
    ∃ S, HasSatGL w ρ S ∧ ∑ k : Fin d, S.map (fun z : ℂˣ => (z : ℂ) * (qV E w : ℂ) ^ (((d : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (k : ℕ))) =
      B.map (fun z : ℂˣ => (z : ℂ)) := by
  obtain ⟨hn, -, p, hp, -⟩ := hσ
  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := hB.exists_vec
  have hx' := hx.2.1
  rw [← map_fixed_of_surjective (isSmooth_prodIrr _ hn) isCompact_KGL hp] at hx'
  obtain ⟨f, hf, rfl⟩ := hx'
  obtain ⟨R, hR, hfR⟩ := exists_sat_multiProd hn (fun k => (spehFactor (IrrGL.of ρ hs hi) d k).2.rep.smooth)
    (fun k => (spehFactor (IrrGL.of ρ hs hi) d k).2.rep.irr) hf fun e => hx.1 (by rw [e, map_zero])
  obtain rfl := hx.unique (hfR.map p.toLinearMap (fun g y => p.isIntertwining _ _ g y) hx.1)
  let T : ∀ k : Fin d, (twist ρ (detChar (Fin a) (absPow _ (((d : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (k : ℕ))))).Equiv
      (spehFactor (IrrGL.of ρ hs hi) d k).rep := fun k =>
    (twistEquiv (IrrGL.equivOf ρ hs hi) _).trans ((IrrGL.of ρ hs hi).twEquiv _ (isSmoothChar_absPow _))
  obtain ⟨S, hS⟩ := exists_hasSatGL_of_isIrreducible hs hi (by
    obtain ⟨y, hy, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 (hR ⟨0, hd⟩).1
    obtain ⟨y, rfl⟩ := (T ⟨0, hd⟩).toLinearEquiv.surjective y
    exact (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨y, (mem_fixed_twist (T ⟨0, hd⟩) y).1 hy, fun e => h0 (by rw [e, map_zero])⟩)
  have hq : ∀ t : ℂ, (qV E w : ℂ) ^ t ≠ 0 := fun t h0 =>
    qV_ne_zero w (by exact_mod_cast ((Complex.cpow_eq_zero_iff _ _).1 h0).1)
  refine ⟨S, hS, ?_⟩
  refine Eq.trans ?_ (map_sum (Multiset.mapAddMonoidHom _) _ _).symm
  rw [← Equiv.sum_comp Fin.revPerm]
  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun k _ => ?_
  rw [(hR k).unique (hasSatGL_of_twist (T k) hS (z := Units.mk0 _ (hq _)) rfl), Multiset.coe_mapAddMonoidHom, Multiset.map_map]
  refine Multiset.map_congr rfl fun z _ => ?_
  have hk : ((k.rev : ℕ) : ℂ) = d - (k + 1) := by
    rw [Fin.val_rev, Nat.cast_sub (Nat.succ_le_of_lt k.2)]
    push_cast
    rfl
  simp only [Function.comp_apply, Units.val_mul, Units.val_mk0, Fin.revPerm_apply, hk]
  congr 2
  ring

end Completion

attribute [local instance] GlobalTheory.LocalData.complRel GlobalTheory.LocalData.isLocalField

theorem satake_locProd_speh : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (c n : ℕ) (a d : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, d i * a i = n) (ρ : ∀ i, LocIrr w (a i)) (σ : ∀ i, LocIrr w (d i * a i)), (∀ i, 1 ≤ d i) → (∀ i, GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsSpeh (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of (ρ i).ρ (ρ i).smooth (ρ i).irred) (d i) (σ i).ρ) → ∀ T : Multiset ℂˣ, GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w (locProd h w σ) T → ∃ S : Fin c → Multiset ℂˣ, (∀ i, GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w (ρ i).ρ (S i)) ∧ ∑ i, ∑ k : Fin (d i), (S i).map (fun z => (z : ℂ) * ((Ideal.absNorm w.asIdeal : ℕ) : ℂ) ^ (((d i : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (k : ℕ))) = T.map (fun z => (z : ℂ)) := by
  intro E _ _ w c n a d h ρ σ hd hσ T hT
  obtain ⟨f, hf⟩ := hT.exists_vec
  obtain ⟨B, hB, hfB⟩ := exists_sat_multiProd h (fun i => (σ i).smooth) (fun i => (σ i).irred) hf.2.1 hf.1
  obtain rfl := hf.unique hfB
  choose S hS hS' using fun i => exists_sat_of_isSpeh (hd i) (ρ i).smooth (ρ i).irred (hσ i) (hB i)
  refine ⟨S, hS, ?_⟩
  simp only [Multiset.bind_def, Multiset.pure_def, Multiset.bind_singleton, Multiset.map_map, Function.comp_def]
  exact (Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => hS' i).trans (map_sum (Multiset.mapAddMonoidHom _) _ _).symm

end GlobalTheory.SatInd

def HasWeightAt (π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ)) (τ : E →+* ℂ) (lam : DomWeight n) : Prop :=
  IsCompAlong π (embU n τ) ((cEmb τ).comp (embU n τ).subtype) (GlobalTheory.Branching.sqRep ℂ n lam)

def infOfWeight (lam : DomWeight n) : Multiset ℂ :=
  Finset.univ.val.map fun k : Fin n => (lam.w k : ℂ) + (((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (k : ℕ))

theorem hasWeightAt_unique (U : AutSub E n) (τ : E →+* ℂ) {lam lam' : DomWeight n} (h : HasWeightAt U.V τ lam)
    (h' : HasWeightAt U.V τ lam') : lam = lam' := by
  obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.archimedeanUnitaryPoints_equiv_unitaryGroup E n τ
  obtain ⟨r, -, hr1, hr⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_continuous_retraction_onto_unitaryArchimedeanPoints E n τ
  let s : Matrix.unitaryGroup (Fin n) ℂ →* UA E n := (embU n τ).subtype.comp e.symm.toMulEquiv.toMonoidHom
  have mk : ∀ mu, HasWeightAt U.V τ mu →
      ∃ f : (smallRep (unitaryIncl n) mu).IntertwiningMap (U.rep.comp s), f ≠ 0 := by
    rintro mu ⟨-, F, hF0, hFm, hFi⟩
    refine ⟨⟨F.codRestrict U.V hFm, fun u => ?_⟩, fun h0 => hF0 ?_⟩
    · ext v : 1
      apply Subtype.ext
      show F (sqRep ℂ n mu (unitaryIncl n u) v) = RU E n (s u) (F v)
      have : unitaryIncl n u = ((cEmb τ).comp (embU n τ).subtype) (e.symm u) := by
        have h3 := he (e.symm u)
        rw [e.apply_symm_apply] at h3
        exact h3
      rw [this, hFi]
      rfl
    · ext v : 1
      exact congrArg Subtype.val (DFunLike.congr_fun h0 v :)
  obtain ⟨f, hf⟩ := mk lam h

theorem isUnitarisable_of_isLocComp (H : AscComp) {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E]
    [IsCMField E] {a : ℕ} (ha : 1 ≤ a) (P : CuspSub E a) (hP : GlobalTheory.DiscSpec.IsNormalised P)
    (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (ρ : LocIrr w a) (hρ : P.IsLocComp w ρ) : IsUnitarisable ρ.ρ := by
  obtain ⟨V, hV, hS⟩ := (H E a ha).1 a P rfl hP
  have hc := (H E a ha).2 V hV a 1 P hP hS
  obtain ⟨-, σ', -, hσ'⟩ := hc.exLoc w
  obtain ⟨hd, h1⟩ := hc.loc w ρ σ' hρ hσ'
  obtain ⟨hd', h2⟩ := GlobalTheory.LocGL.isSpeh_one (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of ρ.ρ ρ.smooth ρ.irred)
  obtain ⟨e⟩ := h1.unique h2
  have hu := GlobalTheory.T.discreteAutomorphicRepGL_localComponent_isUnitarisable E a V hV w σ' hσ'
  exact isUnitarisable_of_equiv' ((GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.equivOf ρ.ρ ρ.smooth ρ.irred).trans e).symm hu

theorem baseChange_speh_of_discreteSeries (H : AscComp)
    (hW : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n →
      ∀ π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ), IsAutRepU π → ∀ τ : E →+* ℂ, ∃ lam : Formalism.BasicNotions.DomWeight n, HasWeightAt π τ lam)
    {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] {n : ℕ} (hn : 1 ≤ n)
    {π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ)} (hπ : IsAutRepU π) {c : ℕ} {N : Fin c → ℕ} {h : ∑ i, N i = n}
    {V : ∀ i, Submodule ℂ (Sm E (N i))} {σ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), SmIrr (GL (Fin (N i)) (w.adicCompletion E))}
    {m : Fin c → (E →+* ℂ) → Multiset ℂ} (bc : IsBaseChangeU π h V σ m) (a d : Fin c → ℕ) (P : ∀ i, CuspSub E (a i))
    (hPn : ∀ i, GlobalTheory.DiscSpec.IsNormalised (P i)) (hPs : ∀ i, AscEmb (V i) (P i) (d i))
    (hcsd : ∀ i, (P i).IsCsd)
    (hNad : ∀ i, N i = d i * a i) :
    ∃ (c : ℕ) (a d : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, d i * a i = n) (P : ∀ i, CuspSub E (a i)) (ρ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), LocIrr w (a i))
        (m : Fin c → (E →+* ℂ) → Multiset ℂ) (σ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), LocIrr w (d i * a i)), Parts π a d h P ρ m ∧
        (∀ i w, IsUnitarisable (ρ i w).ρ ∧ IsUnitarisable (σ i w).ρ ∧ GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsSpeh (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of (ρ i w).ρ (ρ i w).smooth (ρ i w).irred) (d i) (σ i w).ρ) ∧
        ∀ w, IsSplitAt w → IsCompAlong π (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) (locProd h w (σ · w)) := by
  have hN : ∀ i, 1 ≤ N i := bc.pos
  have hc : ∀ i, GlobalTheory.DiscSpec.HasSpehComp (V i) (P i) (d i) := fun i =>
    (H E (N i) (hN i)).2 (V i) (bc.discrete i) (a i) (d i) (P i) (hPn i) (hPs i)
  choose ρ σ' hρ hσ' using fun i w => (hc i).exLoc w
  choose m' hm1 hm2 using fun i τ => (hc i).inf τ
  have hmm : ∀ i τ, m i τ = GlobalTheory.DefiniteBC.spehInf (d i) (m' i τ) := fun i τ =>
    (hasInfCharGL_unique (bc.discrete i).ne_bot (bc.inf i τ) (hm2 i τ)).trans (spehInf_eq _ _)
  have hpos : ∀ i, 1 ≤ a i ∧ 1 ≤ d i := fun i => by
    have := hN i
    rw [hNad i] at this
    exact ⟨Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.2 fun e => by simp [e] at this, Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.2 fun e => by simp [e] at this⟩
  have hsp : ∀ i w, GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsSpeh (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of (ρ i w).ρ (ρ i w).smooth (ρ i w).irred) (d i) (σ i w).ρ :=
    fun i w => (hc i).loc w (ρ i w) (σ i w) (hρ i w) (bc.loc i w)
  have hun : ∀ i w, IsUnitarisable (ρ i w).ρ ∧ IsUnitarisable (σ i w).ρ := fun i w =>
    ⟨isUnitarisable_of_isLocComp H (hpos i).1 (P i) (hPn i) w (ρ i w) (hρ i w),
      GlobalTheory.T.discreteAutomorphicRepGL_localComponent_isUnitarisable E (N i) (V i) (bc.discrete i) w (σ i w) (bc.loc i w)⟩
  have hm0 : ∀ i τ, m i τ ≠ 0 := fun i τ h0 => by
    have h1 := (bc.inf i τ).1
    rw [h0, Multiset.card_zero] at h1
    have := hN i
    omega
  obtain rfl : N = fun i => d i * a i := funext hNad
  refine ⟨c, a, d, h, P, ρ, m', σ, ⟨hpos, hcsd, hρ, hm1, fun τ lam hl => ?_, fun i j hd hs => ?_, ?_⟩,
    fun i w => ⟨(hun i w).1, (hun i w).2, hsp i w⟩, bc.split⟩
  · rw [← bc.weight τ lam hl]
    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => (hmm i τ).symm
  · obtain ⟨τ⟩ : Nonempty (E →+* ℂ) := inferInstance
    obtain ⟨lam, hl⟩ := hW E n hn π hπ τ
    refine GlobalTheory.eq_of_nodup_sum_multiset_of_eq (Fin c) (m · τ) (bc.weight τ lam hl ▸ nodup_infOfWeight lam) i j
      (hm0 i τ) ?_
    rw [hmm i τ, hmm j τ, hd, hasInfChar_eq_of_sigma hs (hm1 i τ) (hm1 j τ)]
  · intro w s ϖ q hs hU α hα
    exact GlobalTheory.T.satakeParameter_spherical_product_spehQuotients E w c n a d h (ρ · w) (σ · w) (fun i => (hpos i).2) (fun i => hsp i w) _
      (bc.inert w s ϖ q hs hU α hα)

theorem bcSpeh_of (H2 : AscComp) (H1 : DefiniteN) : BcSpeh := by
  intro E _ _ _ hE n hn π hπ
  obtain ⟨c, N, h, V, σ, m, bc⟩ := H1 E hE n hn π hπ
  choose a d P _ hPn hPc hPs using bc.csd
  exact baseChange_speh_of_discreteSeries H2 GlobalTheory.T.exists_algebraicWeight_of_automorphic_definiteUnitary hn hπ bc a d P hPn hPs hPc fun i => (ascEmb_mul_eq (hPs i)).symm

def DiscCsd (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] : Prop :=
  ∀ N : ℕ, 1 ≤ N → ∀ V : Submodule ℂ (Sm E N), IsDiscrete V → CsdAsc V →
    ∃ (a d : ℕ) (h : ∑ _ : Fin d, a = N) (P : CuspSub E a), P.IsCsd ∧
      (∀ w σ', IsLocCompGL V w σ' → GlobalTheory.SmGL.IsUnitarisable σ'.ρ ∧
        ∃ σ, P.IsLocComp w σ ∧ GlobalTheory.SmGL.IsUnitarisable σ.ρ ∧ IsSpehQuot h σ σ') ∧
      ∀ τ m', HasInfCharGL V τ m' → ∃ m, P.HasInfChar τ m ∧ m' = GlobalTheory.DefiniteBC.spehInf d m

theorem discCsd_of : AscComp →
    ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E], DiscCsd E := by
  intro H E _ _ _ N hN V hV hc
  obtain ⟨a, d, π, -, hπ, hcs, hS⟩ := hc
  have hSp := hS
  obtain ⟨h, -⟩ := hSp
  have hda : d * a = N := ascEmb_mul_eq hS
  have ha : 1 ≤ a := Nat.pos_of_mul_pos_left (by omega : 0 < d * a)
  have hC := (H E N hN).2 V hV a d π hπ hS
  obtain ⟨V₁, hV₁, hS₁⟩ := (H E a ha).1 a π rfl hπ
  have hC₁ := (H E a ha).2 V₁ hV₁ a 1 π hπ hS₁

structure Parts (π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ)) (a d : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, d i * a i = n) (P : ∀ i, CuspSub E (a i))
    (ρ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), LocIrr w (a i)) (m : Fin c → (E →+* ℂ) → Multiset ℂ) : Prop where

  pos : ∀ i, 1 ≤ a i ∧ 1 ≤ d i

  csd : ∀ i, (P i).IsCsd

  loc : ∀ i w, (P i).IsLocComp w (ρ i w)

  inf : ∀ i τ, (P i).HasInfChar τ (m i τ)

  weight : ∀ τ lam, HasWeightAt π τ lam → ∑ i, spehInf (d i) (m i τ) = infOfWeight lam

  distinct : ∀ i j, d i = d j → (⟨a i, CG.mk (P i)⟩ : Σ b, CG E b) = ⟨a j, CG.mk (P j)⟩ → i = j

  inert : ∀ (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (s : w.adicCompletion E →+* w.adicCompletion E) (ϖ : w.adicCompletion E) (q : ℕ),
    (∀ x : E, s (algebraMap E _ x) = algebraMap E _ (IsCMField.complexConj E x)) → Formalism.UG.IsUnramInv ϖ s q →
    ∀ α, HasSatAt π w s ϖ q α → ∃ S : Fin c → Multiset ℂˣ, (∀ i, GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w (ρ i w).ρ (S i)) ∧
      ∑ i, ∑ k : Fin (d i), (S i).map (fun z => (z : ℂ) * ((Ideal.absNorm w.asIdeal : ℕ) : ℂ) ^ (((d i : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - (k : ℕ))) =
        (GlobalTheory.LGrp.bcParam n α).map (fun z => (z : ℂ))

        (m : Fin c → (E →+* ℂ) → Multiset ℂ) (σ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), LocIrr w (d i * a i)), Parts π a d h P ρ m ∧

theorem isSpherical_of_isUnramU : ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E],
    (∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), Algebra.IsUnramifiedAt (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield E)) w.asIdeal) →
    ∀ (n : ℕ) (π : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ)), IsAutRepU π →
    ∀ (c : ℕ) (a d : Fin c → ℕ) (h : ∑ i, d i * a i = n) (P : ∀ i, CuspSub E (a i))
      (ρ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), LocIrr w (a i)) (m : Fin c → (E →+* ℂ) → Multiset ℂ)
      (σ : ∀ i (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)), LocIrr w (d i * a i)),
      GlobalTheory.BcSpeh.Parts π a d h P ρ m →
      (∀ i w, IsUnitarisable (ρ i w).ρ ∧ IsUnitarisable (σ i w).ρ ∧
        GlobalTheory.LocGL.IsSpeh (GlobalTheory.LocGL.IrrGL.of (ρ i w).ρ (ρ i w).smooth (ρ i w).irred) (d i) (σ i w).ρ) →
      (∀ w, IsSplitAt w → IsCompAlong π (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) (locProd h w (σ · w))) →
      ∀ w, IsUnramU π w → ∀ i (τ : SmIrr (GL (Fin (a i)) (w.adicCompletion E))), (P i).IsLocComp w τ →
        GlobalTheory.LGrp.IsSpherical τ.ρ (GlobalTheory.LFun.KGL w (a i)) := by
  intro E _ _ _ hE n π hπ c a d h P ρ m σ hp hsh hsplit w hu i τ hτ
  suffices hB : ∃ B, GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w (ρ i w).ρ B by
    obtain ⟨B, hB⟩ := hB
    have h1 := (GlobalTheory.BcSpehCompare.hasSat_iff (hp.loc i w) B).2 hB
    exact ((GlobalTheory.BcSpehCompare.hasSat_iff hτ B).1 h1).1
  by_cases hw : IsSplitAt w
  · have hc := hsplit w hw
    have hs : GlobalTheory.LGrp.IsSpherical (locProd h w (σ · w)) (GlobalTheory.LFun.KGL w n) :=
      isSpherical_of_isCompAlong_split hπ hw _ hc hu
    have hsm : GlobalTheory.PRep.IsSmooth (locProd h w (σ · w)) := GlobalTheory.SmGL.isSmooth_parInd _ _
    have hirr : (locProd h w (σ · w)).IsIrreducible := hc.1
    obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := GlobalTheory.SatInd.exists_hasSatGL_of_isIrreducible hsm hirr hs
    obtain ⟨S, hS, -⟩ := GlobalTheory.SatInd.satake_locProd_speh E w c n a d h (ρ · w) (σ · w) (fun i => (hp.pos i).2)
      (fun i => (hsh i w).2.2) T hT
    exact ⟨S i, hS i⟩
  · obtain ⟨s, ϖ, q, α, h1, h2, h3⟩ := GlobalTheory.BcSpehCompare.exists_hasSatAt hE hπ hw hu
    obtain ⟨S, hS, -⟩ := hp.inert w s ϖ q h1 h2 α h3
    exact ⟨S i, hS i⟩

end GlobalTheory.SphBC


[BCS_unramified_consequence_02] src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/Foundations/BaseChangeIdentities/Narrow.lean:21-25

  unram : ∀ w, IsUnramU π w → GlobalTheory.LGrp.IsSpherical (locProd h w (σ · w)) (GlobalTheory.LFun.KGL w n)
  inert : ∀ (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (s : w.adicCompletion E →+* w.adicCompletion E) (ϖ : w.adicCompletion E)
    (q : ℕ), (∀ x : E, s (algebraMap E _ x) = algebraMap E _ (IsCMField.complexConj E x)) → IsUnramInv ϖ s q →
    ∀ α, HasSatAt π w s ϖ q α → GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w (locProd h w (σ · w)) (GlobalTheory.LGrp.bcParam n α)
  split : ∀ w, IsSplitAt w → IsCompAlong π (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) (locProd h w (σ · w))
